This is a must-fix-before-GA mislabeled-destructive-action defect plus a missing detail view, not a production-blocking failure of the admissions pipeline. Adjusted to P1.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Every cited location checks out exactly. (1) lib/features/admissions/applications/admissions_applications_screen.dart:125-152 `_handleApplicationAction`: for status==draft it immediately awaits submitApplicationProvider.execute(app.id) with no confirmation dialog; for all other statuses it shows only a status snackbar. It is wired as `onView` at line 74. (2) lib/features/admissions/applications/widgets/admissions_applications_table.dart:68-72 desktop IconButton (visibility icon, tooltip 'View application') and :130-133 mobile TextButton labeled 'View' — the row's only action, not permission-gated in the UI. (3) No application detail screen/route exists anywhere in lib/ or web/src/ (grep for ApplicationDetail variants: zero hits; admissions/ has only leads detail, approval, documents, enrollment screens). (4) Backend supabase/functions/_shared/admissions/admissions_repository.ts:696-717 submitApplication is a real UPDATE draft→submitted with NO completeness validation and no revert function; ApplicationStatus enum has no path back to draft and 'rejected' is terminal. It also stamps submitted_by, so the clerk who accidentally tapped View becomes the SoD maker and is barred from approving. HOWEVER, the impact's second half is overstated: the approval screen (AD-07, admissions_approval_review_panel.dart) gives approvers a real inspection surface — student, class, parent, counselor, fee plan, docs completion, counselor notes, workflow, approval history — and the table/mobile card already shows the key fields inline; the documents screen covers checklists. Approvers are not approving fully blind.

### V-21 · CONFIRMED **P0** — Admission funnel cannot be resumed or safely interleaved — continuity lives only in in-session memory, with a mock store in the live path
- **Module:** admissions · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** Admissions clerk · **Reported by:** school-erp-domain-expert
- **Original evidence:** lib/features/admissions/admissions_journey_context_provider.dart:7-17 — active application/enrollment/approval ids are plain StateProviders defaulting to null (lost on app restart). lib/features/admissions/enrollment/admissions_enrollment_provider.dart:97-113 — submit() binds the enrollment to whatever admissionsActiveApplicationIdProvider holds (null after restart, or the LAST application touched, not necessarily this family's) and then queries MockAdmissionsWriteStore.instance.findApprovalByApplication in the live submit path. The enrollment wizard has no DraftAutosaveMixin (admissions_enrollment_screen.dart:61 uses only an in-memory unsaved-changes guard), unlike fee collection and marks entry.
- **Original impact:** Indian admission offices process many families concurrently over days (document chase, fee negotiation). Here, a clerk who approves family A today and opens Enrollment tomorrow produces an enrollment orphaned from (or wrongly linked to) its application; a phone-lock mid-way through the 4-step wizard loses everything. The funnel works only as a single linear demo run, not as a work queue — and the mock-store dependency means the approval linkage silently no-ops in an API build.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Make the enrollment wizard take an applicationId route parameter from the approval/fee-handoff queue; persist wizard drafts like REL-3 does for collections/marks; remove MockAdmissionsWriteStore from the live provider.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The core claim survives adversarial contact. Two independent P0-grade defects are confirmed in a live write path of the core admissions funnel: (a) silent data-integrity corruption — enrollment binds to the last application touched in-session (or null after restart), the wizard gives the clerk no visibility or choice of which application is bound, and the backend persists whatever id arrives without validation, so a routine interleaved sequence (create/submit app B, then enroll family A) writes a wrongly-linked admission record with no error anywhere; (b) total draft loss on process death in a 4-step wizard, a workflow the project itself already treats as autosave-mandatory in sibling modules (fee collection, marks entry, teacher attendance) — proving the omission is a gap, not a design choice. Under this project's own PRA severity norms (silent wrong-linkage writes and mock-in-live-path were graded P0), this is production-blocking. One prong is inflated: the MockAdmissionsWriteStore call in the live provider is real code-hygiene rot but only feeds a QA-only provider with no readers, so 'approval linkage silently no-ops in an API build' overstates its user impact — the genuinely dangerous linkage failure is the applicationId one, which is fully confirmed. That correction does not lower the overall severity because the P0 rests on the confirmed orphan/wrong-link and data-loss prongs.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** All cited evidence verified at the exact locations, with one prong overstated. (1) /Users/surendrakanna/Documents/Akshara_ERP/lib/features/admissions/admissions_journey_context_provider.dart:6-17 — all four journey ids are plain Riverpod StateProviders defaulting to null; no persistence layer, so they are lost on process death. (2) admissions_enrollment_provider.dart:97-113 — submit() reads admissionsActiveApplicationIdProvider and passes it into EnrollmentSubmitRequest; the ONLY writers of that provider are admissions_applications_screen.dart:109 (application create) and :133 (draft submit) — it is never set from the approvals screen, from a route parameter, or on restart. The enrollment screen has no application picker and no route param (verified: enrollment reachable only via AdmissionsModuleScaffold tab). So enrolling family A after touching application B genuinely binds A's enrollment to B, and enrolling after restart binds to null. Backend confirms no safety net: supabase/functions/_shared/admissions/admissions_handlers.ts:1556 stores `application_id ?? null` with zero validation or inference — the orphan/wrong link is persisted silently. (3) Mock store: import (line 3) and MockAdmissionsWriteStore.instance.findApprovalByApplication call (lines 108-109) are real in the live provider, but this prong is overstated: its only effect is setting admissionsLastApprovalIdProvider, which is documented as 'Patrol / QA assertions' and has ZERO readers anywhere in lib/ — the real approval/application linkage travels via application_id in the request, so nothing user-facing 'no-ops' from the mock. (4) admissions_enrollment_screen.dart:61 — only AksharaUnsavedChangesGuard (in-memory dialog guard); DraftAutosaveMixin exists (lib/core/reliability/drafts/draft_autosave.dart:42) and is used by exam marks entry, finance workflow actions (fee collection), teacher attendance, and parent leave — but not the 4-step enrollment wizard.

### V-22 · CONFIRMED **P0** — Web ERP is read-only: every core-workflow action button is dead or hardcoded disabled
- **Module:** finance / admissions / teacher / hr (web) · **Platform:** web · **Role:** cashier / teacher / HR / admissions clerk · **Reported by:** qa-usability-reviewer
- **Original evidence:** web/src/pages/finance/CollectionsPage.tsx:46 — <Button icon="add">Collect fee</Button> has no onClick; web/src/pages/teacher/TeacherPages.tsx:78 ('Assign homework'), :91 ('Assign'), :182 ('Apply for leave') — no handlers; web/src/pages/hr/LeavePage.tsx:40-45 — Approve/Reject hardcoded `disabled`; web/src/pages/admissions/EnrollmentPage.tsx:48 — 'Hand off to Finance' hardcoded `disabled`; web/src/pages/detail/DetailPages.tsx:81,154,184 — 'Edit'/'Download PDF'/'Send' dead; grep of web/src/lib/api/*.ts shows no POST/PUT/PATCH anywhere (only OTP request in AuthContext.tsx). Marks entry does not exist at all on web (TeacherPages.tsx:106-116 subtitle promises 'marks entry' but rows are not even clickable).
- **Original impact:** A cashier cannot collect a fee, a teacher cannot assign homework or enter marks, HR cannot approve leave, admissions cannot enroll — on the platform positioned as the desktop 1:1 parity ERP. Buttons render enabled and do nothing on click, which reads as 'broken' not 'not built'. This dead-ends every primary journey and would fail instantly in a sales demo.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Either implement the write layer for the core workflows (fee collect, marks, homework, leave approve, enrollment hand-off) or remove/visibly gate the dead action buttons with an explicit 'available in the mobile app' affordance. Never render an enabled button with no handler.
- **Verifier reasoning:** Attempted refutation failed on every axis. Evidence is precise, not vague — every line number matched. No hidden mitigation exists: I searched for alternate routes/screens (found the orphaned /teacher/homework/create, itself non-functional), for a mutation layer (none — only auth OTP), and for guards that would hide dead buttons from users (none — buttons render enabled and silently do nothing, except the two explicitly disabled ones). This is not a backend-only issue: backends for these actions exist (fee collection, leave decisions, homework are live on the Flutter/pilot side), so this is purely a web-client gap with direct UX impact. On severity: the strongest refutation angle is that the live pilot runs the Flutter app and the web lane is owner-frozen — so one could argue P1 "known track state." But that fails for three reasons: (a) the web lane was live-certified (229/229 routes) and frozen with the ONLY recorded open item being token refresh, meaning the program record materially overstates web completeness — this finding corrects that record; (b) the web app is live-hosted for role-based review with 8 demo logins, where enabled-but-dead primary actions read as broken; (c) for the platform's stated purpose (1:1 parity desktop ERP), 100% of write journeys dead-end — cashier, teacher, HR, admissions clerk can complete zero core tasks, and two pages carry copy falsely asserting live posting. That is production-blocking for any web release and squarely P0 within the web platform scope the finding declares. Confirmed at P0.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Every cited location checked and holds exactly as claimed. (1) web/src/pages/finance/CollectionsPage.tsx:46 — `<Button icon="add">Collect fee</Button>` with no onClick and no route to any collect flow (router.tsx has /finance/collections and /finance/collections/:id only, no create route). (2) web/src/pages/teacher/TeacherPages.tsx:78 "Assign homework", :91 "Assign", :182 "Apply for leave" — all handler-less; worse, a create route /teacher/homework/create EXISTS (router.tsx:336) but nothing navigates to it, and its own "Assign" button (line 91) has no submit handler, no form state, and its copy falsely claims "Submitting posts to the live /teacher/homework endpoint" (line 100). (3) web/src/pages/hr/LeavePage.tsx:40-45 — "Reject selected"/"Approve selected" hardcoded `disabled` with no selection mechanism; copy at :37 claims "Approve/reject posts to the live decision endpoint" (false). (4) web/src/pages/admissions/EnrollmentPage.tsx:48-50 — "Hand off to Finance" hardcoded `disabled`. (5) web/src/pages/detail/DetailPages.tsx:81 "Edit", :154 "Download PDF", :184 "Send" — all dead; the conversation composer input at :183 is an uncontrolled bare input wired to nothing. (6) Repo-wide grep of web/src: the ONLY non-GET requests in the entire web app are /auth/login and /auth/verify-otp in lib/auth/AuthContext.tsx:115,127. Zero useMutation, zero POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE in any page or api module. Marks entry: TeacherExamsPage (:106-116) subtitle promises "marks entry" but rows have no onRowClick and no marks-entry page exists.

### V-23 · CONFIRMED **P1** — Web pages assert false and fabricated state to the user
- **Module:** teacher / legal / settings (web) · **Platform:** web · **Role:** teacher / school admin · **Reported by:** qa-usability-reviewer
- **Original evidence:** web/src/pages/teacher/TeacherPages.tsx:100 — caption states 'Submitting posts to the live /teacher/homework endpoint. Class and subject options load from your teaching assignments.' while the Selects have options={[]} (lines 94-95) and the Assign button has no submit logic; web/src/pages/misc/MiscPages.tsx:131-137 — LegalAcceptancePage renders StatusChip 'Accepted' unconditionally for Terms of Service, Privacy Policy, DPA and AUP; web/src/pages/settings/SettingsPage.tsx:42-43 — 'Compact density'/'Reduce motion' toggle local useState that affects nothing; :59-60 — notification switches are no-ops (onChange={() => {}}) with hardcoded checked state.
- **Original impact:** The UI makes claims that are untrue: a form says it posts live when it cannot; a compliance page shows legal documents as 'Accepted' that were never accepted; settings appear to save but do nothing. This is worse than a missing feature — it erodes trust in every state indicator in the product and misstates legal-acceptance facts.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Remove the false endpoint caption; drive legal-acceptance chips from real acceptance records or remove the status column; wire or remove decoy settings toggles.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The evidence is airtight — the UI does assert false state (a form claiming live posting with no submit logic; legal docs hardcoded "Accepted"; no-op settings). No refutation held. However, P0 (production-blocking) inflates current impact: the web app is a frozen parity-recreation lane currently exposed only as a UX-review demo on an isolated test tenant, while the actual pilot runs the Flutter app; the prior web live-cert only covered crash/blank/401, not functional truth, so nothing certified is contradicted. Nothing here corrupts data or blocks a workflow, and the legal page is display-only (no acceptance flow exists whose record it falsifies). The false legal "Accepted" chips and the fabricated endpoint caption are mandatory fixes before web GA or real-admin use — hence P1, not P0.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** All three citations verified exactly. TeacherPages.tsx:94-95 has Select options={[]}, line 91 Assign button has no handler, yet line 100 literally reads "Submitting posts to the live /teacher/homework endpoint. Class and subject options load from your teaching assignments." MiscPages.tsx:131-137 renders StatusChip status="success" label="Accepted" unconditionally for all four legal documents with no data source. SettingsPage.tsx:13-14/42-43 toggles set dead local useState; lines 59-60 switches are hardcoded checked with onChange={() => {}}. All three pages are live routes in web/src/routes/router.tsx (lines 286, 336, 502). No guard, alternate route, or data-driven mitigation exists.

### V-24 · CONFIRMED **P1** — Simulated 'EdgeAiProvider' fabricates AI responses labeled as live inference on live surfaces
- **Module:** copilot / core-ai · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** teacher, parent, student, staff · **Reported by:** ai-experience-reviewer
- **Original evidence:** lib/core/ai/providers/edge_ai_provider.dart:4-6 ('Simulated live inference provider'), :60-67 (returns '**Live inference (akshara-edge-v1)**... Based on current school data... _Generated by Akshara live inference pipeline._'), :89-103 (fabricated stats: 'Rebalance 2 sections with >110% teacher workload', 'reduce 18% idle capacity', 'Priority score: 82'). Wired as PRIMARY provider by default: lib/core/ai/ai_inference_providers.dart:28-42 (LIVE_AI_INFERENCE_ENABLED defaultValue true, no config/live_release.json override). Consumers not behind any API flag: copilot_persona_shell_screen.dart:42, copilot/content/ai_content_providers.dart:10, parent_meetings_mutations_provider.dart:106, resource_optimization_providers.dart:12.
- **Original impact:** Teachers, parents and students (who lack full-copilot permission per role_permissions.dart:602-662) receive hardcoded template text presented as data-grounded live AI. Fabricated operational numbers look authoritative. This is deceptive labeling that destroys trust the moment any user notices the same reply for every question, and it violates the project's own deterministic-first firewall in spirit: fake AI masquerading as governed AI.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Remove EdgeAiProvider from production wiring. Route persona-shell/content/meeting-summary inference through the governed backend gateway (which already has a correct degrade-to-deterministic-stub path), or honestly disable those features until wired. Never render 'Live inference' copy from a synthesizer.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The core defect is real and the technical evidence is accurate: a locally hardcoded template generator explicitly labels its output as live, data-grounded AI ('Based on current school data', 'Generated by Akshara live inference pipeline', metadata live:true) with invented operational numbers, and it is the default primary provider in every release build. Fabricated parent-meeting summaries are even persisted to the backend via saveSummary. That is genuine deceptive labeling and must be fixed before GA. But the P0 framing rests on two false premises. First, the stated victims — teachers, parents, students — cannot receive this content at all: the pipeline fail-closes on RBAC (viewAiCopilot+runAiCopilot) which only seven admin/leadership roles hold; the reviewer cited the very permission table that refutes their own impact claim. Second, the flagship copilot chat on live builds runs against the real backend API (HybridCopilotRepository, AI_COPILOT_ENABLED=true in live_release.json), so 'fake AI on live surfaces' applies only to secondary surfaces (persona shell, AI content, parent-meeting summary, resource optimization hub) reachable by privileged roles. The code comment also marks this as an acknowledged interim edge-mirroring layer (FV-PLAT-10), not silent deception in the primary path. Real issue, admin-scoped, secondary surfaces, with the worst concrete harm being fabricated optimization stats and persisted templated summaries shown to school leadership — a serious trust defect to fix before GA (P1), not an all-roles production-blocking P0.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Cited code verified exactly: lib/core/ai/providers/edge_ai_provider.dart:4 says 'Simulated live inference provider'; :60-67 returns '**Live inference (akshara-edge-v1)** ... Based on current school data ... _Generated by Akshara live inference pipeline._'; :89-103 contains the fabricated stats ('Rebalance 2 sections with >110% teacher workload', '18% idle capacity', 'Priority score: 82'), and :28 even sets metadata {'live': true} with usedFallback:false. Wiring confirmed: ai_inference_providers.dart:25-42 makes EdgeAiProvider primary when API mode is on and LIVE_AI_INFERENCE_ENABLED (defaultValue:true) is not overridden — config/live_release.json sets ENABLE_API_MODE:true and does NOT set LIVE_AI_INFERENCE_ENABLED, and environment.dart guardForRelease THROWS unless API mode is on in release, so every release build gets the simulated provider as primary. No real remote AI provider exists in lib/core/ai/providers/ (only edge/fallback/stub). All four cited consumers exist at the cited lines. HOWEVER, two major mitigations the reviewer missed: (1) AiInferencePipeline._assertPermission (ai_inference_pipeline.dart:110-117) requires viewAiCopilot AND runAiCopilot before ANY provider call; role_permissions.dart grants these only to superAdmin, schoolAdmin, principal, vicePrincipal, management, financeAdmin, admissionsCounselor — teacher, parent, student (the roles named in the finding) hold neither and get a thrown RBAC error, rendered as 'Unable to generate a response right now' (copilot_persona_shell_screen.dart:83-87), never the fabricated text. (2) The PRIMARY copilot surface (/copilot, CopilotScreen) does not use this pipeline in live builds: repository_providers.dart:300-309 routes to HybridCopilotRepository → ApiCopilotRepository → real remote datasource when AI_COPILOT_ENABLED=true (which live_release.json sets); the simulated pipeline only reaches users via secondary surfaces (persona-shell route, AI content screen, parent-meeting summary generation, resource optimization — the last is Mock-only with no API variant).

### V-25 · **REFUTED** (do not action as reported) — Fabricated AI meeting summaries are saved to real parent-meeting records
- **Module:** parent_meetings · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** teacher/staff · **Reported by:** ai-experience-reviewer
- **Original evidence:** lib/features/parent_meetings/parent_meeting_detail_screen.dart:90-95 ('Generate AI Summary' button) -> parent_meetings_mutations_provider.dart:99-126 (pipeline.complete then repository.saveSummary persists response.content) -> edge_ai_provider.dart:70-81 (canned template inserts claims never discussed: 'Attendance: Reviewed against class benchmark', action items 'Complete pending homework daily', 'Schedule a follow-up in two weeks').
- **Original impact:** A canned, partly fabricated narrative becomes the stored record of a real parent conference — the exact 'AI output mistaken for authoritative school records' safety failure. Staff will trust and forward it; parents may be told their child was discussed in ways that never happened.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Block save until real summarization exists; generate strictly from the typed notes via the governed backend, show a draft-review step (editable, explicitly marked AI-drafted) before persisting.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The core claim — 'fabricated AI summaries are saved to REAL parent-meeting records' — does not survive contact with the code. In the production configuration the write path is fail-closed by design: ApiParentMeetingsRepository.saveSummary throws before any persistence, so no fabricated content can reach a real record, be forwarded by staff, or be shown to parents. In non-API (mock/demo) builds the summary is written only to an in-memory mock repository seeded with demo data, which is not a real record of a real conference. So the claimed safety failure ('AI output mistaken for authoritative school records') is structurally blocked, and P0 production-blocking severity is unjustified. What IS true and worth filing as a separate, lower-severity finding: (1) in production the 'Generate AI Summary' button is a dead feature that fails without any user-facing feedback (_generateSummary at parent_meeting_detail_screen.dart:184-188 has no try/catch, so the ApiFailureException is an unhandled async error — silent no-op UX); (2) EdgeAiProvider is a canned-template simulator that self-labels its output metadata as 'live': true, which is misleading plumbing that could become dangerous if a future teacher-scope saveSummary endpoint is wired up without replacing the simulated provider. Those are P1/P2 hygiene items, but they are different findings from the one claimed; as stated, the reviewed P0 is refuted.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** The cited code fragments exist exactly as quoted: the 'Generate AI Summary' button (parent_meeting_detail_screen.dart:90-95), the pipeline.complete -> saveSummary flow (parent_meetings_mutations_provider.dart:99-134), and the canned template with fabricated attendance/action-item claims (edge_ai_provider.dart:70-81; EdgeAiProvider is indeed the primary provider when API mode is on, per ai_inference_providers.dart:25-42 with LIVE_AI_INFERENCE_ENABLED defaulting true). BUT the reviewer stopped one hop short of the persistence layer. parentMeetingsRepositoryProvider (repository_providers.dart:414-422) resolves in live API mode (PARENT_API_ENABLED=true in scripts/run_live.sh and scripts/ios_device_install.sh) to ApiParentMeetingsRepository, whose saveSummary (api_parent_meetings_repository.dart:97-105) unconditionally throws UnsupportedError ('Editing the PTM summary is a teacher-scope action, not available to parents') — the class doc explicitly says write ops are 'intentionally unsupported here, so we never silently fabricate or drop a write.' The mutation's catch rethrows as ApiFailureException; nothing is persisted, nothing is invalidated, and the fabricated text is never even displayed (the screen renders meeting.aiSummary from the repository read, not the pipeline response). The only path where saveSummary succeeds is MockParentMeetingsRepository (in-memory _meetings list, mock/demo data only — not real parent-meeting records), used only when the parent module is not in live API mode.

### V-26 · CONFIRMED **P1** — Production-reachable developer harness can publish hand-typed AI 'guidance' to the real parent hub
- **Module:** intelligence · **Platform:** all · **Role:** staff (viewStudentRisk) · **Reported by:** ai-experience-reviewer
- **Original evidence:** lib/features/intelligence/intelligence_screen.dart:25-44 (controllers prefilled 'Arjun Reddy', 'Grade 8', 'student_1', attendance '62', marks '54'), :245-293 (staff types raw Student ID + attendance/marks/homework, 'Publish to parent hub' toggle defaults TRUE, snackbar 'Guidance published — visible in parent hub'). Route live: app_router.dart:617 ('/intelligence'), guard route_guards.dart:44 (only Permission.viewStudentRisk), linked from Platform Operations Hub as 'Intelligence Hub' (platform_operations_hub_screen.dart:157-161).
- **Original impact:** Any staffer with student-risk permission can fabricate inputs for an arbitrary student ID and push AI-generated 'guidance' to a real parent, one tap from a production hub link. Also instantly reads as demo-ware in a sales demo (prefilled fake student name).
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Unship this screen from production routing (or gate behind an internal/dev flag). Real risk workflows already exist in student_success/. If a manual-guidance tool is wanted, it must pick real students from the roster, source metrics from the system, and default publish OFF behind a review step.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The core claim survives: a production-reachable screen lets trusted staff hand-type attendance/marks for an arbitrary student UUID, have those fabricated values override real risk data server-side, and publish templated 'guidance' straight into the real parent hub with publish defaulting ON — plus prefilled fake demo data ('Arjun Reddy'/'student_1') on a live production route, which genuinely reads as demo-ware. That is a real data-integrity and product-quality defect, not a misreading. But P0 is inflated on three counts: the actor must hold generateIntelligence (teacher and above — people already trusted to communicate with parents through other channels), the prefilled harness values cannot actually publish (UUID FK rejects 'student_1', so the literal 'one tap from the hub' scenario errors out), and blast radius is confined to the staffer's own tenant/school with an audit record emitted (emitMutationAudit). It is a serious pre-GA fix — replace free-text inputs with a student picker bound to real data, drop the demo prefill, default publish to draft — but it is not a production-blocking security breach class issue. Confirmed at P1.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Every cited location checks out. lib/features/intelligence/intelligence_screen.dart:25-46 has the prefilled demo controllers exactly as claimed ('Arjun Reddy', 'Grade 8', 'student_1', '62', '54') and _publishGuidance=true; :245-293 has the raw Student ID/attendance/marks/homework fields, the 'Publish to parent hub' switch, and the exact snackbar text. Route live at lib/router/app_router.dart:617 ('/intelligence'), guard lib/router/route_guards.dart maps RouteNames.intelligence -> Permission.viewStudentRisk only, and platform_operations_hub_screen.dart:157-161 links it as 'Intelligence Hub'. The publish path is REAL, not mock: repository_providers.dart:459 -> HybridIntelligenceRepository -> remote datasource POST /intelligence/parent-guidance/generate; backend handler (supabase/functions/_shared/intelligence/intelligence_handlers.ts:236-330) defaults publish=true, lets client-typed inputs OVERRIDE the real risk snapshot (inputs.attendancePercent ?? riskInputs...), and INSERTs status='published'; parent_experience_service.ts:111 reads published rows into the real parent hub. Mitigations the reviewer understated: (1) the generate form and backend both require Permission.generateIntelligence (intelligence_provider.dart:21-22; requireIntelligenceWrite), not mere viewStudentRisk — though role_permissions.dart grants it to superAdmin, schoolAdmin, principal, vicePrincipal AND teacher, so the population is still broad; (2) student_id is UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES students(id) (migration 20260621000000:87-101), so the prefilled 'student_1' can never insert — the default one-tap flow fails at the DB, and an attacker must paste a real student UUID; (3) tenant/org/school scoping from claims confines it to the staffer's own school; (4) the 'AI guidance' is a deterministic template generator, not live AI.

### V-27 · CONFIRMED **P1** — Teacher Assistant 'Add intervention' writes a hardcoded student_1 record to the live backend
- **Module:** evolution / teacher-assistant · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** teacher · **Reported by:** ai-experience-reviewer
- **Original evidence:** lib/features/evolution/teacher_assistant_screen.dart:38-51 — FloatingActionButton.extended calls createIntervention(studentId: 'student_1', interventionType: 'academic', title: 'Schedule doubt-clearing session', priority: 'high') with no dialog or student picker. EVOLUTION_API_ENABLED=true in config/live_release.json:45; backend router exists (supabase/functions/_shared/teacher_assistant/teacher_assistant_router.ts). Class filter also hardcodes only 'Grade 8'/'Grade 7' (lines 30-34).
- **Original impact:** The primary action on the teacher's AI surface creates a bogus intervention for a nonexistent student every time it is pressed — garbage in the live intervention tracker and a broken core flow (a teacher cannot actually log an intervention for a real student from here).
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Replace with a proper intervention sheet: student picker from the risk list (tapping a flagged student should prefill it), editable type/title/priority, confirm. Populate the class filter from the teacher's actual classes.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The code evidence is real and the primary action on a live-flag-enabled teacher screen is a shipped stub with no way to log an intervention for a real student — a genuinely broken core flow, so confirmed. But the P0 rested on 'writes a bogus student_1 record to the live backend / garbage in the live intervention tracker', which is refuted: the UUID NOT NULL FK on students(id) makes the insert impossible, so there is zero data pollution or integrity damage. What remains is a silently failing button on a differentiation/AI-assist surface with intact read paths — a broken feature, not production-data corruption or a money/safety path. That is an honest P1 (fix before GA: replace stub with a student picker sourced from riskStudents/suggestions and surface errors), not a production-blocking P0.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Cited code is accurate: teacher_assistant_screen.dart:38-51 has the FAB calling createIntervention with hardcoded studentId 'student_1', type 'academic', title 'Schedule doubt-clearing session', priority 'high', no dialog/picker; lines 30-34 hardcode Grade 8/7 filter. EVOLUTION_API_ENABLED=true at config/live_release.json:45; evolutionRepositoryProvider (repository_providers.dart:553) serves HybridEvolutionRepository whose createIntervention (hybrid_evolution_repository.dart:86-101) forwards directly to the live API — no mock interception. Screen is routed (app_router.dart:764) and permission-granted (viewTeacherAssistant held by 5 roles). BUT the impact claim fails: supabase/migrations/20260623000000_teacher_assistant_foundation.sql:8 defines student_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES students(id) — 'student_1' is not a valid UUID, so the parameterized INSERT in teacher_assistant_handlers.ts errors at Postgres and no row is ever created. The button instead fails silently (unhandled awaited exception, no error UI in onPressed).

### V-28 · CONFIRMED **P0** — Circular/broadcast to parents is unreachable from any navigation on mobile — core principal journey is dead
- **Module:** communication · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** principal · **Reported by:** journey-principal
- **Original evidence:** The only compose UI is BroadcastAdminScreen (lib/features/communication/broadcast_admin_screen.dart). Its only in-app entry points are: school_completion_hub_screen.dart:94 (the hub itself has ZERO inbound links — grep for RouteNames.schoolCompletionHub across lib/ hits only router registration files app_router.dart:785-788, route_guards.dart:70), communication_delivery_screen.dart:31 (same orphaned hub), and parent_meeting_detail_screen.dart:98 (parentMeetings route is disabled in school_build_scope.dart:76-77). No 'Communication' destination exists among the 25 admin nav entries (admin_navigation_provider.dart labels list) and global_search_registry.dart has no broadcast entry. On a phone there is no URL bar, so the screen is literally unreachable.
- **Original impact:** A principal cannot send a notice/circular to all parents — arguably the most frequent school-to-home task. The feature is fully built (audiences, templates, ack tracking, scheduling, history, delivery analytics) and completely wasted.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Add a top-level Communication nav destination (parity with web navConfig.ts:70) and a 'Send circular' quick action on the principal's Management overview panel.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The core claim survives full contact with the code: BroadcastAdminScreen is routed and permission-guarded (route_guards.dart:85, manageCommunication) but every in-app path to it is either an orphaned hub with no inbound edges or a build-scope-disabled route, and neither navigation nor global search offers any Communication compose entry. On a phone there is no address bar, so the screen is genuinely unreachable for real users. The severity is honest, not inflated: sending a circular/notice to all parents is a core, high-frequency principal journey in a mobile-first school ERP; the feature is fully built (audiences, scheduling, delivery, analytics) yet completely dead, and there is no mobile workaround (teacher messages is 1:1 only, notifications is receive-only). A fully built primary journey that no user can reach is a production-blocking UX defect, so P0 stands. The fix is trivial (add a nav/global-search entry), but ease of fix does not lower severity.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Every cited fact verified against the code. (1) Exactly three push sites to RouteNames.communicationBroadcastAdmin: school_completion_hub_screen.dart:94, communication_delivery_screen.dart:31, parent_meeting_detail_screen.dart:98 — no others in lib/. (2) RouteNames.schoolCompletionHub has zero inbound context.push/go anywhere; it appears only in route registration (app_router.dart:785-788, route_guards.dart:70, route_names.dart, school_completion_navigation.dart), so the hub — and the delivery/whatsapp screens that hang off it — is a dead subtree. (3) school_build_scope.dart:76-77 gates parentMeetings/parentPtm OFF ('no backend'), killing the third entry point. (4) admin_navigation_provider.dart's 25 labels contain no Communication destination; lib/features/management/ has zero communication references. (5) global_search_registry.dart:173-177 has one Communication entry, but it routes to CommunicationAnalyticsScreen, which I confirmed has no outbound navigation (view-only dead end). Mitigation hunt failed: TeacherMessagesScreen is 1:1 parent messaging by phone (not broadcast), NotificationsScreen is an inbox, no other compose surface exists in lib/. Web (EngagePages.tsx) has a broadcast list page but is a separate frozen lane, out of the mobile-scoped claim, and its own copy says backend wiring is pending.

### V-29 · CONFIRMED **P0** — Admin Hub landing hero shows hardcoded fake stats as live data in production
- **Module:** admin · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** principal · **Reported by:** journey-principal
- **Original evidence:** workspace_landing_config.dart:9-12 admits stats are 'curated demo figures consistent with the seeded school so demos read as live'; lines 31-43 hardcode '1,248 Students', '96% Attendance', '₹4.2L Collected today', '₹1.8L Pending', '92% This month'. AdminHubScreen renders them unconditionally (admin_hub_screen.dart:58-63) with no API-mode gate and no 'demo' label.
- **Original impact:** The first screen a principal sees every morning presents fabricated attendance and fee-collection figures as truth. The numbers never change, which destroys trust in every real number elsewhere in the app; in a sales demo a returning prospect will notice the frozen ₹4.2L.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Replace with real summary providers or drop the stat row until endpoints exist (the hero widget already accepts any stat list).
- **Verifier reasoning:** The core claim is fully accurate and no mitigation exists: fabricated financial and attendance figures render as live data on the production landing screen for every staff workspace, with the code comment explicitly stating the intent is that fake figures 'read as live'. There is no demo gate, no label, no alternate real-data path, and the route is registered in the live router. Under this project's own PRA standard (fake data presented as real in production paths = P0-class), and given a live pilot where a principal sees frozen fake money figures ('₹4.2L Collected today') daily, P0 is the honest severity — the numbers never change and directly undermine trust in every real figure in the app. Severity is not inflated; if anything the finding understates scope (5 additional workspaces affected).
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Every cited line verified verbatim. workspace_landing_config.dart:9-12 contains the admitted-demo comment; lines 31-43 hardcode the exact figures quoted ('1,248 Students', '96% Attendance', '₹4.2L Collected today', '₹1.8L Pending', '92% This month'). admin_hub_screen.dart:39-40 looks up kWorkspaceLandingConfig and lines 58-63 render the stats unconditionally with no API-mode gate and no demo label (grep of akshara_workspace_landing.dart finds no 'demo' text). kWorkspaceLandingConfig's sole consumer is AdminHubScreen; no real-data stat provider exists; the app's demo flags (isDemoAuthEnabledProvider, tenant_mock_scope) do not gate this path. Route is live: app_router.dart:564 wires adminHubRouteBuilder into the production router at '/admin'. Golden dark_admin_hub_834x1194.png shows the hero stat row rendering. The reviewer actually UNDERSTATED scope: front office, inventory, transport, hostel, and library workspaces also carry fabricated stats (config lines 44-83).

### V-30 · CONFIRMED **P1** — Web ERP is a read-only shell: 80 of 103 page action buttons have no handler; zero business mutations exist
- **Module:** web-platform · **Platform:** web · **Role:** principal · **Reported by:** journey-principal
- **Original evidence:** grep across web/src/pages: 80/103 <Button> instances lack onClick — including 'New broadcast' (engage/EngagePages.tsx:42), 'Add student' (sis/StudentRegistryPage.tsx:71), 'Create exam' (academics/AcademicsPages.tsx:39), 'Approvals' (management/ManagementPages.tsx:49), 'Mark all read' (EngagePages.tsx:52). The web Approval Center renders a table with status chips but NO approve/reject actions (ManagementPages.tsx:106-124). The only POST in the entire web app is login (web/src/lib/auth/AuthContext.tsx). SchoolConfigPage renders 8 'interactive' cards with no onClick (EngagePages.tsx:110-134).
- **Original impact:** At the office desk the principal can look but not act: cannot approve a leave or concession, cannot send a circular, cannot add anything. Every primary CTA is a dead control that looks enabled — worse than absent, because it teaches users the product is broken.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Either wire the top-10 principal actions (approve/reject, broadcast compose) or visibly disable dead CTAs with a 'coming soon' state until the write layer ships.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The factual claim survives completely — the web app is a read-only shell with dead primary CTAs and zero business mutations. But P0 (production-blocking) is inflated: (1) the product is mobile-first and the Flutter app — the surface the live pilot actually runs — provides every mutation the impact statement claims is impossible (approve leave/concession, send circular, add student), so a working alternative route exists for all listed tasks; (2) the web lane's read-only scope is deliberate and documented in-page ('once the backend is connected'), was certified only for route rendering, and is owner-frozen — a known phased state, not a latent defect; (3) web currently reaches users only as an isolated-tenant review demo, not as principals' production tool. The genuine defect is that 80 dead controls render enabled instead of disabled/hidden (plus the silently-discarding 'Save marks'), which misleads demo users and violates the project's hide-first principle — a real must-fix-before-web-GA gap. Confirmed at P1.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Every cited datum verified true: 103 <Button> instances in web/src/pages, 23 with onClick (80 dead). EngagePages.tsx:42 'New broadcast' and :52 'Mark all read' have no handlers; SchoolConfigPage (lines 124-130) renders 8 interactive cards with no onClick; StudentRegistryPage.tsx:68-72 'Import'/'Add student' dead; AcademicsPages.tsx:39 'Create exam' dead; ManagementPages ApprovalCenterPage table has zero approve/reject actions, and ManagementDashboardPage even renders check/close icon buttons with no onClick. Only POSTs in the whole web app are /auth/login and /auth/verify-otp in AuthContext.tsx. Additionally, the one 'wired' mutation CTA (AcademicsPages.tsx:90 'Save marks') just calls setEdits({}) — discards edits, persists nothing. Pages carry explicit 'once the backend is connected' copy, showing the read-only state is a known scoped phase.

### V-31 · CONFIRMED **P1** — Global 'submitted' flag locks every class after first attendance submit — a 6-period teacher can mark only one class per session
- **Module:** teacher/attendance · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** Class Teacher · **Reported by:** journey-teacher
- **Original evidence:** lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_provider.dart:22 (teacherAttendanceSubmittedProvider is a single non-autoDispose StateProvider<bool>), :94 (isSubmitted applied to whatever class is currently selected, also OR'd with the global MockAttendanceSyncStore.hasTeacherSubmission), :226 (set true on submit; grep shows no reset anywhere). Class switching only writes teacherAttendanceClassProvider (teacher_attendance_screen.dart:247-249). When isSubmitted is true: updateStudentMark/applyBulkMark/fillRemainingAsPresent early-return (provider :104, :120, :141), the grid disables (screen :355), submit disables (:368), and a false 'Attendance submitted successfully' banner shows (:258-263).
- **Original impact:** After submitting period-1 attendance for 8-A, selecting 8-B shows the whole roster read-only under a 'submitted' banner for a class that was never marked. The teacher's core daily loop breaks before second period; the false banner also creates phantom attendance records in her mind.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Make submitted-state per class per day (e.g. Map<classId, bool> keyed like the draft keys already are), reset on class switch, and derive the banner from the selected class's real submission status.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The core defect is confirmed exactly as coded: a session-global submitted flag with no per-class/per-session keying and no reset path, producing a disabled grid and a false success banner on any other session selected afterward. But the P0 framing rests on a scenario the product forbids — attendance is restricted to the class teacher's single class (both mock and API repos enforce this), so 'every class of a 6-period teacher locks' cannot occur. The real blast radius is the second period-session of the SAME class (e.g. 8-A P5 after 8-A P1), which is a genuine break with a dangerous false 'submitted' banner that can cause silently missing afternoon attendance, but: it affects one session per class-teacher per day at most, schools running once-daily attendance are unaffected, and an app restart clears the in-memory flag. Real, user-facing, must fix before scale — but not the total-workflow collapse claimed. Severity honestly lands at P1 (fix before GA; false-success state in a core flow), not production-blocking P0 as evidenced.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Every cited line checks out: /Users/surendrakanna/Documents/Akshara_ERP/lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_provider.dart:22 is a single non-autoDispose StateProvider<bool> (app-lifetime); :94-95 ORs it with the global MockAttendanceSyncStore.instance.hasTeacherSubmission (mock_attendance_sync_store.dart:20 — a singleton with one lastSubmittedAt, no class keying); :226 sets it true on submit; grep across lib/ confirms NO write back to false anywhere (only the default and a test). Class switching writes only teacherAttendanceClassProvider (teacher_attendance_screen.dart:247-249) with no reset listener. When isSubmitted: updateStudentMark/applyBulkMark/fillRemainingAsPresent early-return (:104/:121/:140), grid disables (screen :355 enabled: !data.isSubmitted), submit disables (:368), and the 'Attendance submitted successfully' banner renders for whatever class is selected (:258-263). HOWEVER, the reviewer's scenario is wrong on scope: attendance classes are CLASS-TEACHER-scoped, not subject-teacher-scoped. mock_teacher_repository.dart:35-43 filters the roster to only classes whose label == teachingContext.classTeacherClassLabel (non-class-teachers get NONE), and api_teacher_repository.dart:39 states 'Server scopes attendance classes to the class teacher.' So a '6-period teacher marking 6 classes' does not exist in this product — a teacher marks only their own class. The lockout still bites, though: _mockClasses() (mock_teacher_repository.dart:706-731) gives class-teacher 8-A TWO attendance sessions (8-A Period 1 and 8-A Period 5, both isPending), and after submitting P1 the P5 session is read-only under a false 'submitted' banner. In-memory flag clears on app restart (crude workaround, undiscoverable to a teacher who trusts the banner).

### V-32 · CONFIRMED **P0** — Attendance correction dialog ships hardcoded demo data: date '12 Jun 2026' and canned reason 'Biometric sync error — student was present'
- **Module:** teacher/attendance · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** Class Teacher · **Reported by:** journey-teacher
- **Original evidence:** lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_workflow.dart:31-34 — reasonController prefilled with 'Biometric sync error — student was present', dateController prefilled with fixed text '12 Jun 2026'; the date is a free-text AksharaFormField (:63-67), not a date picker; toMark defaults to Present (:30) even when correcting to Late.
- **Original impact:** My 9:40 late-arrival correction: unless I notice and retype, the correction is filed for the wrong date (12 Jun 2026) with a fabricated excuse, then routed to principal approval — a wrong, plausible-looking audit record created by the default UI state. Also instantly embarrassing in any sales demo (visible canned text).
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Default the date to today via showDatePicker (the homework screen already does this correctly, teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:100-114), leave reason empty and required, preselect the tapped student, and default toMark from context.
- **Verifier reasoning:** I set out to refute this and could not — every element of the evidence is literally present, and the failure path is worse than 'cosmetic demo text' because the prefilled values flow unedited into three durable records: the correction row, the principal's approval-queue payload, and the audit log. The required-field validation is defeated by the prefill itself (both fields arrive non-empty), the free-text date accepts anything, and toMark defaults to Present regardless of the intended correction, so the path of least resistance (open dialog → pick student → submit) files a plausible-looking but fabricated record dated 12 Jun 2026 with a canned biometric-excuse reason, then routes it through the school's formal approval workflow. Possible refutations checked and eliminated: (a) demo-only screen — no, it is the production teacher attendance screen; (b) downstream guard — the mutation provider does RBAC only, no content validation or date parsing; (c) alternate correct route — the only caller is this dialog. On severity: this is default-state fabrication of attendance audit records in a live maker-checker approval flow — a data-integrity defect, not a polish issue — plus a hardcoded past date that becomes silently wrong on every real day of use. That meets the P0 bar (wrong records created by the default UI state, invisible until an audit/dispute), consistent with this project's PRA standard that hardcoded/demo residue in live paths is production-blocking. Confirmed at P0 as filed.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Every cited line holds exactly as claimed. /Users/surendrakanna/Documents/Akshara_ERP/lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_workflow.dart: line 31-33 `reasonController = TextEditingController(text: 'Biometric sync error — student was present')`; line 34 `dateController = TextEditingController(text: '12 Jun 2026')`; line 30 `toMark = StudentAttendanceMark.present.label`; lines 63-67 the date is a plain `AksharaFormField` free-text field (required:true only — the prefill satisfies the validator), not a date picker. I then traced further than the reviewer: the prefilled strings are submitted verbatim — dialog line 131/134 passes `dateController.text.trim()` and `reasonController.text.trim()` into `TeacherAttendanceCorrectionRequest`, and teacher_mutations_provider.dart lines 467-516 writes them into a real correction record (`attendanceCorrectionRepositoryProvider.createCorrection`, dateLabel at :476), a principal Approval Center payload (`'date': request.dateLabel` at :500, `'reason'` at :507), and an audit entry (`recordTeacherAudit` :511). No date parsing/validation anywhere — dateLabel is an opaque string end-to-end. The dialog is wired into the live screen (teacher_attendance_screen.dart:269), not a demo/dev-only path; the only guard is RBAC permission, which does nothing about the content.

### V-33 · CONFIRMED **P0** — Teacher marks entry cannot record AB — digits-only field, no absent affordance; absent students are permanently 'pending'
- **Module:** teacher/exams · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** Class Teacher · **Reported by:** journey-teacher
- **Original evidence:** lib/shared/marks_grid/marks_grid.dart:125 (FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly) and :123 (number keyboard); lib/features/teacher/exams/teacher_exams_provider.dart:184-194 (save path is int.parse only); lib/features/teacher/exams/exam_models.dart has no status field (grep for absent/AB returns nothing). The admin-side grid DOES support AB/ML/DB (lib/features/academics/exam_admin/exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:642, :874) — the capability exists but was never brought to the teacher surface.
- **Original impact:** Entering unit-test marks for 42 students with 2 absentees can never be completed: 'Pending marks' KPI and '/42 entered' stats never clear, and the only way to make the grid look finished is to type 0 — exactly the data-corrupting behavior the frozen exam-status design (NULL + AB) exists to prevent. This is the top-priority module.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Add a per-row status affordance (long-press or a small AB/ML/DB chip next to the field) that clears the mark and saves a status, mirroring the admin grid; count status'd rows as 'entered' in MarksColumnStats.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The core claim survives contact with the code exactly as written: the teacher marks-entry surface physically cannot express an absent student (digits-only formatter, int-parse-only save path, no status field in the model or DTO), while the same product already ships the AB/ML/DB capability on the admin-only grid. The only mitigation I found — an admin (staff role) recording AB via the AdminShell screen — does not rescue the teacher workflow: it requires a different role, is undiscoverable from the teacher UI, and even after an admin sets AB the teacher model drops the status, so the entry either stays 'pending' forever (null marks) or displays a bare 0 — the precise corruption the project's frozen Exam Result Status design (absent = NULL + AB, never 0, excluded from averages/ranks) forbids. Severity is honest, not inflated: exams are the declared top-priority module, teacher marks entry is the primary production flow, the in-role task is impossible to complete, and the path of least resistance (typing 0) silently corrupts averages/ranks class-wide. That combination — task-impossible for the primary role plus a built-in data-corruption incentive violating a frozen design decision — is P0, not P1.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Every cited location checks out. (1) /Users/surendrakanna/Documents/Akshara_ERP/lib/shared/marks_grid/marks_grid.dart:123-125 — TextField with keyboardType: TextInputType.number and inputFormatters: [FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly]; no status affordance anywhere in the widget. (2) lib/features/teacher/exams/teacher_exams_provider.dart:184-194 — saveTeacherExamMarkFromInput validates via validateTeacherExamMarkInput (rejects empty/non-numeric, requires 0..max) then int.parse; updateExamMark sends only marksObtained. (3) lib/features/teacher/exams/exam_models.dart:35-67 — ExamMarkEntry has id/name/rollNo/marksObtained/maxMarks only, no status field; copyWith can only carry marksObtained; grep for absent/AB across lib/features/teacher/exams/ returns only putIfAbsent hits. The teacher-side ExamMarkEntryDto (lib/core/repositories/api/teacher/dto/teacher_responses_dto.dart:156) is a raw map with no status mapping. (4) lib/features/academics/exam_admin/exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:642 and :874 — admin grid has an EXM-D6 PopupMenuButton<ExamMarkStatus> that clears marks and persists AB/ML/DB, exactly as claimed. (5) Mitigation check failed: the AB-capable screen is routed only via examAdministrationMarks inside the AdminShell (app_router.dart:798-818), and route_guards.dart:243 gates that shell on auth.role == UserRole.staff — a teacher-role user cannot reach it; the teacher's only marks surface is TeacherExamsScreen (TeacherShell) using the digits-only shared grid, whose pending count is computed as marksObtained == null (teacher_exams_screen.dart:129, :620).

### V-34 · CONFIRMED **P2** — Homework photo attachment is impossible — 'attachment' is a typed filename plus a pasted link, no camera/gallery/upload
- **Module:** teacher/homework · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** Class Teacher · **Reported by:** journey-teacher
- **Original evidence:** lib/features/teacher/homework/teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:34-36 (comment: 'optional teacher attachment (reference/label, not a real upload)') and :246-264 — two plain TextFormFields: 'Attachment name (optional)' (hint 'worksheet.pdf') and 'Attachment reference / link (optional)' (hint 'shared drive link').
- **Original impact:** The single most common real homework action — photograph the textbook page and send it — cannot be done. A phone-first teacher between classes will not type a filename and paste a drive link; she will use WhatsApp, and the module loses its daily anchor. The fields also fake an affordance the product doesn't have.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Add camera/gallery pick + real upload (an image_picker + storage upload); until the backend exists, remove the pseudo-attachment fields rather than shipping a decoy.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The factual core is true and not misread: a teacher cannot attach a photographed textbook page from camera/gallery; the "attachment" is a typed label plus a pasted link. So confirmed=true. But P0 (production-blocking) is inflated. (1) The homework module's daily core flow — create, target multiple sections, due date, deliver to parents/students — works; the attachment fields are explicitly optional and honestly labeled as name/link text fields, not a fake upload button that fails. A teacher with a shared-drive or WhatsApp link can still convey the reference, and the link is delivered end-to-end. (2) The product's own frozen enhancement backlog already identifies this exact gap (HWK-4) and the owner classified it P2 — the current text-reference implementation is a partial delivery of that P2 item, matching its code comment. Re-litigating a frozen owner prioritization as P0 without new evidence is severity inflation. (3) The reviewer's adoption-risk argument ("teacher will use WhatsApp instead") is a plausible product thesis, not evidence of a production-blocking defect; nothing crashes, lies, or corrupts data. Real UX gap worth building (upload plumbing already exists in memories/staff-attendance), but per the owner's own frozen classification it is P2.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Cited evidence holds exactly. /Users/surendrakanna/Documents/Akshara_ERP/lib/features/teacher/homework/teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:34-36 contains the comment "HWK-4 — optional teacher attachment (reference/label, not a real upload)" and two TextEditingControllers; lines 246-264 are two plain TextFormFields labeled "Attachment name (optional)" (hint "e.g. worksheet.pdf") and "Attachment reference / link (optional)" (hint "e.g. shared drive link"). No ImagePicker/FilePicker/camera anywhere in lib/features/teacher/homework/ (only files: create/history/list screens, provider, models — none touch upload). Real photo upload DOES exist elsewhere in the app (lib/features/memories/school_memory_event_screen.dart uses uploadMediaBytes; staff_attendance uses camera capture), so the platform capability exists but homework does not use it — no hidden mitigation route for this task. The attachmentName/attachmentRef strings are genuinely delivered end-to-end to student/parent views (student_mapper.dart:66-71, parent_mapper.dart:435-437), so the fields are not dead. Critically, docs/PRODUCT_ENHANCEMENT_BACKLOG.md:613 (owner-FROZEN rev 5) already tracks exactly this gap as HWK-4 "Teacher attachment on create (worksheet/photo)" at priority **P2** ("half-wired"), alongside HWK-7 (student photo submit, also P2).

### V-35 · CONFIRMED **P0** — Fee payment is simulated end-to-end — 'Payment successful' + receipt with no money movement
- **Module:** parent/payment · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** parent · **Reported by:** journey-parent
- **Original evidence:** lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_screen.dart:115 wires the Pay button to submitMockPayment; lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_provider.dart:104-163 fabricates transactionRef: 'txn_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}' client-side and flips phase to success; payment_models.dart:4 offers UPI/Card/NetBanking but selection is cosmetic — no UPI intent, no QR, no gateway is ever invoked. Web is worse: web/src/pages/parent/ParentPages.tsx:98 'Pay now' is `window.location.hash = ''` (a no-op) and :141 shows a placeholder 'gateway load ... once connected'.
- **Original impact:** The #1 parent journey (pay this month's fee) produces a fake success ceremony and a receipt number without collecting money. In production this creates phantom receipts and reconciliation chaos; in a sales demo, one probing question ('which gateway?') collapses it. Known owner-gated dependency (P0-02), but the flow must not present success until real.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Until the gateway SDK lands, replace the mock submit with an explicit blocked state ('Online payment coming soon — pay at the school office, here's the exact amount and reference') rather than a fake success. Never render a receipt that no money produced.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The core claim survives contact with the code at every cited line: the #1 parent journey renders a full 'Payment successful' ceremony with a receipt number while no gateway (UPI intent/QR/SDK) is ever invoked, and method selection is cosmetic. Worse than claimed on one axis: in stub mode — the current default and the state of the live pilot — the backend writes a real collections + receipts row into the finance ledger for a payment that moved no money, which is exactly the reconciliation-chaos scenario. The one real mitigation (fail-closed signature guard in live mode) does not rescue UX: it only converts fake-success into guaranteed-failure once real creds exist, confirming no working payment path exists at all. The 'Web is worse' characterization is inflated (web is honestly disabled, not deceptive), and the issue overlaps the already-tracked owner-gated P0-02, but neither reduces severity: a production parent flow that presents success and issues ledger-backed receipts without collecting money is production-blocking. P0 stands.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** All cited locations verified. (1) /Users/surendrakanna/Documents/Akshara_ERP/lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_screen.dart:115 — Pay button is exactly `onPay: () => submitMockPayment(ref)`. (2) parent_payment_provider.dart:104-163 — `submitMockPayment` exists as claimed and line 139 fabricates `transactionRef: 'txn_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}'` client-side, then flips phase to success; there is also a `parentPaymentSimulateFailureProvider` test hook and a hardcoded fallback summary ('Ravi Kumar', 8-A). (3) web/src/pages/parent/ParentPages.tsx:98 — 'Pay now' is exactly `window.location.hash = ''`; :141 shows the 'gateway load ... once connected' placeholder. One evidence correction: the flow is NOT purely client-side — initiate/confirm call real backend endpoints (parent_mutations_provider.dart:314/346 → supabase/functions/_shared/payment/payment_service.ts), and in stub mode (the default, and per the RT-23 comment in payment_handlers.ts:202 'live on the pilot') the backend's `createCollection` mints a REAL finance collection + receipt row for the fake payment, which strengthens the phantom-receipt claim rather than weakening it. Mitigations found: payment_service.ts:210-234 is a fail-closed guard — with a live Razorpay config, confirm without a verified gateway signature throws, so phantom receipts cannot occur once real credentials exist (but the Flutter client sends no signature, so live mode = the flow can never succeed; there is no real payment path either way). Overstatement found: 'Web is worse' is wrong in spirit — ParentPaymentPage's 'Proceed to pay' is disabled with an honest placeholder; web never fakes success. The dead 'Pay now' hash no-op is still a real defect.

### V-36 · CONFIRMED **P0** — Fees, dashboard, attendance, payment and profile silently render fabricated demo data on load/error
- **Module:** parent (cross-cutting) · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** parent · **Reported by:** journey-parent
- **Original evidence:** fees_provider.dart:298-300 `data ?? future.value ?? ParentFeesData.mock()` (mock = '₹4,200 due', isOverdue: true); parent_dashboard_provider.dart:312-314 falls back to ParentDashboardData.mock() ('Ravi Kumar', chip 'Present', '₹4,200 due'); parent_attendance_provider.dart:44-48 AttendanceMonthData.mock(); parent_payment_provider.dart:63-101 _fallbackSummary hardcodes 'Ravi Kumar · 8-A' with a live Pay button; parent_profile_provider.dart:33+ hardcodes 'Suresh Kumar / +91 98765 43210'. The guarding loading/error providers (e.g. fees_provider.dart:281-283) are manual StateProviders that NOTHING sets on real API failure, so the skeleton/error branches in the screens never trigger — mock data shows during every cold load and forever on API error.
- **Original impact:** A real parent can see a fictional child's name, a fabricated 'Present' status for an absent child, and fabricated rupee amounts presented as fact. On money and attendance — the two highest-trust surfaces — this is a product-killing trust failure, and it also flashes on every cold start before real data arrives.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Delete every `?? Mock()` fallback in parent providers and adopt the exams pattern (parent_exams_screen.dart:34-42): real async.isLoading -> skeleton, async.hasError -> retry, empty -> honest empty state.
- **Verifier reasoning:** Could not refute. The evidence is exact, the guard the reviewer says is dead really is dead (manual StateProviders only ever set true by tests), and the mock fallbacks are reachable in the live-configured build (PARENT_API_ENABLED=true in config/live_release.json). No alternative route, cache, or hybrid repository mitigates the parent lane. Only overstatement found: after one successful load, Riverpod copyWithPrevious retains stale real data on refresh errors, so 'forever on API error' strictly applies to first-load errors (cold start with backend unreachable) — but cold-start on flaky mobile networks is the common parent scenario, and the mock flash on every cold load is unconditional regardless. Severity is honest at P0: fabricated rupee amounts and a fabricated 'Present' attendance status presented as fact to real parents, and the payment screen's fabricated _fallbackSummary feeds the real payment initiation (submitMockPayment reads parentPaymentSummaryProvider), meaning a parent could be shown and act on invented money figures. This is a trust-destroying, production-blocking defect on money and attendance surfaces of a live pilot.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Every cited line verified verbatim. fees_provider.dart:298-300, parent_dashboard_provider.dart:312-314, parent_attendance_provider.dart:45-47, parent_payment_provider.dart:63 (+_fallbackSummary 66-101 with 'Ravi Kumar · 8-A'), and parent_profile_provider.dart:32-48 ('Suresh Kumar / +91 98765 43210') all fall back to hardcoded mock data via `data ?? future.value ?? mock`. core/providers/repository_future.dart confirms watchRepositoryFuture returns null on BOTH AsyncLoading and AsyncError, so the mock renders during cold load and on first-load API error. The guarding flags (parentFeesLoadingProvider etc., all default false) are set to true ONLY in test files — no production code path ever sets them, so the screens' skeleton/error branches (e.g. parent_fees_screen.dart:61-68) are dead code in real use. Reachability in production confirmed: repository_providers.dart:407-412 selects ApiParentRepository when PARENT_API_ENABLED=true, which config/live_release.json sets; ApiParentRepository.getFees (line 69-72) throws with no fallback. The project's own MODULE_JOURNEY_AUDIT.md independently documents the sibling pattern (parent communications fallback showing fabricated messages) as a live defect, corroborating the mechanism.

### V-37 · CONFIRMED **P1** — More menu offers 'PTM' which dead-ends in a bare 'Access Denied' screen
- **Module:** parent/navigation · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** parent · **Reported by:** journey-parent
- **Original evidence:** parent_shell.dart:94-98 puts PTM in the parent More sheet; persona_nav.dart:205-216 renders spec.more with NO SchoolBuildScope filtering; school_build_scope.dart:76-77 hides RouteNames.parentPtm; app_router.dart:2611-2615 returns AccessDeniedScreen for hidden routes; route_guards.dart:247-259 renders a bare lock icon + 'Access Denied'. Dashboard mock quick-action 'ptm' and hardcoded notice n1/event e2 routing (parent_navigation.dart:62-64,86-87) also lead there.
- **Original impact:** The app's own menu invites the parent into a locked door. 'Access Denied' reads as 'my account is broken' to a non-technical parent — a guaranteed support call, and an instant fail in any demo where someone taps every tile.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Filter MoreNavSheet/PersonaNavSpec destinations through SchoolBuildScope.isRouteHidden (and entitlements) so gated modules never render as menu items.
- **Verifier reasoning:** The core defect is real and reproducible in production for every parent: the shell's static More sheet advertises PTM while SchoolBuildScope deliberately gates the route off (PTM has no backend), and the unfiltered nav + bare AccessDeniedScreen produce a locked-door dead-end. But P0 (production-blocking) is inflated: no user task is blocked (the PTM feature does not exist behind the door), no data loss, no security or financial impact, and all core parent flows are unaffected. It is a trust/polish defect — embarrassing in demos and a likely support-call generator — with a one-line class of fix (filter spec.more via SchoolBuildScope.isRouteHidden or remove the tile with the two scope entries). That profile is P1 (must fix before demo/GA), not P0. Secondary evidence (dashboard quick action, hardcoded notice/event routing) applies only to mock/demo data paths, further weakening the P0 case.
- **What the verifier found at the cited locations:** Verified: parent_shell.dart:94-98 hardcodes a PTM tile in the parent More sheet; persona_nav.dart:205-216 renders spec.more with no SchoolBuildScope/entitlement filtering; school_build_scope.dart:19,77 has enabled=true and hides RouteNames.parentPtm; app_router.dart:2610-2615 returns AccessDeniedScreen for the hidden route (and no global redirect intercepts — the only isRouteHidden use in the router is this builder); route_guards.dart:247-259 renders a bare lock icon + 'Access Denied' with no explanation or CTA. So every parent sees a PTM menu item that dead-ends in a bare Access Denied screen. However, the dashboard 'ptm' quick-action evidence is overstated: it exists only in ParentDashboardData.mock() (parent_dashboard_provider.dart:90); the live path maps raw['quickActions'] ?? [] (parent_mapper.dart:36) and the backend /parent/dashboard handler emits no quickActions, so live dashboards do not show the PTM quick action. notice_n1/event_e2 routing (parent_navigation.dart:86-87) also fires only on mock-data ids.

### V-38 · CONFIRMED **P1** — Transport shows a hardcoded fake bus ETA ('approximately 8 minutes away') with a dead 'Refresh ETA' button
- **Module:** parent/transport · **Platform:** all-mobile · **Role:** parent · **Reported by:** journey-parent
- **Original evidence:** parent_transport_screen.dart:74-81 — const AksharaInsightCard(message: 'Bus is approximately 8 minutes away (telemetry preview). Live map integration is not enabled in this build.', actionLabel: 'Refresh ETA') with NO onAction (akshara_insight_card.dart:19,28 shows onAction is nullable and none is passed), rendered under the 'AKSHARA SUGGESTS' AI eyebrow.
- **Original impact:** A parent deciding when to walk their child to the stop sees a fabricated ETA that never changes, plus developer jargon ('telemetry preview', 'not enabled in this build') and a button that does nothing. This is safety-adjacent fabricated information and it poisons trust in every other 'AKSHARA SUGGESTS' card.
- **Reviewer recommendation:** Remove the fake ETA card entirely; show route/stop facts only, with an honest 'Live bus tracking coming soon' note if needed.

But the finding is overstated on two counts: (1) the 'dead Refresh ETA button' does not exist — the widget's null-onAction guard means no button renders, so there is no broken interactive control; (2) the card self-discloses as a non-live preview in its own text, so it is not a silent fabrication (though the disclaimer is developer jargon and may truncate at 2 lines).
