## Reviewer: Mobile UX Specialist (Android + iOS)  (`mobile-ux-specialist`)
**Vote:** CERTIFIED — moderate improvements required · **Scores:** overall 6, mobile 6, accessibility 7, maturity 6

**Independent executive assessment:** Akshara's Flutter app has unusually strong mobile bones for an ERP: a real offline-first reliability platform (global sync banner, draft flush on background, outbox-backed writes for attendance/exams with optimistic results), a disciplined persona bottom-nav contract (max 4 primary tabs + More, enforced by test), broad error-retry adoption (264 files), above-norm accessibility (214 Semantics files, text-scale clamping only where density demands it), and correct hide-first gating — the out-of-scope salon/restaurant/healthcare/franchise/white-label verticals are genuinely unreachable at both nav and route level. The exam marks-entry flow (top-priority module) is the best mobile form in the app: per-row focus advance, dirty tracking, and save-all with per-student errors.

However, the app currently fails several phone-product fundamentals. The AI FAB is positioned directly over the middle bottom-nav tab in all three persona shells, hijacking icon taps on teacher "Teach" (exams/homework), parent "Fees", and student "Schedule" — the highest-value tabs. The live-reachable parent "Pay Now" flow completes a simulated payment (client-generated txn ref, no gateway checkout) and shows success + receipt. Every one of the ~280 routes uses NoTransitionPage, so there are zero push animations and the iOS back-swipe gesture is dead app-wide. Parent leave dates are free-text fields with a decorative calendar icon; "attachments" in homework and leave are typed name strings, not files; pull-to-refresh exists on only 4 of 297 screens; and tablet renders persona dashboards as a 480px column pinned to the left edge with a third of the screen blank. These are targeted, fixable defects — not a redesign — hence CERTIFIED_MODERATE.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Offline-first reliability platform is real, not aspirational: global SyncBanner only when offline/pending/conflicted (lib/app/app.dart:77-88), draft flush on every lifecycle pause (app.dart:46-59), and attendance/exam writes routed through ReliableWriter with optimistic outcomes (lib/core/repositories/api/teacher/remote/teacher_remote_datasource.dart:179-197, 495-520)
- Persona bottom-nav contract: <=4 primary tabs + shared More sheet with workspace switcher, single source of truth across teacher/parent/student shells, enforced by persona_nav_test (lib/shared/navigation/persona_nav.dart:58-70, 147-224)
- Out-of-scope verticals (salon/restaurant/healthcare/franchise/white-label) correctly unreachable: hidden from nav (lib/core/config/school_build_scope.dart:22-84) AND route-blocked with AccessDeniedScreen (lib/router/route_guards.dart:411-413), plus backend-less surfaces hidden in live builds (lib/router/surface_backend_gate.dart:22-55) — hide-first executed honestly
- Exam marks entry (top-priority module) has excellent mobile ergonomics: numeric keyboard + next-action, per-row focus advance, dirty-cell state, save-all with per-student error reporting (lib/features/teacher/exams/teacher_exams_screen.dart:520-556, 637-649; lib/shared/marks_grid/marks_grid.dart:123-124)
- Accessibility discipline above ERP norm: 214 files use Semantics, icon buttons get accessible names (lib/shared/widgets/akshara_navigation.dart:515-521), text scaling clamped only in dense grids (lib/shared/marks_grid/marks_grid.dart:114), dedicated a11y tests (test/a11y/semantics_daily_flows_test.dart)
- Async-state discipline: shared MobileAsyncBody with loading/error/empty/retry semantics (lib/shared/widgets/mobile_async_body.dart), onRetry/AksharaErrorState in 264 feature files
- Dark mode is coherent and deliberate across dashboards (test/golden/goldens/dark_teacher_dashboard_390x844.png shows correct dark surfaces and re-toned alert banner)
- Admin shell adapts by breakpoint: drawer + bottom nav on phones, rail on tablet/desktop (lib/features/admin/admin_shell.dart:50-88); finance dashboard genuinely uses tablet width (test/golden/goldens/finance_dashboard_834x1194.png: 3-column KPI grid + chart with side panel)
- OTP login has resend timer with countdown and numeric keyboard on both parent and staff paths (lib/features/auth/otp_verification_screen.dart:41-65, 216-217)

**Findings:**

### F-026 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-04) — AI FAB is positioned directly over the middle bottom-nav tab, hijacking taps on Teach/Fees/Schedule
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** navigation / copilot · **Role:** teacher, parent, student
- **Evidence:** lib/features/copilot/widgets/copilot_bottom_nav_ai_slot.dart:22-32 (Positioned left:0,right:0,top:-22, centered 56px button) painted last in the Stack over the NavigationBar (lib/shared/navigation/persona_nav.dart:86-119). With 5 slots (4 primary + More), the FAB sits exactly over slot 3: teacher 'Teach' = homework+exams (lib/features/teacher/shell/teacher_shell.dart:37-43), parent 'Fees' (lib/features/parent/shell/parent_shell.dart:47-48), student 'Schedule' (lib/features/student_app/shell/student_shell.dart:37-38). Default AI mode for these roles is bottomNavCenter (lib/features/copilot/settings/ai_access_preferences_provider.dart:50-54). Goldens test/golden/goldens/persona_shell_teacher_390x844.png and persona_shell_parent_390x844.png show the circular FAB covering the middle tab's icon with the orphaned label beneath it. At 390px width each slot is 78px; the 56px FAB (x 167-223) fully covers the middle tab's icon zone (icon ~x 183-207).
- **Impact:** Tapping the icon of the Fees tab (parent), Teach tab (teacher, gateway to Exams — the top-priority module) or Schedule tab (student) opens the AI sheet instead of navigating. The tab is reachable only via the thin label strip below the FAB. Daily mis-taps on the highest-value destinations; visually the FAB appears mislabeled with the covered tab's label ('Teach'/'Fees'), which would embarrass in a sales demo.
- **Recommendation:** Reserve the center slot for the AI entry (4 destinations become 2+2 around a dedicated center slot), or move the AI entry to the app bar for these personas. Never place a floating button over a live NavigationDestination.

### F-027 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-05) — Live-reachable parent 'Pay Now' completes a simulated payment with success screen and receipt
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent / fees / payment · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_provider.dart:104-163 — production flow is submitMockPayment(): initiate then confirm with a client-generated transactionRef 'txn_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}', no gateway checkout UI (no UPI handoff, no card entry), then PaymentFlowPhase.success with receipt. Route /parent/payment is registered (lib/router/app_router.dart:371-377), reached from Pay Now (lib/router/parent_navigation.dart:115-118), and is NOT in the backend-less surface gate (lib/router/surface_backend_gate.dart:22-40), so it is reachable in a live API build. Backend has a real Razorpay layer awaiting credentials (supabase/functions/_shared/payment/payment_handlers.ts:199-210), which the client flow bypasses.
- **Impact:** A parent selects a payment method, watches 'Processing payment', and receives a success screen and receipt without any money moving. This is a trust-destroying experience for the single most sensitive parent journey, and a school demo of fee collection would show fake receipts.
- **Recommendation:** Gate /parent/payment behind the payment-gateway API flag exactly like the eight backend-less surfaces (show fees, receipts, and dues, but replace Pay Now with 'Pay at school office' until the gateway ships), and delete the client-generated transaction reference path.

### F-028 · [P1] — Parent leave From/To dates are free-text with a decorative, non-functional calendar icon
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent / leave · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent/leave/widgets/leave_apply_form.dart:56-82 — 'From date' and 'To date' are plain TextFormField with hintText 'e.g. 12 Jun 2026' and suffixIcon: Icon(Icons.calendar_today_outlined) that has no tap handler and no showDatePicker; validation is only non-empty (lib/features/parent/leave/leave_models.dart:101-105 — isValid checks fromDateLabel.isNotEmpty). Contrast: teacher homework create uses a real showDatePicker with a FormField wrapper (lib/features/teacher/homework/teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:100-114, 208-234).
- **Impact:** Parents must type dates in an arbitrary format on a phone keyboard; the calendar icon is a false affordance users will tap repeatedly. Any string ('asap', '12/6') passes validation and flows to the school-side approval queue as an unparseable label. High friction and garbage data on a primary parent journey.
- **Recommendation:** Reuse the homework-create date-picker pattern (readOnly field + showDatePicker + ISO value), add from<=to validation.

### F-029 · [P1] — All ~280 routes use NoTransitionPage: no push animations anywhere and iOS back-swipe gesture is dead app-wide
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** navigation / router · **Role:** all
- **Evidence:** lib/router/app_router.dart — 280 occurrences of NoTransitionPage, 0 MaterialPage/CupertinoPage (grep counts). Even drill-in detail routes use it: parentPayment (app_router.dart:374), parentConversation :threadId (app_router.dart:399-405), parentReceiptDetail :receiptId (app_router.dart:415-421). NoTransitionPage provides no transition and no iOS swipe-back gesture recognizer.
- **Impact:** Every navigation — including pushes into message threads, receipts, and payment — instantly teleports with zero animation, losing spatial hierarchy and reading as unfinished. On iOS the universal edge-swipe-to-go-back gesture does nothing on any screen, a platform-convention violation across all 297 screens (worst for iPhone users who rely on it instead of a back button).
- **Recommendation:** Keep NoTransitionPage only for sibling tab switches inside each persona shell; use default MaterialPage/CupertinoPage (or platform-adaptive transitions) for every drill-in route so push animation and iOS swipe-back return.

### F-030 · [P1] — Tablet renders persona dashboards as a 480px column pinned to the left edge with the right third blank
- **Platform:** tablet · **Module:** parent / teacher / student dashboards · **Role:** parent, teacher, student
- **Evidence:** Goldens: test/golden/goldens/parent_dashboard_834x1194.png (content occupies left ~530px, right ~300px entirely white below the header) and teacher_dashboard_834x1194.png (split body crammed into the left half, section headers wrapping to 3 lines in ~230px columns). Code: lib/features/parent/dashboard/parent_dashboard_screen.dart:83-88 wraps content in ConstrainedBox(MobileDashboardLayout.contentConstraints(width)) with no Center/Align, and contentConstraints caps maxWidth at 480 on tablet (lib/shared/layout/mobile_dashboard_layout.dart:22-24). Teacher dashboard has a purpose-built _TabletSplitBody but it is inside the same 480px, start-aligned ConstrainedBox (lib/features/teacher/dashboard/teacher_dashboard_screen.dart:71-103). Contrast: finance_dashboard_834x1194.png uses the full width, and teacher_exams_screen.dart:86-93 correctly wraps in Align(topCenter).
- **Impact:** On the named 834x1194 tablet target, the three persona home screens look broken — a phone layout shoved into the top-left corner. The teacher tablet 'split' layout is squeezed into half its intended width, causing severe header wrapping. Tablet is a first-class certification surface and this is the first screen every user sees.
- **Recommendation:** Center the constrained column (Align topCenter, as exam screen already does) as the minimum fix; for dashboards, let _TabletSplitBody span the real width instead of the 480px compact cap.

### F-031 · [P1] — Teacher homework targeting is free-text: typed class labels and typed student name instead of pickers
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher / homework · **Role:** teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/homework/teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:141-170 — class-sections are added by typing 'e.g. 8-A' into a text field with an Add button; :236-242 — individual assignment via 'Student name (blank = whole class)' free-text field. The app already knows the teacher's real classes: initState prefills from resolvedTeacherTeachingContextProvider.teachingClassLabels (:51-57), proving structured data exists but is not offered as a picker.
- **Impact:** Daily teacher task requires phone typing where two taps should do; a typo ('8A' vs '8-A') silently mis-targets homework to no class, and a misspelled student name mis-targets an individual assignment. Highest-frequency teacher creation flow has the highest error surface.
- **Recommendation:** Replace the class text field with FilterChips of teachingClassLabels (+ 'other' escape hatch) and the student field with a roster picker for the selected class.

### F-032 · [P1] — Pull-to-refresh exists on only 4 of 297 screens
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** app-wide lists and details · **Role:** all
- **Evidence:** grep RefreshIndicator across lib/features: exactly 4 files — student/parent/teacher dashboard screens and notifications_screen.dart. Zero on parent fees, homework lists, messages/threads, attendance history, teacher homework/exams lists, finance collections, admissions lists, approval center.
- **Impact:** The most ingrained mobile refresh gesture silently does nothing on nearly every list in the app. A parent waiting for a fee status update or a teacher waiting for homework submissions has no way to refresh except leaving and re-entering the screen; data appears stale and users blame the app.
- **Recommendation:** Add RefreshIndicator (invalidating the backing provider) to every scrollable list/detail surface; it is a mechanical change given the provider architecture.

### F-033 · [P1] — 'Attachments' in homework and leave are typed text references, not real files or photos
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher / homework + parent / leave · **Role:** teacher, parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/homework/teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:244-264 — 'Attachment name (optional)' + 'Attachment reference / link (optional)' text fields ('not a real upload', comment line 34). lib/features/parent/leave/widgets/leave_apply_form.dart:100-115, 142-168 — 'Add medical certificate (optional)' opens a dialog asking the parent to TYPE a document name ('e.g. Dr Rao medical certificate.pdf'); comment lines 138-141 admit no real upload exists.
- **Impact:** The UI promises attachment semantics it does not deliver: a parent believes the medical certificate is attached when only its typed filename is stored; the school can never see the document. On mobile, photographing a worksheet/certificate is the natural expectation. Expectation mismatch on two daily flows that will surface as trust complaints in pilot.
- **Recommendation:** Until a storage bucket ships, relabel honestly ('Note down document name — bring the certificate to school'); then wire image_picker + upload as the real fix.

### F-034 · [P1] — OTP fields have no autofill hints or SMS retrieval — parents must transcribe codes manually
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** auth · **Role:** parent, staff
- **Evidence:** lib/features/auth/otp_verification_screen.dart:216-217 and lib/features/auth/staff/staff_otp_screen.dart:129-130 — numeric keyboard only; zero autofillHints (AutofillHints.oneTimeCode / telephoneNumber) anywhere in lib/features/auth (grep), no SMS Retriever integration; phone field at login_screen.dart:196-197 likewise lacks telephoneNumber hint.
- **Impact:** OTP-to-parent-phone is the ONLY login path for every parent (per product decision), and the target audience is low-tech. Without oneTimeCode hints, iOS never offers the 'From Messages' code suggestion and Android never auto-reads the SMS — users must app-switch, memorize 6 digits, and type them. First-run friction on 100% of parent activations.
- **Recommendation:** Add autofillHints: [AutofillHints.oneTimeCode] to both OTP fields and AutofillHints.telephoneNumber to the phone field; consider sms_autofill/SMS Retriever on Android.

### F-035 · [P2] — Shared app bar touch targets below platform minimums: 32px profile avatar, 40px icon buttons
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** shared UI / app bar · **Role:** all
- **Evidence:** lib/shared/widgets/akshara_app_bar.dart:239-252 — profile is an InkWell wrapping a CircleAvatar(radius:16) = 32x32 tap target with no minimum-size constraint; lib/shared/widgets/akshara_navigation.dart:481, 505-507 — AksharaAppBarIconButton defaults to a 40x40 InkWell. Material minimum is 48dp, iOS HIG 44pt; the codebase's own AksharaSpacing.minTouchTarget token exists (lib/theme/spacing.dart:43) and is used by the child-selector chip (akshara_app_bar.dart:34-36) but not by these buttons.
- **Impact:** The notification bell and profile actions — present on nearly every screen — are hard to hit for users with larger fingers or motor impairments; repeated mis-taps on the top-right cluster.
- **Recommendation:** Wrap both in ConstrainedBox(minWidth/minHeight: AksharaSpacing.minTouchTarget); visual size can stay 32/40.

### F-036 · [P2] — Teacher screens mis-wire profile action to dashboard and announce it as 'Parent profile' to screen readers
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher / shared app bar · **Role:** teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/homework/teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:127 — onProfileTap: () => context.go(RouteNames.teacherDashboard) (profile avatar navigates to the dashboard, silently discarding the half-typed homework form since no unsaved guard covers this screen; guard adoption is only 2 forms: lib/shared/forms/akshara_unsaved_guard.dart consumers = sis_profile_edit_sheet.dart, admissions_enrollment_screen.dart). The semantic label defaults to 'Parent profile' on every non-parent screen that does not override it (lib/shared/widgets/akshara_app_bar.dart:109).
- **Impact:** Tapping the profile avatar mid-form throws away typed homework with no confirmation and lands on the dashboard instead of a profile; TalkBack/VoiceOver users on teacher screens hear 'Parent profile' — wrong persona and wrong destination.
- **Recommendation:** Point onProfileTap at RouteNames.teacherProfile, set profileSemanticLabel per persona, and extend AksharaUnsavedChangesGuard to the homework/leave/marks forms.

### F-037 · [P2] — Loading states are mostly bare spinners — content-shaped skeletons on only ~12 files
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** app-wide · **Role:** all
- **Evidence:** grep AksharaSkeleton/skeleton across lib/features = 12 files (dashboards use AksharaSkeleton.dashboard(), e.g. lib/features/teacher/dashboard/teacher_dashboard_screen.dart:57); MobileAsyncBody supports a skeleton slot (lib/shared/widgets/mobile_async_body.dart:33-43) but most of the 66 consumers pass none, falling back to a centered spinner.
- **Impact:** List-heavy screens (fees, homework, messages, collections) pop from spinner to full content, feeling slower and cheaper than the polished dashboards; inconsistent perceived quality across the app.
- **Recommendation:** Provide list-row and card skeleton presets and pass them through existing MobileAsyncBody call sites.

### F-038 · [P2] — No orientation strategy: landscape is unlocked but no screen adapts to it
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** app shell · **Role:** all
- **Evidence:** grep setPreferredOrientations / OrientationBuilder across lib = zero hits (lib/main.dart has no SystemChrome call); all layouts are portrait-designed single columns with an 80px bottom nav (lib/theme/spacing.dart:40).
- **Impact:** On a phone rotated to landscape, forms have roughly 300px of usable height between the app bar, keyboard, and bottom nav; dashboards become absurdly wide single columns. Nothing breaks, but the experience is degraded and clearly untested.
- **Recommendation:** Lock phones to portrait via SystemChrome (standard for form-heavy apps) while leaving tablets free; revisit only if a landscape use-case emerges.

---

## Reviewer: Web/Desktop UX Specialist  (`web-ux-specialist`)
**Vote:** NOT CERTIFIED · **Scores:** overall 3.5, web 3.5, workflowEfficiency 2, visualDesign 7.5, accessibility 4

**Independent executive assessment:** The web platform is a beautifully engineered viewing shell that cannot perform a single business transaction. The design-system port is genuinely disciplined (M15 tokens 1:1, light/dark, honest empty/error/awaiting-backend states, deep-linkable routes, a real command palette), and the information architecture is coherent. But a full-codebase sweep found ZERO non-auth mutation calls anywhere in web/src — no POST, PUT, PATCH, or DELETE — and 57 of 62 primary action buttons ("Collect fee", "Save marks", "New broadcast", "Add student", "Apply for leave", "Approve") have no onClick handler at all. The flagship workflow is the worst case: in Exam Marks Entry, "Save marks" is wired to `setEdits({})` — it silently DISCARDS everything the teacher typed. A parent's "Pay now" button executes `window.location.hash = ''` (nothing). A teacher clicking a class to mark attendance hits `onRowClick={() => {}}`. The Approval Center — the most desktop-natural workflow in the product — lists pending maker-checker approvals with AI recommendations but offers no approve/reject action. This directly falsifies the project's own claim of "100% in-scope Flutter parity" (web/PARITY_TRACKER.md): the Flutter marks-entry screen is 960 lines with verify/submit-for-approval lifecycle actions; the web version is a 44-line read-only table. The backend is not the blocker — POST routes for offline payments, QR payments, fee assignments, and parent-communication already exist in supabase/functions.

As a desktop tool, the table layer is also shallow: client-side sort only, no pagination against hard pageSize caps (registry fetches 200 students max — a 2,000-student school can never see 90% of its registry), no bulk selection, no column control, and all row interactions are mouse-only (rows and sortable headers are not keyboard-operable; dialogs have Escape but no focus trap). The global search box promises "Search students, staff, actions…" but only fuzzy-matches ~25 sidebar labels. Several routes registered as "REAL_PAGES" call endpoints that don't exist (/school/memories, /parent-meetings, /entitlements/plans) and others are brochure InfoPages with disabled buttons — including "Copilot", a top-level sidebar item. The foundations (theme, states, routing, RBAC-aware nav) are worth keeping wholesale; the product needs its action layer built before it can be called an ERP rather than a report viewer.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Disciplined design-system port: M15 color/spacing/motion tokens mirrored 1:1 from Flutter with full light/dark support and a perf-conscious subset icon font (web/src/index.css, web/src/theme/tokens.ts)
- Honest state architecture — DataUnavailable / Empty / Error-with-retry / entitlement-aware 403 ('Module not enabled') and 422 guidance states; the platform never fabricates business data (web/src/components/system/AsyncBoundary.tsx:37-66)
- Every route deep-links and survives refresh: BrowserRouter paths + localStorage session restore + parametric detail routes with breadcrumbs (router.tsx:550-556, AuthContext.tsx:89-101, DetailPages.tsx:80)
- Cmd/Ctrl+K command palette with arrow-key/Enter navigation exists and is role-filtered (CommandPalette.tsx:81-85)
- Role-aware IA: sidebar filtered by live /auth/permissions grants with role-map fallback; portals (teacher/parent/student) get separate nav shells (navConfig.ts:278-288, AppShell.tsx:19-26)
- Polished login page with a correctly demo-gated persona switcher — RoleSwitcher explicitly hides persona switching in live mode to protect the real session (RoleSwitcher.tsx:10-15, 56)
- SIS Student Registry + student profile drawer→detail flow is a genuinely good list-to-record pattern (StudentRegistryPage.tsx:173-227)

**Findings:**

### F-039 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-06) — Entire web platform is read-only: zero mutation calls, 57/62 action buttons dead
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** all · **Role:** all staff roles
- **Evidence:** grep for method POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE across web/src returns only auth calls (AuthContext.tsx:115,127); grep for useMutation/onSubmit finds only the login form (LoginPage.tsx:157). Heuristic count: 57 of 62 primary action buttons (icon add/save/send/payment/edit/etc.) render with no onClick — e.g. 'Collect fee' (finance/CollectionsPage.tsx:46), 'Create exam' (academics/AcademicsPages.tsx:39), 'New broadcast' (engage/EngagePages.tsx:42), 'Add student'/'Import' (sis/StudentRegistryPage.tsx:68-71), 'Apply for leave' (parent/ParentPages.tsx:213), 'Assign substitute' (AcademicsPages.tsx:151). Backend write routes exist and are unused: POST /finance/payments/offline (supabase/functions/_shared/finance/finance_router.ts:320), POST /finance/payments/qr (:341), POST /finance/fee-assignments (:187).
- **Impact:** No school task can be completed on desktop — the console is a dashboard viewer. Any sales demo that clicks a primary button dead-ends. Contradicts web/PARITY_TRACKER.md's '100% in-scope parity' claim at the capability level.
- **Recommendation:** Build the action layer module-by-module starting with the money and exams paths (collect fee, record offline payment, save marks, approve). Until then, remove or visibly disable dead buttons — a disabled button with a tooltip is honest; an enabled dead button is a trap.

### F-040 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-07) — Marks entry 'Save marks' silently discards all entered marks
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** academics/exams · **Role:** teacher / exam admin
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/academics/AcademicsPages.tsx:90 — actions={<Button icon="save" disabled={!examId || !dirty} onClick={() => setEdits({})}>Save marks</Button>}. The handler clears the local edits map; inputs revert to server values (null → placeholder '—'). No POST exists. Compare Flutter: lib/features/academics/exam_admin/exam_marks_entry_screen.dart (960 lines) with verifyCoordinatorResults (:505-509) and submitForApproval (:539-543).
- **Impact:** A teacher entering marks for 40 students clicks Save and watches every mark vanish. Data loss on the product's declared top-priority module; worse than a missing feature because it simulates one.
- **Recommendation:** Wire save to the marks endpoint with per-row validation (max marks), an unsaved-changes guard on navigation, Enter-to-advance-row, and the frozen AB/ML/DB absent codes (exam-result-status design) which have no web representation at all.

### F-041 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-08) — Parent 'Pay now' button does nothing (sets location.hash to empty)
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** parent portal / fees · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/parent/ParentPages.tsx:98 — const navPay = () => { window.location.hash = ''; }; wired to the 'Pay now' header action (:102). The /parent/payment page it should reach renders only a disabled 'Proceed to pay' inside a dashed placeholder (ParentPages.tsx:142).
- **Impact:** The single highest-intent parent action on web is a no-op. Even accepting that the gateway SDK is owner-gated, the button should at minimum navigate to the payment page; today it looks broken.
- **Recommendation:** Point 'Pay now' at /parent/payment via navigate(); on that page show due amount from /parent/fees and an explicit 'online payment coming soon — pay at school office' state instead of a dead dashed box.

### F-042 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-09) — Teacher cannot mark attendance: class row click is an empty function, homework form cannot submit
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** teacher portal · **Role:** teacher
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/teacher/TeacherPages.tsx:44 — onRowClick={() => {}} on the 'Mark class attendance' list; no marking screen exists behind it. TeacherHomeworkCreatePage: 'Assign' button has no onClick (:91) and both Selects have options={[]} (:94-95) while the page text claims 'Submitting posts to the live /teacher/homework endpoint' (:100). Backend GET /teacher/attendance/classes exists (supabase/functions/_shared/teacher/teacher_router.ts:83) but no web marking flow consumes it.
- **Impact:** The two most frequent daily teacher tasks (attendance, homework) dead-end after one click. The false 'posts to the live endpoint' copy actively misleads.
- **Recommendation:** Build a class-roster marking screen (P/A/L toggles, bulk 'all present', save) and wire the homework form; until then remove the claim text and dead-end rows.

### F-043 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-10) — Approval Center lists maker-checker approvals but has no approve/reject action
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** management / governance · **Role:** principal / management (checker)
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/management/ManagementPages.tsx:106-124 — ApprovalList renders request, AI recommendation, and status columns via ResourceList with no onRowClick, no actions, no detail route. Same for attendance corrections: web/src/pages/attendance/AttendancePages.tsx:51-67 shows pending correction requests with no action, while its own awaiting-backend copy promises 'Approve/reject posts to the live maker-checker endpoint' (:66).
- **Impact:** Every governance flow the product is proudest of (fee concessions FIN-D4, stock write-downs, dues waivers, attendance corrections) has no checker UI on the platform where checkers actually work — desktops. Approvals pile up unless the approver uses a phone.
- **Recommendation:** Add row-level Approve/Reject with a confirm dialog and reason capture; this is the single highest-leverage write feature for desktop.

### F-044 · [P1] — Hard pageSize caps with no pagination — large schools can never see most of their data
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** all list pages (SIS registry, collections, defaulters, etc.) · **Role:** all staff roles
- **Evidence:** fetchList has no paging protocol (web/src/lib/api/list.ts:13-26); every caller hard-codes pageSize 100-400 (StudentRegistryPage.tsx:40 pageSize:200; CollectionsPage.tsx:10 pageSize:200; AcademicsPages.tsx:70 pageSize:400). DataTable has no pager (DataTable.tsx). ResourceList search/filters run client-side over the fetched slice only (ResourceList.tsx:77-85), and the registry's class-filter options are derived from the loaded rows (StudentRegistryPage.tsx:83).
- **Impact:** A 2,000-student school sees '200 of 2000' with no way to reach the rest; searching for a student outside the first page returns nothing, which reads as 'student missing'. This breaks trust in the registry and finance queues at exactly the scale the product sells to.
- **Recommendation:** Add server-side pagination + server-side search to ResourceList as a platform capability (page controls, page-size selector, total from listEnvelope), then remove the hard caps.

### F-045 · [P1] — Global search promises entity search but only jumps to ~25 nav labels
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** shell / command palette · **Role:** all
- **Evidence:** Topbar search button reads 'Search students, staff, actions…' (web/src/components/shell/Topbar.tsx:37); CommandPalette filters only NavItem labels from the sidebar config, capped at 8 results (CommandPalette.tsx:18-29). Typing a student name returns 'No matches.'
- **Impact:** Front-office staff's most common desktop reflex — type a student's name — always fails, on a control that explicitly promises it. Undermines the 'easiest ERP' promise on every page.
- **Recommendation:** Either wire the palette to /sis/students search (backend exists) or change the placeholder to 'Jump to module…' until it can search entities.

### F-046 · [P1] — Routes registered as real pages call endpoints that do not exist (guaranteed 404/error screens in live mode)
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** memories, parent-meetings, entitlements · **Role:** school admin
- **Evidence:** Web calls GET /school/memories (web/src/pages/misc/MiscPages.tsx:45) but the backend route is /memories/events (supabase/functions/_shared/memories/school_memories_router.ts:26). Web calls /parent-meetings (MiscPages.tsx:151) but backend is /parent/meetings (_shared/parent/parent_router.ts:117). Web calls /entitlements/plans (MiscPages.tsx:71) — no such route exists anywhere in supabase/functions. All three are listed in REAL_PAGES (router.tsx:475,486,507).
- **Impact:** Pages certified as 'live-wired' render permanent error states against the real backend — the exact 'previously-certified module with P0 inside' pattern the PRA warned about.
- **Recommendation:** Fix the three paths and add a contract test that every endpoint string in web/src resolves against the backend route inventory.

### F-047 · [P1] — No session-expiry handling: expired token turns every page into an error wall with no path back to login
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** auth / API layer · **Role:** all
- **Evidence:** apiFetch throws ApiError on any !res.ok with no 401 interception or redirect (web/src/lib/api/client.ts:87-90); AuthContext stores only the access token, never a refresh token, and nothing reacts to 401s (AuthContext.tsx:127-135). The known backend /auth/refresh endpoint is never called. AsyncBoundary renders a generic ErrorState for 401s (AsyncBoundary.tsx:63).
- **Impact:** After token expiry a signed-in user sees 'Something went wrong' + Retry (which can never succeed) on every screen, with no 'session expired — sign in again' message. Multi-tab desktop usage makes this a daily event.
- **Recommendation:** Intercept 401 in apiFetch → clear session → redirect to /login with a 'session expired' notice; adopt the refresh-token flow the backend already exposes.

### F-048 · [P1] — Demo 'Explore by role' is reachable in live production builds and creates a token-less broken session
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** auth · **Role:** any visitor
- **Evidence:** LoginPage always renders the demo/credentials tab switcher regardless of mode (web/src/pages/auth/LoginPage.tsx:97-110) and enter() calls loginAsRole unconditionally (:23-26), which persists a preview user with no token (AuthContext.tsx:73-82). In live mode (IS_DEMO=false) queries then run unauthenticated → 401 error states on every page. /qa-login is also registered in the production router (router.tsx:536).
- **Impact:** One click on a public login screen puts a real prospect into an authenticated-looking shell where every panel shows an error — the worst possible first impression, and it persists via localStorage until manual sign-out.
- **Recommendation:** Hide the demo tab and /qa-login when VITE_DATA_MODE=live (the RoleSwitcher already models the correct gating).

### F-049 · [P1] — Tables and dialogs fail basic desktop keyboard/accessibility expectations
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** shared UI (DataTable, Dialog, Drawer) · **Role:** keyboard and screen-reader users
- **Evidence:** DataTable sortable headers are th elements with onClick only — no button role, no tabIndex, no aria-sort (web/src/components/ui/DataTable.tsx:81-99); clickable rows are tr with onClick only — unreachable by Tab, no Enter activation (:103-110). Dialog and Drawer set aria-modal but implement no focus trap, no initial focus, and no focus restoration; Tab escapes into the inert background (Dialog.tsx:34-45, Drawer.tsx:21-30).
- **Impact:** Row-click is the ONLY path to collection details, student drawers, and conversations — all mouse-only. Sorting is mouse-only. Modals leak focus. For a professional desktop tool this blocks keyboard-first users entirely.
- **Recommendation:** Make rows focusable (tabIndex=0, Enter/Space), render header sort controls as buttons with aria-sort, and add a focus trap + restore to Dialog/Drawer.

### F-050 · [P1] — Attendance register dead-ends: 422 guidance tells users to use filters that do not exist
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** attendance · **Role:** school admin
- **Evidence:** AttendanceRegisterPage defines no class/date/year controls — only a text search (web/src/pages/attendance/AttendancePages.tsx:38-49). AsyncBoundary maps 422 to 'Select a class, date or academic year above to load this view' (AsyncBoundary.tsx:54-62). The /attendance/register endpoint requires those parameters, so live mode shows instructions pointing at controls that are not on the page.
- **Impact:** The daily register — a primary admin view — is unreachable, with copy that gaslights the user into hunting for missing filters.
- **Recommendation:** Add class + date pickers to the register (and audit every list page for required-parameter endpoints).

### F-051 · [P1] — Parent portal has no child switcher: multi-child families cannot control whose data they see
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** parent portal · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** ParentFamilyViewPage ('Switch between your children') renders interactive-styled cards with no onClick (web/src/pages/parent/ParentPages.tsx:236-241); no child-context selector exists on attendance/homework/fees pages or the portal shell (PORTAL_NAV parent items, navConfig.ts:98-108).
- **Impact:** A parent with two enrolled children sees ambiguous data everywhere (whose attendance is this?) and the page explicitly built for switching does nothing. OTP-to-parent login makes multi-child accounts the norm, not the edge case.
- **Recommendation:** Add a persistent child selector in the portal topbar and make family cards set that context.

### F-052 · [P1] — Brochure 'InfoPages' with disabled buttons are presented as finished pages — including top-level sidebar item 'Copilot'
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** copilot, setup wizard, workflow, backup, school config · **Role:** school admin
- **Evidence:** InfoPage renders a description paragraph + disabled action (web/src/pages/misc/MiscPages.tsx:12-23); used for /copilot ('New chat' disabled, :37), Setup wizard (:34), Workflow automation (:92), Backup & restore (:121), and more — all in REAL_PAGES (router.tsx:470-508). SchoolConfigPage shows 8 'interactive' cards with no onClick while claiming 'Each area opens its configuration form' (EngagePages.tsx:126-132). AdminHub's 'Audit log' tile navigates to /notifications — a different feature (MiscPages.tsx:104).
- **Impact:** The sidebar's Overview section leads with a feature (Copilot) that is a paragraph about itself. School Setup — the most desktop-appropriate module in the product — is a dead-end grid. 'Audit log' → notifications is a mislabeled destination that erodes trust in navigation.
- **Recommendation:** Move brochure pages behind a 'coming soon' visual treatment or remove them from primary nav; wire School Setup areas or collapse them; point Audit log at a real audit view or remove the tile.

### F-053 · [P1] — Every Reports page across 10 modules is a permanently disabled catalog
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** reports (finance, HR, exams, transport, library, hostel, inventory, alumni, admissions) · **Role:** admin / management
- **Evidence:** ReportsHub defaults ready=false, disabling every Preview and Export button (web/src/components/system/ReportsHub.tsx:25-33,51-56); grep shows no caller ever passes ready — 10 modules render disabled tiles (e.g. ExamReportsPage, AcademicsPages.tsx:113-122).
- **Impact:** Reports are a core reason schools open the desktop app (result sheets, daily collection, defaulter aging). Zero reports can be previewed or exported anywhere; 'Download' on the parent report card is likewise dead (ParentPages.tsx:92).
- **Recommendation:** Wire at least the finance daily-collection and exam result-sheet exports first; keep the rest visibly gated with an expected-date note.

### F-054 · [P2] — Unknown URLs render 'parity in progress' scaffold instead of a 404
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** router · **Role:** all
- **Evidence:** Catch-all route (router.tsx:557) renders ModuleScaffold with 'This route is part of the parity build and will be implemented with its module' for ANY unmatched path — including typos like /financee.
- **Impact:** Users who mistype a URL are told a nonexistent page is 'being recreated for the web' — misleading, and it hides real navigation bugs from QA.
- **Recommendation:** Distinguish known-but-unbuilt paths from a true 404 with a 'Page not found' state and a link home.

### F-055 · [P2] — IA duplication: 'Academics' and 'Examinations' sidebar items open the identical page; Approval Center duplicates Tasks
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** navigation · **Role:** staff
- **Evidence:** navConfig.ts:48-49 lists both /academics and /academics/exams; router maps both to <ExamAdministrationPage /> (router.tsx:277-278). ApprovalCenterPage and ManagementTasksPage render the same ApprovalList over the same /management/tasks endpoint with different titles (ManagementPages.tsx:107,122-127). NavLink prefix-matching also lights both Academics entries at once.
- **Impact:** Two nav items to the same content (and two 'different' pages showing one dataset) make the module tree feel padded and confuse spatial memory.
- **Recommendation:** Collapse duplicates; if Approval Center and Tasks are meant to differ, filter by type server-side.

### F-056 · [P2] — No list/filter/tab state is URL-persisted — desktop deep links lose all context
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** all list pages · **Role:** staff
- **Evidence:** Search terms, filters (ResourceList.tsx:61-62), selected exam in marks entry (AcademicsPages.tsx:69), and the active tab in the student profile (DetailPages.tsx:72) are all useState only — never synced to query params.
- **Impact:** Refresh or sharing a link drops the user's working context (e.g. 'defaulters for Class 8', 'marks entry for Term 1 Physics'). Multi-tab workflows — the reason desks use browsers — lose their state per tab.
- **Recommendation:** Serialize search/filters/selection/tab into URL query params via useSearchParams.

### F-057 · [P2] — Student profile ships 5 tabs of which 3 are permanently empty by design
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** sis · **Role:** school admin
- **Evidence:** toSisProfile documents that the composite endpoint does not carry academic history, fee account, or attendance summary — those sections are left undefined (DetailPages.tsx:33-39), so the Academics/Fees/Attendance tabs always show 'No academic history yet' / 'No fee account linked' / 'No attendance summary' (:94-96). The 'Edit' header button also has no handler (:81).
- **Impact:** The record page invites exploration into three guaranteed dead tabs, teaching users the profile is unreliable.
- **Recommendation:** Fetch those sections from their real endpoints (parent/fees-equivalent, attendance summary) or hide the tabs until wired.

---

## Reviewer: Enterprise SaaS Designer — Information Architecture & Role/Workspace Scale  (`enterprise-saas-designer`)
**Vote:** CERTIFIED — moderate improvements required · **Scores:** overall 6, workflowEfficiency 5, web 6, maturity 6

**Independent executive assessment:** Akshara's navigation architecture is far more disciplined than most ERPs at this stage: a real USER→ROLE→WORKSPACE→TASK model is implemented in code (workspace catalog, role→workspace map, workspace-scoped nav), and hide-first is enforced through five stacked, mutually-reinforcing gates (SchoolBuildScope compile-time hide, ChainScope runtime chain gating, per-school capability wizard, RBAC longest-prefix permission matching with denied-access auditing, and a backend-less-surface gate) that agree between nav filtering and deep-link guarding. Out-of-scope verticals (salon/restaurant/healthcare/accommodation/white-label) are correctly unreachable in the school build on both platforms, and the plan-entitlement UX (locked-not-hidden with WhatsApp upgrade CTA) is genuinely world-class for the Indian school market.

However, the audit found wiring-level IA failures that damage exactly the workflows the product declares most important. The flagship Exams module is silently filtered out of the Admin Hub cards and the mobile bottom nav because the schoolAdministration workspace's module set omits AdminModule.exams — visually confirmed in the admin-hub golden (14 cards, no Exams). An entire academic-operations cluster (~20 destinations including Class Teacher assignment, Subject Assignments, Timetable Automation, Broadcast Admin) hangs off an orphaned "School Completion" hub with zero inbound links, making core setup tasks unreachable in the mobile-first app while the web app exposes them. On web, the Copilot item sits in every staff role's top nav section but resolves to a static brochure card with a disabled button. Applying the 3-click rule to 10 common tasks: 5 pass, 2 marginal, 3 fail (exam marks entry, class-teacher assignment, multi-school portfolio). These are all repairable wiring/config fixes on a sound foundation — moderate improvements required, not a redesign.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Layered hide-first stack is real and airtight for staff surfaces: SchoolBuildScope + ChainScope + school-capability wizard + RBAC + backend-less gate are enforced identically in nav filters AND the deep-link route guard (lib/router/route_guards.dart:396-457), with longest-prefix permission matching explicitly preventing child-route privilege escalation (route_guards.dart:158-173) and denied-access audit logging.
- Plan-entitlement UX is excellent: modules locked by plan are shown-not-hidden with the plan name, a WhatsApp sales CTA and a 'View plans' link — no in-app payment dead-ends (lib/features/entitlements/entitlement_module_gate.dart:64-133; isLocked affordance in admin_nav_models.dart:66-68).
- Persona shells are disciplined and consistent: max 4 primary tabs + a More sheet across parent/teacher/student, with the workspace switcher embedded in the More sheet for multi-hat users (lib/shared/navigation/persona_nav.dart:188; parent_shell.dart:27-110; teacher_shell.dart:22-78).
- One shared AksharaModuleSubNav component powers all 12+ module sub-navs with a well-designed phone overflow (4 inline tabs + pinned More sheet, selected tab always kept visible) so sub-navs cannot drift apart (lib/shared/widgets/akshara_navigation.dart:266-333).
- Global search searches actual entities (students/staff/leads, RBAC-scoped) plus ~20 module shortcuts from every admin screen's app bar — a genuine 2-tap path to any student (lib/features/admin/global_search/global_search_overlay.dart:106,130; admin_content_scaffold.dart:82).
- The web sidebar IA is well-grouped (Overview/People/Academics/Operations/Finance/Engage/Configure) with Examinations as a first-class Academics item, and contains zero out-of-scope verticals (web/src/routes/navConfig.ts:25-84).
- The workspace model is genuinely implemented, not just documented: role→workspace catalog, workspace-scoped nav provider, and per-workspace landing configs exist and function (lib/core/workspace/workspace.dart:59-200, admin_navigation_provider.dart:272-284).

**Findings:**

### F-058 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-11) — Flagship Exams module missing from Admin Hub cards and mobile bottom nav (workspace set omission)
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admin / exams / navigation · **Role:** principal, schoolAdmin, vicePrincipal, management
- **Evidence:** lib/core/workspace/workspace.dart:67-85 — schoolAdministration workspace module set lists 16 modules but NOT AdminModule.exams. lib/features/admin/admin_navigation_provider.dart:58-68 added Exams as a top-level destination ('A5 — un-bury the flagship Exam Administration module'), but the workspace filter (lines 272-284) drops it again for every surface that uses workspaceScopedNavDestinationsProvider: the Admin Hub module cards (admin_hub_screen.dart:28) and the phone bottom nav (admin_bottom_nav.dart:47). activeWorkspaceProvider never resolves null for staff (workspace_providers.dart:24-34), so the filter always applies. Visually confirmed: test/golden/goldens/dark_admin_hub_834x1194.png shows exactly 14 module cards — Marketing, Alumni, Director all present, Exams absent.
- **Impact:** The owner-declared top-priority module is invisible on the two primary mobile surfaces (hub landing + bottom nav) and reachable only via the hamburger drawer, which uses the UNSCOPED provider (admin_navigation_rail.dart:56) — so the drawer and the hub show different module sets in the same session, undermining trust in the nav. Exam marks entry on mobile costs 4+ taps (More drawer → Exams → exam → marks), failing the 3-click rule for the flagship workflow.
- **Recommendation:** Add AdminModule.exams to the schoolAdministration workspace module set (one line), then reconcile the drawer and hub to use the same workspace-scoped provider so the two surfaces can never diverge.

### F-059 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-12) — Entire academic-operations cluster orphaned on mobile — 'School Completion' hub has zero inbound links
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** school_completion / academics setup · **Role:** schoolAdmin, principal
- **Evidence:** Route-graph analysis: RouteNames.schoolCompletionHub ('/school/completion') is registered (app_router.dart:785-788) but no screen or nav surface links to it (grep across lib/ returns only the router registration). Its hub screen (lib/features/school_completion/school_completion_hub_screen.dart:14-141) is the ONLY inbound linker for ~20 destinations including Class Teachers ('Set one class teacher per section (takes attendance)', line 79-83), Subject Assignments, Timetable Automation, Lesson Logs, Room Allocation, Syllabus Automation, Broadcast Admin, Communication Delivery/Analytics, Parent Activation. substituteManager's only other inbound is timetable_optimization_screen.dart:174 — itself reachable only from the orphan hub. The web app DOES expose the cluster via Configure → 'Setup & Ops' (web/src/routes/navConfig.ts:78 → /school-completion).
- **Impact:** Foundational setup workflows — assigning the class teacher who takes attendance, generating timetables, subject assignment, communication broadcasts — cannot be reached through any navigation in the mobile-first Flutter app. A school admin onboarding from a phone or tablet cannot complete school setup; the same tasks are reachable on web only, silently contradicting the mobile-first promise.
- **Recommendation:** Give the cluster a real home: either add an 'Academics Setup' card/destination to the schoolAdministration workspace, or fold its items into the Exams/SIS/Management IA. Also rename the user-facing title 'School Completion' (internal program name) to task language like 'School Setup'.

### F-060 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-13) — Web: Copilot is a first-class sidebar item for every staff role but resolves to a brochure page with a disabled button
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** copilot / navigation · **Role:** all staff roles
- **Evidence:** web/src/routes/navConfig.ts:33 puts 'Copilot' in the Overview section gated on moduleKey 'dashboard', which every staff role holds (roles.ts ROLE_META — even storekeeper has 'dashboard'). web/src/routes/router.tsx:470 maps '/copilot' to Misc.CopilotPage; web/src/pages/misc/MiscPages.tsx:37 defines CopilotPage as InfoPage('Akshara Copilot', … action='New chat') where InfoPage renders the action as <Button … disabled> (MiscPages.tsx:16). The same InfoPage brochure pattern backs '/ai-assistant', SetupWizardPage, AiAssistantSettingsPage and the dynamic-widget pages. The Flutter app, by contrast, routes /copilot to a real copilot experience.
- **Impact:** One of the five 'Overview' items every staff user sees is a static marketing card whose only button is permanently disabled — a guaranteed dead-end in the first minute of any web demo, and a parity break with the mobile app the web was built to mirror.
- **Recommendation:** Remove Copilot from the web sidebar until the real experience ships (hide-first is the house rule), or wire it to the actual AI gateway. Audit all InfoPage-backed routes reachable from visible nav and apply the same rule.

### F-061 · [P1] — Parent 'PTM' tile in the More sheet dead-ends at an Access Denied screen
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent / navigation · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent/shell/parent_shell.dart:94-98 — static More-sheet entry for RouteNames.parentPtm. lib/router/app_router.dart:2610-2619 — parentPtmRouteBuilder returns AccessDeniedScreen because parentPtm is in SchoolBuildScope.hiddenRoutePrefixes (school_build_scope.dart:76-77, gated off until the PTM backend ships). MoreNavSheet renders spec.more with no hidden-route filtering (lib/shared/navigation/persona_nav.dart:197-216).
- **Impact:** A primary persona (parent — the product's core login audience) has a visible menu item that always produces a lock-icon 'Access Denied' error. The hide decision was made but only half-enforced: the route is blocked, the nav entry is not. Erodes trust and looks broken in front of parents.
- **Recommendation:** Filter persona More-sheet entries through SchoolBuildScope.isRouteHidden (the same source of truth the route builder uses), so hiding a route always hides its nav entry.

### F-062 · [P1] — Franchise vertical (owner-decided OUT of scope) is not build-hidden — only chain-gated, with 4 roles holding its permission
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** franchise / scope governance · **Role:** schoolAdmin, principal, vicePrincipal, management (chain orgs)
- **Evidence:** lib/core/config/school_build_scope.dart:49-53 — franchise is deliberately excluded from hiddenRoutePrefixes and instead chain-gated via ChainScope (chain_scope.dart:26). lib/core/security/role_permissions.dart:220,325,428,527 grant Permission.viewFranchiseOperations to schoolAdmin/principal/vicePrincipal/management; route_guards.dart:58 maps /franchise to that permission. Org Builder (reachable for chain orgs) still advertises non-school verticals: kAdminModuleInfo describes it as provisioning 'schools, salons, hospitals, and restaurants' (admin_navigation_provider.dart:402-408) and the mock repository serves salon/hospital/restaurant packs to the hub (mock_organization_builder_repository.dart:17-48).
- **Impact:** For any real chain organization, out-of-scope business verticals resurface: /franchise stays a live, permission-granted route, and the Organization Builder presents salon/hospital/restaurant packs. This contradicts the recorded scope decision (franchise/salon/restaurant/healthcare/white-label = never) on the exact tenants ('KEEP multi-school') that remain in scope.
- **Recommendation:** Add RouteNames.franchise to SchoolBuildScope.hiddenRoutePrefixes, strip viewFranchiseOperations from role grants, and filter Org Builder to school packs only (including copy).

### F-063 · [P1] — Admin bottom-nav tabs are declaration-ordered, giving a principal Marketing over SIS/Exams/Management
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admin / navigation · **Role:** principal, schoolAdmin
- **Evidence:** lib/features/admin/admin_bottom_nav.dart:31,50 — _maxTabs=4, visible = first 4 of the workspace-scoped list. Order comes from kAllAdminNavDestinations declaration order (admin_navigation_provider.dart:17-221): Admin Hub, Admissions, Marketing, Finance precede SIS, Exams, Management. Principal holds all these view perms (role_permissions.dart:299+).
- **Impact:** A principal's four permanent one-tap destinations are Admin Hub, Admissions, Marketing, Finance. The daily academic surfaces (SIS, Management, Exams — the latter missing entirely per P0-1) all cost an extra 'More' step. Slot allocation reflects code declaration order, not role task frequency.
- **Recommendation:** Order bottom-nav slots per workspace/role priority (e.g., principal: Hub, SIS, Exams, Management) or make slots configurable per role landing config.

### F-064 · [P1] — Cashier's primary task (Collections) is buried in the finance sub-nav overflow with no dashboard shortcut
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** finance / navigation · **Role:** financeAdmin
- **Evidence:** lib/features/finance/finance_navigation.dart:7-22 — kFinanceNavScreens order puts feeStructures/studentAccounts/feeAssignment before collections (5th). AksharaModuleSubNav shows only 4 inline tabs on phones (akshara_navigation.dart:279 maxInlineOnMobile=4), so Collections lands in the More sheet. The finance dashboard's only quick links go to Defaulters (finance_dashboard_screen.dart:74,100,140).
- **Impact:** The single most frequent daily job in an Indian school office — collecting a fee — requires Finance → More → Collections on the phone, while rarely-used setup screens (Fee Structures, Fee Assignment) occupy the golden inline slots. Repeated dozens of times per day, this is real cumulative friction.
- **Recommendation:** Reorder kFinanceNavScreens by frequency (Dashboard, Collections, Student Accounts, Defaulters first) and add a 'Collect fee' primary action to the finance dashboard.

### F-065 · [P1] — Single-module staff land on the Admin Hub instead of their workspace dashboard
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** auth / landing · **Role:** librarian, hostelManager, transportManager, admissionsCounselor, storekeeper
- **Evidence:** lib/features/auth/qa_login_persona.dart:207-216 — homeRouteForStaffErp special-cases only financeAdmin, inventoryManager, principal, vicePrincipal; every other ERP role falls through to RouteNames.admin. Each workspace already defines a homeRoute (workspace.dart:45-46, e.g., library → libraryDashboard) that the login lander ignores. The near-empty single-card hub this produces is visible in test/golden/goldens/workspace_switcher_multihat_834x1194.png (one module card, >50% blank canvas).
- **Impact:** A librarian logs in to a hub whose only content is a single 'Library' card — an extra tap on every session and a hollow first impression, inconsistent with financeAdmin who lands straight on the finance dashboard. Contradicts the USER→ROLE→WORKSPACE→TASK landing intent already encoded in the workspace model.
- **Recommendation:** Replace the hand-maintained switch with activeWorkspace.homeRoute so every role lands on its workspace home by construction.

### F-066 · [P1] — Web role→module scoping diverges from Flutter for the same roles (hide-first parity broken)
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** auth / rbac / navigation · **Role:** financeAdmin, transportManager, hostelManager, librarian
- **Evidence:** web/src/lib/auth/roles.ts — financeAdmin modules include 'admissions' and 'sis'; transportManager/hostelManager/librarian include 'sis'. On Flutter, financeAdmin's workspace contains only AdminModule.finance (workspace.dart:95-103) and their hub/bottom-nav show finance alone. Also web gates Director, Alumni, Intelligence and Attendance all on the coarse 'management' moduleKey (navConfig.ts:31,32,42,56), so any management-module holder sees all four.
- **Impact:** The same person sees materially different module sets on phone vs desktop: a finance admin gets Admissions and Students on web but not on mobile. Role-scoping is the product's central promise ('hide-first'); platform divergence makes access feel arbitrary and complicates RBAC reasoning, training and support.
- **Recommendation:** Derive both platforms' nav scoping from one shared role→module contract (the /auth/permissions grants already planned as the override), and split the coarse 'management' moduleKey into per-module keys on web.

### F-067 · [P1] — Orphan routes: Multi-School Portfolio, Branches, Backup & Restore, AI Content, Homework Intelligence have no inbound navigation
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** multi_school / admin / intelligence · **Role:** superAdmin, chain admins
- **Evidence:** Full route-graph scan (302 route constants vs all context.push/go references outside router core): zero inbound links for multiSchoolPortfolio, branches, backupRestore, aiContent, homeworkIntelligence, schoolCompletionHub, plus the deliberately-hidden set. Raw-path grep confirms no string-literal navigation either. Backup & Restore is registered at app_router.dart:582-586 with a real screen (lib/features/admin/backup/backup_restore_screen.dart) but nothing links to it; /multi-school/portfolio and /branches contradict the 'KEEP multi-school management' decision by being unreachable even for chain orgs.
- **Impact:** Real, permission-mapped screens (including an operationally important backup-status view and the entire multi-school portfolio surface) are dead weight: reachable only by hand-typed deep link. Each orphan also keeps its guard/permission wiring alive, inflating the IA that must be reasoned about.
- **Recommendation:** For each orphan: either wire a visible entry (Backup under Management→Settings; Portfolio/Branches under Director for chain orgs) or delete the route. Add a CI check that every registered route has at least one inbound nav reference or an explicit orphan-exemption.

### F-068 · [P1] — Org Builder hub shows a 'Setup wizard' button that dead-ends at Access Denied
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** platform / organization_builder · **Role:** chain admins with viewOrganizationBuilder
- **Evidence:** lib/features/platform/organization_builder/organization_builder_hub_screen.dart:146-151 — visible TextButton pushes RouteNames.setupWizard; school_build_scope.dart:70 lists setupWizard in hiddenRoutePrefixes ('demo wizard; real onboarding is separate'), so ErpRouteGuard (route_guards.dart:411-413) renders AccessDeniedScreen.
- **Impact:** On a reachable chain-org surface, tapping a labeled action produces 'Access Denied' — the second instance of a hide decision enforced at the route but not at its visible entry points.
- **Recommendation:** Remove the button (or point it at the real unified onboarding flow); add a lint/test that no visible action targets a SchoolBuildScope-hidden route.

### F-069 · [P2] — Web 'Configure' section sprawls across five overlapping destinations with indistinguishable labels
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** settings / IA · **Role:** schoolAdmin
- **Evidence:** web/src/routes/navConfig.ts:75-83 — Configure section: 'School Setup' (/school-config), 'Setup & Ops' (/school-completion), 'Operations' (/operations), 'Admin' (/admin), 'Settings' (/settings). Three of the five names are near-synonyms; '/operations' resolves to a KPI page (MiscPages.tsx:88) and '/admin' to another hub (MiscPages.tsx:99).
- **Impact:** A school admin looking for any configuration task must guess among five vaguely-named doors; the distinction (per-school capabilities vs setup checklist vs ops health vs platform admin vs personal settings) is not expressed by the labels. Classic settings sprawl.
- **Recommendation:** Collapse to two: 'School Settings' (capabilities, branding, academic setup) and 'My Settings' (appearance, AI); fold ops-health KPIs into Management.

### F-070 · [P2] — Internal program vocabulary leaks into IA: 'School Completion' title, phase4/phase5 modules, stale module descriptions
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** naming / IA hygiene · **Role:** all staff
- **Evidence:** school_completion_hub_screen.dart:12 — AppBar literally titled 'School Completion' (an internal roadmap phase name); lib/features/phase4/ + phase5/ and lib/router/phase4_navigation.dart/phase5_navigation.dart name modules after roadmap phases; kAdminModuleInfo retains stale copy — Admissions: 'will be built on this shell. Not started yet.' and Admin Hub: 'Module screens will be added in upcoming releases' (admin_navigation_provider.dart:290-300) — for fully-built modules. That copy renders only via AdminModulePlaceholderScreen (admin_module_placeholder_screen.dart:24), which is itself now dead code with no route referencing it.
- **Impact:** Mostly latent, but the 'School Completion' title is user-visible (and web nav labels its path 'Setup & Ops'), and the stale 'Not started yet' descriptions are one accidental re-wire away from appearing in production UI — the exact failure mode that previously misled this project's own audit (admin_navigation.dart:45-51).
- **Recommendation:** Rename user-facing 'School Completion' to 'School Setup'; delete AdminModulePlaceholderScreen and the stale descriptions; fold phase4/phase5 glue into their real feature modules.

### F-071 · [P2] — Out-of-scope verticals persist as latent modules behind a single compile-time flag
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** verticals / scope governance · **Role:** superAdmin
- **Evidence:** ~20 salon/restaurant/healthcare/accommodation/white-label route names (route_names.dart:211+, app_router.dart:1060-1185 registrations), 5 dedicated nav files (lib/router/salon_navigation.dart etc.), AdminModule enum entries (admin_nav_models.dart:24-31), and superAdmin still granted viewHealthcare/viewSalonBusiness/viewRestaurantHospitality/viewAccommodation (role_permissions.dart:166-172). All hidden solely by SchoolBuildScope.enabled = true (school_build_scope.dart:19) — 'hide now, delete later' with delete never executed.
- **Impact:** Currently unreachable (verified: hidden in nav + route-guarded + backend-less-gated), so no user-facing defect today — but the entire out-of-scope product ships in the binary, one const flip from resurfacing, and permanently taxes every IA/RBAC/nav audit with 20+ phantom destinations.
- **Recommendation:** Execute the approved 'delete later' step for the four business verticals and white-label: remove routes, nav files, enum entries and permissions (git history preserves them).
