## Reviewer: Human-Computer Interaction Specialist  (`hci-specialist`)
**Vote:** NOT CERTIFIED · **Scores:** overall 5, workflowEfficiency 6, mobile 7, web 2

**Independent executive assessment:** Akshara's Flutter app contains pockets of genuinely world-class interaction engineering on its three highest-frequency workflows: fee collection, teacher attendance, and exam marks entry all have debounced draft autosave with explicit Resume/Discard recovery, uniform double-submit guards on every mutation notifier (state.isLoading check plus idempotency keys for money), honest offline "queued" ceremonies distinct from green success ceremonies, and thoughtful ergonomics like exception-first attendance grids and keyboard focus-chaining down the marks roster. This is better than most school ERPs I have evaluated. However, the quality is an island. Outside those three flows the interaction model fragments into three different validation behaviors for the same "required field" pattern (disabled-until-valid, silent dead button, and no validation at all), raw AlertDialogs with 16 unvalidated TextFields in HR versus design-system dialogs in finance, barrier-dismissible multi-field dialogs that destroy input on an outside tap, one-tap no-confirmation Approve in the principal's approval center, one-tap no-confirmation publication of parent-visible exam results, and essentially zero undo anywhere (3 SnackBarActions in the whole app).

Three findings prevent certification. First, the admissions→finance fee handoff — the exact cross-module journey this product's north star is built around — silently discards the clerk's fee-structure selection: the dropdown visually updates but its onSelected callback is never wired at either call-site, and "Send to Finance" falls back to the first structure in the list, meaning a student can be silently enrolled on the wrong fee plan. Second, the web ERP, built as a "1:1 parity recreation," is in reality a read-only viewer: the only POSTs in the entire web codebase are login/OTP, and I counted 42 enabled-looking primary action buttons ("Collect fee", "Add book", "Apply for leave", "Assign homework") with no onClick handler, plus attendance rows wired to an empty function. A cashier or admin at a desk cannot complete a single workflow on web. Third, the parent "Pay now" journey terminates in a simulated gateway (submitMockPayment) that renders a real success ceremony and receipt without moving money — tracked as owner-gated, but a certification-blocking journey as shipped.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Draft autosave + explicit Resume/Discard on the three highest-frequency flows: fee collection (finance_workflow_actions.dart:943-1009, never silently prefills a money amount), attendance (teacher_attendance_screen.dart:149-190), exam marks (exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:192-239) — half-finished work survives phone-lock/app-switch/kill
- Uniform double-submit protection: every finance mutation notifier begins with `if (state.isLoading) return` (finance_mutations_provider.dart:120,192,228,...) and collections dedupe via idempotency key; QR confirm button disables while busy (finance_qr_payment_screen.dart:61,169)
- Honest offline money UX: offline-recorded collection closes with an amber 'Payment queued — receipt issued once it syncs' ceremony with warning haptic, deliberately distinct from the green server-confirmed success ceremony (finance_workflow_actions.dart:832-878)
- Attendance marking ergonomics: exception-first grid, 'Fill remaining present', live present/absent/late tally, and a submit button that reads 'N unmarked' and stays disabled until the roster is complete (teacher_attendance_screen.dart:280-314,366-392,478-487)
- Marks entry: Enter/Tab focus-chaining down the roster, per-row save spinners, row_version optimistic-concurrency to catch concurrent overwrites, range validation against maxMarks, and AB/ML/DB status codes that lock the marks field (exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:254-264,666-688,875-936)
- Destructive finance actions consistently confirm with mandatory typed reason and disabled-until-valid confirm (executeCancelCollection + _promptForReason, finance_workflow_actions.dart:1355-1447)
- Out-of-scope verticals (salon/restaurant/healthcare/franchise) are correctly unreachable: hidden from nav and blocked at the route guard (school_build_scope.dart:22-42, route_guards.dart:411)
- Standardized loading/error/empty/retry states via shared async-body wrappers across Flutter modules (e.g. FinanceAsyncBody, AksharaLoadingState/ErrorState/EmptyState)

**Findings:**

### F-072 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-14) — Admissions→Finance fee handoff silently discards the clerk's fee-structure selection
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admissions/finance handoff · **Role:** Admissions clerk
- **Evidence:** lib/features/admissions/fee_handoff/widgets/admissions_fee_handoff_panel.dart:81-96 — the 'Fee structure' DropdownMenu's onSelected is null unless onFeeStructureChanged is passed; lib/features/admissions/fee_handoff/admissions_fee_handoff_screen.dart:63-74 and 106-117 — BOTH call-sites omit onFeeStructureChanged, and onSendToFinance falls back to `selected.selectedFeeStructureId ?? feeStructures.first.id`. Flutter's DropdownMenu updates its own visible text on selection even with a null onSelected, so the UI confirms a choice that is never stored.
- **Impact:** The clerk picks fee structure B, sees B in the field, taps 'Send to Finance', and the system silently sends structure A (first in list). A student can be enrolled on the wrong fee plan with no error, on the product's flagship cross-module journey. Silent wrong-money assignment.
- **Recommendation:** Wire onFeeStructureChanged into a provider that updates the handoff's selectedFeeStructureId, and make 'Send to Finance' hard-require an explicit selection (disabled until chosen) instead of falling back to feeStructures.first.

### F-073 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-15) — Web ERP is a read-only shell: every primary workflow action is a dead button
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** web (all modules) · **Role:** Cashier / Teacher / Admin on desktop
- **Evidence:** Only POSTs in the entire web app are auth (web/src/lib/auth/AuthContext.tsx:115,127 — /auth/login, /auth/verify-otp); zero useMutation/POST in any page. 42 `<Button icon="add">` primary actions with no onClick (e.g. web/src/pages/finance/CollectionsPage.tsx:46 'Collect fee', web/src/pages/library/CatalogPage.tsx:39 'Add book', web/src/pages/teacher/TeacherPages.tsx:78 'Assign homework', :182 'Apply for leave'). TeacherPages.tsx:43-44 attendance rows wired to `onRowClick={() => {}}`. TeacherPages.tsx:87-104 'Assign homework' form has empty Select options, a dead 'Assign' button, and copy claiming 'Submitting posts to the live /teacher/homework endpoint'.
- **Impact:** No fee can be collected, no attendance marked, no marks entered, no leave applied, nothing approved on web. Buttons render enabled and styled as primary CTAs, so users click and get silence — the worst feedback failure class. The 'once the backend is connected' copy contradicts the enabled buttons. This would fail instantly in a sales demo at a desk.
- **Recommendation:** Either wire the mutations (the Supabase endpoints exist — the Flutter app calls them) or remove/disable the dead CTAs and label the web app read-only. Never render an enabled primary button with no handler.

### F-074 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-16) — Parent 'Pay now' completes a simulated payment with a real-looking receipt
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/payments · **Role:** Parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_screen.dart:115 — `onPay: () => submitMockPayment(ref)`; lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_provider.dart:104-112 — mock flow with a parentPaymentSimulateFailureProvider switch; success view then shows 'Payment successful' + receipt number (parent_payment_screen.dart:338-347).
- **Impact:** The parent's primary money journey ends in a fake gateway that renders a success ceremony and receipt without moving money. Known owner-gated dependency (payment-gateway SDK), but as shipped the journey is a production-blocking simulation.
- **Recommendation:** Gate the 'Pay now' CTA off entirely (with a 'pay at school office' explainer) until the real gateway lands; never show the success ceremony from a mock path in production builds.

### F-075 · [P1] — One-tap Approve with no confirmation and no undo in the Principal Approval Center
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** management/approvals · **Role:** Principal
- **Evidence:** lib/features/management/approval/approval_center_actions.dart:8-32 — approveApprovalRequest executes immediately on tap; no confirm dialog, no undo. Lines 29 and 65 surface raw `$error` strings to the user, unlike finance's ApiFailure mapping.
- **Impact:** The approval center is the maker-checker backstop for money items (concessions, fee structures, refunds). A single accidental tap irreversibly approves; the two-person control is defeated by a mis-tap. Raw exception text also leaks to the principal.
- **Recommendation:** Add a confirm step summarizing the item (type, amount, requester) before approve; route errors through the shared failure mapper.

### F-076 · [P1] — Attendance-correction dialog pre-fills a canned excuse and a hardcoded date '12 Jun 2026'
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/attendance · **Role:** Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_workflow.dart:31-34 — reason defaults to 'Biometric sync error — student was present' and dateController defaults to text '12 Jun 2026'; the date is a free-text AksharaFormField (line 63-67), not a date picker (AksharaDateField exists in lib/shared/forms/).
- **Impact:** A governed, principal-audited correction flow ships with a fabricated default reason and a wrong default date. Teachers submitting quickly will file corrections dated 12 Jun 2026 with an excuse they never wrote — corrupting the audit trail the flow exists to protect.
- **Recommendation:** Default the date to the roster date being corrected via the shared date field; leave reason empty and required (disabled-until-valid, like finance's _promptForReason).

### F-077 · [P1] — 'Publish results' to parents is a single tap with no confirmation or summary
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** academics/exams · **Role:** Exam coordinator / Principal
- **Evidence:** lib/features/academics/exam_admin/exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:488-497 — FilledButton 'Publish results' calls _publishDirect directly; :558-572 — publishes and snackbars 'Published N student results' with no pre-confirmation, no summary of missing/absent marks, no undo.
- **Impact:** The single most consequential action in the top-priority module (results become parent-visible) has less friction than cancelling an invoice. An accidental tap publishes a whole class's results; the count of affected students is revealed only AFTER the irreversible act.
- **Recommendation:** Confirm with a summary (class, subject, N students, M absent, K unmarked) before publishing; consider a short revocation window.

### F-078 · [P1] — Same 'required field' pattern has three contradictory behaviors across modules
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** cross-module (forms) · **Role:** All staff
- **Evidence:** lib/shared/forms/akshara_form_field.dart:68 — `required` only appends '*' to the label, no enforcement. Behavior A (correct): finance _promptForReason disables confirm until valid (finance_workflow_actions.dart:1434-1439). Behavior B (silent dead button): library Add book (library_workflow_actions.dart:403-408) and finance discount rule (:543-546) silently `return` on empty required fields — tap produces nothing, no error shown. Behavior C (no validation): HR Add employee pops true with all fields blank (hr_workflow_actions.dart:643-647), refund dialog (finance_workflow_actions.dart:295-300), concession dialog (:376-381), exam grace 'Reason (required)' never checked (exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:792-816).
- **Impact:** Users cannot build a mental model of what '*' means. Behavior B is a feedback black hole (button appears broken); Behavior C submits blanks that fail server-side AFTER the dialog is gone, so all typed input is lost and must be re-entered.
- **Recommendation:** Enforce required-field validation inside AksharaDialogActions/AksharaFormField once (disabled-until-valid plus inline errors) and migrate all dialogs onto it.

### F-079 · [P1] — Dialog forms lose all input on outside tap or mutation failure; no loading state after confirm
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** cross-module (dialogs) · **Role:** All staff
- **Evidence:** lib/shared/widgets/akshara_motion.dart:154 — showAksharaDialog defaults barrierDismissible: true (7-field refund and HR employee dialogs dismiss on an outside tap with no unsaved-changes guard; AksharaUnsavedGuard is adopted in exactly one place: lib/features/sis/profile/sis_profile_edit_sheet.dart). All dialog flows pop BEFORE executing the mutation, and AksharaDialogActions has no isLoading support (lib/shared/widgets/akshara_dialog.dart:14-53), so after 'Record payment'/'Create' the screen shows nothing until the snackbar/ceremony arrives; on failure the dialog is gone and input is unrecoverable for every flow without draft autosave.
- **Impact:** Dead-air after every confirm invites re-taps and uncertainty on slow networks; a network failure on a 7-field form (refund, employee, book) costs full re-entry. Only 3 flows are protected by drafts.
- **Recommendation:** Keep the dialog open with a spinner on confirm and render server errors inline; set barrierDismissible: false (or unsaved-guard) for multi-field forms.

### F-080 · [P1] — 'All absent'/'All present' bulk actions wipe an in-progress roster with no confirm or undo
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/attendance · **Role:** Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_provider.dart:120-134 — applyBulkMark overwrites EVERY student's mark unconditionally; buttons sit side-by-side at equal weight (teacher_attendance_screen.dart:280-302). Debounced autosave then persists the wiped state over the previous draft.
- **Impact:** A teacher who has hand-marked 35 students and fat-fingers 'All absent' loses everything instantly, and the draft-recovery net saves the destroyed state. 'Fill remaining present' (which is non-destructive) already covers the legitimate fast path, making the destructive pair mostly a trap.
- **Recommendation:** Confirm when marks would be overwritten, or make bulk actions apply only to unmarked students; offer a snackbar Undo.

### F-081 · [P1] — Refund and concession creation require typing raw internal IDs and free-text student names
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** finance/refunds · **Role:** Finance officer
- **Evidence:** lib/features/finance/finance_workflow_actions.dart:259-264 — 'Fee account ID' free-text with hint 'e.g. acct_1024'; :339-373 — concession takes free-text student name + account ID. Contrast the collection dialog, which builds a real invoice picker from loaded data (:734-753), and student-accounts search which passes student context (:928-931).
- **Impact:** Money mutations targeted by hand-typed database IDs are maximally error-prone (typo → refund/concession against the wrong account or a failed submit after the dialog closed). Two students with the same name are indistinguishable. The product already demonstrates the right pattern one dialog away.
- **Recommendation:** Reuse the searchable student/account picker (PSID/name/class) for refunds and concessions; never expose acct_* IDs to users.

### F-082 · [P1] — Create-record interaction is a different product in each module
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** cross-module (HR/inventory/SIS/transport/hostel vs finance/library) · **Role:** Admin staff
- **Evidence:** HR: 16 raw TextFields, 0 AksharaFormField, plain AlertDialog, zero validation (hr_workflow_actions.dart:575-680). Inventory purchase order: 'Items' as one free-text field, unvalidated amount (inventory_workflow_actions.dart:16-105). SIS (sis_workflow_actions.dart:39,110,187,294), transport (:24,75,123,216) raw AlertDialog; hostel mixes raw and design-system dialogs in one file (hostel_workflow_actions.dart:56-594); finance/library use AksharaAlertDialog + AksharaFormField with icons and required markers.
- **Impact:** The same daily task (create a record) presents different visual shells, different validation, different required-field affordances depending on module — breaking the 'easiest school ERP' promise and multiplying training cost; HR can create employees with empty names.
- **Recommendation:** One shared create-record dialog/wizard primitive (the finance/library pattern) adopted everywhere; lint against raw AlertDialog+TextField in feature code.

### F-083 · [P1] — Create fee structure pre-fills magic money values and validates nothing
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** finance/fee-structures · **Role:** Finance admin
- **Evidence:** lib/features/finance/finance_workflow_actions.dart:64-66 — fields pre-filled with 'New fee structure', '₹1,85,000', 'Nursery – 12'; :90-95 — confirm pops true unconditionally; request hardcodes a Tuition category of ₹1,45,000 (:108-114).
- **Impact:** One tap on 'Create' produces a plausible-looking but fabricated fee structure (₹1,85,000 with a ₹1,45,000 tuition line) that then enters the approval pipeline — an error-prevention failure on money configuration.
- **Recommendation:** Empty defaults, required validation, and a real category-line editor instead of a hardcoded tuition line.

### F-084 · [P2] — Rejection-reason prompt in approval center throws away the dialog before telling you the reason was required
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** management/approvals · **Role:** Principal
- **Evidence:** lib/features/management/approval/approval_center_actions.dart:39-47 — 'Reject' pops with whatever text is present; the empty-reason check happens AFTER the dialog closes, surfacing a snackbar and forcing a restart. Finance's identical prompt disables confirm until valid (finance_workflow_actions.dart:1434-1439).
- **Impact:** Wasted round-trip and inconsistent behavior for the identical interaction two screens apart.
- **Recommendation:** Reuse finance's disabled-until-valid reason prompt as the single shared component.

### F-085 · [P2] — Receipt-link rows show an 'open in new' affordance but are inert
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** finance/collection-detail · **Role:** Finance officer
- **Evidence:** lib/features/finance/collection_detail/finance_collection_detail_screen.dart:516-543 — InkWell with onTap: null yet renders Icons.open_in_new; the code comment admits admin has no receipt view so navigation would be bounced.
- **Impact:** A false affordance on the money screen: users tap, nothing happens, trust erodes.
- **Recommendation:** Drop the open_in_new icon (render as plain text rows) until an admin receipt view exists.

### F-086 · [P2] — 'N saved, M failed' bulk-marks feedback never identifies which rows failed
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** academics/exams · **Role:** Teacher / Coordinator
- **Evidence:** lib/features/academics/exam_admin/exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:306-312 — Save-all outcome is a transient snackbar '{saved} saved, {failed} failed' with no per-row error marking.
- **Impact:** After '38 saved, 2 failed' the teacher must hunt through the roster to find the 2 failures before the deadline; the snackbar vanishes in seconds.
- **Recommendation:** Mark failed rows inline (error border + retry) and keep a persistent banner until resolved.

### F-087 · [P2] — Bottom-nav tab labels wrap to two cramped lines on phones
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** app shell · **Role:** Parent / Teacher
- **Evidence:** test/golden/goldens/persona_shell_parent_390x844.png — tabs 2 and 4 of the 5-tab bottom bar render two-line wrapped labels at 390px width; test/golden/goldens/persona_shell_teacher_390x844.png shows the same.
- **Impact:** Visually noisy primary navigation on the most common phone width; labels compete with the center action button.
- **Recommendation:** Shorten tab labels to single words or reduce label font/tab count so labels never wrap.

---

## Reviewer: Accessibility Specialist (WCAG 2.1 AA lens)  (`accessibility-specialist`)
**Vote:** CERTIFIED — moderate improvements required · **Scores:** overall 5.5, accessibility 5, mobile 7, web 4

**Independent executive assessment:** Akshara ERP is a tale of two platforms through an accessibility lens. The Flutter mobile app has one of the strongest accessibility foundations I have audited in a Flutter codebase: 48dp touch targets are enforced at the theme level and guarded by lint tests, a real AksharaAccessibility helper computes WCAG ratios with luminance-picked on-colors, text scaling is left free everywhere except two sanctioned dense-grid clamps at 1.3x, 348 Semantics wrappers plus 363 semanticLabels cover 159/296 screens and effectively all shared widgets, and the dark theme's text contrast is outstanding (11-15:1). However, the light theme's semantic tones (warning amber, tertiary teal) fail AA at 3.07-3.74:1 on the 11-12px chip/badge labels where they are actually used — including the "Today" badge on the parent exams screen — because the design system's own contrast tests misapply the WCAG "large text" floor to 12px labels. Input field boundaries sit at ~1.2:1 against the page on both platforms (visually confirmed in the enrollment golden), and the parent OTP login — the front door for every parent — has unassociated labels, a meaningless '••••••' hint, and no AutofillHints.oneTimeCode, so every parent hand-types the OTP.

The web app (built as a 1:1 parity port) got the primitives right (label association via useId, aria-hidden icon pattern, landmarks, role=status toasts, focus-visible rings on buttons) but is not keyboard-operable where it matters most: DataTable's clickable rows and sortable headers are plain onClick handlers with no tabindex, role, or key handling, which means a keyboard-only user cannot open a student, lead, approval, employee, or collection record on at least 8 core list pages — the product's central list-to-detail pattern. Dialogs have no focus trap, initial focus, or focus restore; the web OTP screen is six anonymous inputs that reject pasted codes; there is no skip link and no reduced-motion support. The mobile product is close to certifiable with targeted token and auth fixes; the web product needs a focused keyboard-operability pass before wide rollout.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Theme-level touch-target enforcement: AksharaSpacing.minTouchTarget=48 (lib/theme/spacing.dart:43) with dedicated lint tests asserting IconButton/Checkbox/FilledButton >=48dp and padded root tap-target size (test/theme/tap_target_lint_test.dart)
- Real accessibility engineering infrastructure: lib/theme/accessibility.dart implements WCAG contrast math, luminance-picked onColorFor glyph colors, and governed text-scale clamps; contrast is regression-tested in test/theme/rendered_contrast_audit_test.dart and contrast_checker_test.dart
- Text scaling is free by design: zero global textScaleFactor suppression in lib/; only two sanctioned dense-grid clamps at 1.3x (marks grid lib/shared/marks_grid/marks_grid.dart:114, attendance exception grid) per documented policy (accessibility.dart:17-21)
- Dark theme text contrast is world-class: onSurface 15.4:1, onSurfaceVariant 11.4:1, all semantic text tones 4.7-10.1:1 on their containers (computed from lib/theme/color_tokens.dart dark factory)
- Semantics coverage flows through the shared widget layer: 30 shared widgets (error/empty/loading/nav/KPI/chips/app bar) carry Semantics, so even screens without direct annotations inherit accessible names; 159/296 screens add their own
- State coverage is systematic, not sampled-in: 208/296 screens use governed async state handling, 260 reference error/retry paths, 122 use dedicated empty states — loading states even carry semanticLabels (exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:101,115)
- Web primitives were built accessibly: Field.tsx associates labels via useId/htmlFor with text-based (not color-only) errors; Icon.tsx applies aria-hidden to decorative icons and role=img+aria-label when titled; Toast uses role=status; shell has main/nav/aside/header landmarks and html lang="en"
- Exam marks entry (top-priority workflow) is the best-governed screen: semantic loading labels, Semantics-wrapped grid, and the only permitted text-scale clamp applied exactly per policy

**Findings:**

### F-088 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-17) — Web DataTable rows and sort headers are mouse-only — keyboard users cannot open records on core list pages
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** shared web UI (DataTable) — affects SIS, Admissions, HR, Finance, Teacher, Parent, Alumni · **Role:** any keyboard-only or switch-access user (admin/clerk personas most affected)
- **Evidence:** web/src/components/ui/DataTable.tsx:103-109 — <tr onClick={() => onRowClick?.(row)}> with no tabIndex, no role, no onKeyDown; :81-91 — sortable <th onClick={toggleSort}> is not a button, no keyboard path, no aria-sort. onRowClick drives navigation on 8 core pages: web/src/pages/sis/StudentRegistryPage.tsx, admissions/LeadsPage.tsx, admissions/ApprovalPage.tsx, hr/EmployeesPage.tsx, finance/CollectionsPage.tsx, teacher/TeacherPages.tsx, parent/ParentPages.tsx, alumni/AlumniPages.tsx
- **Impact:** The list-to-detail pattern is the web ERP's primary journey. A keyboard-only user can reach a table but can never open a student, lead, approval, employee, or collection record, and can never sort. This is a hard WCAG 2.1.1 failure that blocks daily workflows and would fail any enterprise accessibility procurement review (VPAT).
- **Recommendation:** In DataTable: render sortable headers as <button> inside <th> with aria-sort; give clickable rows tabIndex={0}, role="button" (or a real link in the first cell), Enter/Space key handling, and a focus-visible style. One component fix repairs all 8+ pages.

### F-089 · [P1] — Parent OTP login (the product's front door) has no accessible labels and no OTP autofill
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** auth · **Role:** parent (and staff via staff OTP)
- **Evidence:** lib/features/auth/login_screen.dart:188-226 — visible 'Mobile number' Text is not programmatically associated (no labelText/Semantics; TalkBack announces only hint '9876543210'); lib/features/auth/otp_verification_screen.dart:213-232 — OTP TextFormField's only description is hintText '••••••' and there is no autofillHints: [AutofillHints.oneTimeCode]; grep confirms the entire auth module (6 screens) contains zero Semantics/semanticLabel annotations — the only module cluster at the app's entry with none
- **Impact:** Every parent session starts here. Screen-reader users hear an unnamed edit box whose hint is bullets; sighted users get no SMS OTP autofill on iOS (oneTimeCode) or Android, so the product's signature 'OTP to parent phone' flow forces manual code transcription on every login — friction plus an AA labeling failure (WCAG 1.3.1/2.5.3) on the primary journey.
- **Recommendation:** Add labelText (or Semantics textField label) to both fields, autofillHints: [AutofillHints.telephoneNumber] on phone and [AutofillHints.oneTimeCode] on OTP, and announce the 'code sent' confirmation via SemanticsService/live region.

### F-090 · [P1] — Light-theme warning/tertiary text fails AA on the small labels where it is actually used, on both platforms
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** design system (color tokens) — surfaces in parent exams, teacher timetable, chips/badges/banners everywhere · **Role:** low-vision users in every role
- **Evidence:** Computed from lib/theme/color_tokens.dart: warning #D97706 on warningContainer #FFFBEB = 3.07:1 and on white = 3.19:1; tertiary #0D9488 on white = 3.74:1, on tertiaryContainer = 3.32:1 (all need 4.5:1 at 11-12px). Concrete uses: lib/features/parent/exams/widgets/upcoming_exam_card.dart:71 ('Today' badge, labelSmall 11px); lib/features/teacher/timetable/widgets/timetable_period_row.dart:84,90 (tertiary bodySmall 12px); web Chip.tsx:11, KpiCard.tsx:24, SyncBanner.tsx:27, Misc.tsx:13 all pair text-warning with bg-warning-container at ak-label sizes. Root cause: test/theme/rendered_contrast_audit_test.dart:49 holds chip labels to the 3.0 'large text' floor on the claim 'bold >=14 → large-text', but WCAG large text is 14pt (18.66px) bold — lib/theme/typography.dart labelSmall/labelMedium are 11/12px, so the CI gate is passing text that fails AA.
- **Impact:** Amber warning text — the tone used for exactly the states users must not miss (due today, pending, offline sync) — is the least readable text in the light theme across both apps. The dark theme passes everywhere, so this is a light-token calibration issue, not a design overhaul.
- **Recommendation:** Introduce an onWarningContainer/onTertiaryContainer text tone (e.g. amber-800 #92400E = 7.3:1 on #FFFBEB, teal-800) for text, keeping current tones for icons; correct the large-text floor in the audit test to >=18.66px bold so CI enforces reality.

### F-091 · [P1] — Text input boundaries are effectively invisible — non-text contrast ~1.2:1 on both platforms and both themes
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** design system (input decoration) — all forms · **Role:** all data-entry roles (clerk, teacher, admin)
- **Evidence:** lib/theme/app_theme.dart:618 — enabledBorder = outlineVariant (#E2E8F0, 1.23:1 on white) at 0.75 alpha ≈ 1.17:1; fill surfaceContainerLow #F8FAFC vs white page = 1.07:1; dark outlineVariant #3D4250 on #1A1D24 = 1.68:1. Web identical: Field.tsx:15 'border-outline-variant/75' (#CBD5E1 = 1.48:1 before alpha). Visually confirmed in test/golden/goldens/qa_x_028_admissions_enrollment_390x844.png — the four form fields read as faint grey smudges whose borders are imperceptible.
- **Impact:** WCAG 1.4.11 requires 3:1 for input boundaries. Low-vision users (and anyone on a low-brightness or high-glare screen — common in Indian school offices) cannot tell where fields begin, whether a field exists, or how many fields a form has until they tap around. Affects every form in the product including admissions enrollment and marks entry dialogs.
- **Recommendation:** Darken the enabled border to slate-400 (#94A3B8 = 3.0:1) or add a persistent 3:1 bottom border; the focused 2px primary border is already good.

### F-092 · [P1] — Web dialogs and drawers have no focus management — no trap, no initial focus, no restore
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** shared web UI (Dialog, Drawer) · **Role:** keyboard and screen-reader users, all roles
- **Evidence:** web/src/components/ui/Dialog.tsx:34-70 — Escape handler and aria-modal exist, but nothing moves focus into the dialog on open, nothing constrains Tab (focus continues into the scrim-obscured page behind), nothing restores focus on close; Drawer.tsx:23,45-47 identical pattern. Only 1 autoFocus and 2 onKeyDown exist in all of web/src.
- **Impact:** Every confirm/create/edit dialog in the web ERP (fee collection, approval confirmations, destructive actions marked dismissible=false) leaves keyboard users typing into hidden background content — the classic modal failure (WCAG 2.4.3). For destructive confirmations this can mean acting on the wrong surface.
- **Recommendation:** Add a small focus-trap effect to Dialog/Drawer (store document.activeElement, focus the panel on mount, wrap Tab at the sentinels, restore on unmount) — one shared fix.

### F-093 · [P1] — Web OTP entry: six unlabeled inputs, no one-time-code autocomplete, pasted codes rejected
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** auth (web) · **Role:** all web users at login
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/auth/OtpVerificationPage.tsx:65-71 — six <input maxLength={1}> with no aria-label ('digit N of 6'), no autoComplete="one-time-code", and set() at :43-49 rejects any multi-character value, so pasting the 6-digit code fills nothing; no onPaste handler exists.
- **Impact:** Screen-reader users hear six anonymous edit boxes; every user who copies the code from SMS-forwarded email/WhatsApp finds paste silently broken and must retype per digit. Login is the one flow every user must pass.
- **Recommendation:** Add aria-label per digit, autoComplete="one-time-code" on the first input, and an onPaste handler that distributes digits.

### F-094 · [P2] — Design-system secondary/marginal pairs sit just under AA in light theme
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** design system (color tokens) · **Role:** low-vision users
- **Evidence:** Computed from lib/theme/color_tokens.dart light factory: onSurfaceVariant #64748B on surfaceContainer #F1F5F9 = 4.34:1 (secondary text on tinted tiles/containers); error #DC2626 on errorContainer #FEF2F2 = 4.41:1. Both pass the project's own 3.0 floor (accessibility.dart criticalSchemePairs sets onSurfaceVariant to the large-text floor) but fail AA 4.5 at body/label sizes; web mirrors the same values (index.css:49,52-53).
- **Impact:** Secondary text on any container-tinted surface (KPI tiles, list metadata) and error-banner text are marginally below AA — individually small, but these are the two most-used text tones after onSurface.
- **Recommendation:** Shift onSurfaceVariant to slate-600 #475569 (7.1:1 on white, 6.4:1 on container) and error-container text to red-700; both are one-line token changes already covered by existing tests.

### F-095 · [P2] — No reduced-motion support on web; Flutter honors it in only one widget
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** motion system · **Role:** vestibular-disorder users
- **Evidence:** grep across web/src returns zero hits for useReducedMotion/MotionConfig/prefers-reduced-motion/motion-reduce while framer-motion animates every Dialog, Drawer, Toast, RoleSwitcher, and page card (e.g. Dialog.tsx:51-70, OtpVerificationPage.tsx:54); in Flutter only lib/shared/widgets/premium/akshara_mount_fade.dart:55 checks MediaQuery.disableAnimations
- **Impact:** Users with vestibular sensitivity who set OS-level reduced motion still get scale/slide animations on every dialog, page mount, and toast (WCAG 2.3.3). Not blocking, but a systemic gap that is trivial to close centrally.
- **Recommendation:** Wrap the web app in framer-motion's <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">; audit remaining Flutter explicit animations against disableAnimations.

### F-096 · [P2] — No skip-to-content link on web — keyboard users traverse the full sidebar on every page
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** web shell · **Role:** keyboard-only users
- **Evidence:** web/src/components/shell/AppShell.tsx:66 has a <main> landmark and Sidebar.tsx:28 a <nav>, but grep for 'Skip to' returns nothing; the sidebar (navConfig.ts drives up to dozens of NavLinks per role) precedes main content in DOM order
- **Impact:** Screen-reader users can jump by landmark, but sighted keyboard users must Tab through every sidebar item plus topbar on every page before reaching content (WCAG 2.4.1).
- **Recommendation:** Add a visually-hidden-until-focused 'Skip to content' anchor as the first focusable element in AppShell.

### F-097 · [P2] — Web charts carry no text alternative (Flutter equivalent does)
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** charts (dashboards) · **Role:** screen-reader users on director/finance/admissions dashboards
- **Evidence:** grep of web/src/components/charts/Charts.tsx for aria-/role= returns zero hits — SVG charts render with no accessible name or summary; the Flutter counterpart lib/shared/widgets/akshara_chart.dart:49-51 wraps every chart in Semantics(label: semanticLabel ?? title)
- **Impact:** Dashboard KPIs are duplicated as text tiles (mitigating), but trend charts on director/finance dashboards are silent to screen readers on web — a parity regression against the app's own mobile standard.
- **Recommendation:** Add role="img" + aria-label summarizing series and latest value to the chart wrapper, mirroring the Flutter semanticLabel.

### F-098 · [P2] — Weak focus indicator (50%-alpha ring) and unannotated RoleSwitcher menu on web
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** shared web UI (Button, RoleSwitcher) · **Role:** keyboard users
- **Evidence:** web/src/components/ui/Button.tsx:49 — 'focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/50' (≈#8DABED blended on white ≈ 2.2:1, below the 3:1 focus-indicator guidance); web/src/components/shell/RoleSwitcher.tsx:27-36 — menu trigger has no aria-expanded/aria-haspopup and the open menu closes only via click-away div (no Escape handler)
- **Impact:** Focus is technically visible but faint on light surfaces; the identity/persona menu is operable but announces no state to screen readers and traps no Escape.
- **Recommendation:** Use a solid 2px primary ring (ring-primary, no alpha) and add aria-expanded + Escape handling to RoleSwitcher.

### F-099 · [P2] — Fixed-height feedback banners truncate at large text scale
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** shared widgets (warning banner) · **Role:** large-text users (elderly parents, low-vision staff)
- **Evidence:** lib/shared/widgets/akshara_warning_banner.dart:14,61,77-78 — default height: 56 SizedBox with maxLines: 2 + ellipsis; at 1.5-2x system text scale a two-line warning ellipsizes and the truncated tail is unrecoverable (no tap-to-expand)
- **Impact:** Warning banners carry exactly the content (sync pending, action required) a large-text user must read in full; graceful (no overflow crash) but lossy. The pattern is the exception — most of the app scales freely — which is why it stands out.
- **Recommendation:** Replace the fixed height with minHeight (IntrinsicHeight or padding-based layout) so the banner grows with text scale.

### F-100 · [P2] — Screen-level Semantics coverage is uneven — strongest modules 100%, entry and SIS modules thin
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** cross-cutting (semantics) · **Role:** screen-reader users
- **Evidence:** Corpus measurement: 159/296 screens carry direct Semantics/semanticLabel; per-module: platform 14/29, finance 12/16, parent 11/23, but auth 0/6, academics 1/6, sis 4/9, admissions 4/10, admin 1/3 — the gap clusters at high-traffic entry (auth) and record-keeping (SIS) modules; shared-widget semantics (30 files) partially compensates
- **Impact:** Screens composed purely of shared widgets remain navigable, but custom content regions (student registry rows, exam admin summaries) in the thin modules lack accessible names/grouping, making TalkBack traversal noisy where clerks spend the most time.
- **Recommendation:** Prioritize a semantics pass on auth (see P1 above), sis, and academics/exam_admin — the shared-widget vocabulary already exists, so this is annotation work, not redesign.

---

## Reviewer: School ERP Domain Expert (15 years Indian K-12 ERP implementations)  (`school-erp-domain-expert`)
**Vote:** NOT CERTIFIED · **Scores:** overall 4.5, workflowEfficiency 5, maturity 4

**Independent executive assessment:** Akshara is two products wearing one skin. The teacher-facing daily loops are genuinely excellent — the attendance roster (exception-first grid, "Fill remaining present", draft autosave, correction requests) and the exam marks grid (Enter/Tab focus chaining, AB/ML/DB status codes that never write a spurious zero, audited grace/moderation, coordinator-to-principal verification chain) are among the best I have seen in this segment, and the defaulters screen's one-tap WhatsApp + promise-to-pay workflow shows real understanding of how Indian schools actually chase fees. But the office-clerk journeys — the ones that process money and admissions all day — do not survive contact with a real front desk. The fee-collection dialog lets a cashier post student A's balance against an unrelated, school-wide invoice preselected from a global first-200 list whose labels don't even show a student name; the admissions "View" button silently submits a draft application and there is no application detail screen at all; the entire admission funnel is stitched together with in-session "journey context" state (with a MockAdmissionsWriteStore call still in the live submit path), so a clerk cannot resume a half-done admission tomorrow or safely interleave two families today. The web ERP — the natural home of a fee counter — has no fee-collection write path whatsoever: its "Collect fee" button has no handler.

The exam module (declared top priority) enters marks beautifully but creates exams badly: free-text date/time fields prefilled with stale hardcoded values ("15 Mar 2026", "Term 2", "Room 8A"), raw enum labels ("unitTest", "halfYearly") in the type dropdown, and a one-dialog-per-class-section-subject model that means dozens of manual creations per exam series. Year-2 operations are structurally absent in a live build: promotion/reshuffle/section-balance are backend-less and gated off, and fee assignment only exists as an admissions-handoff queue, so continuing students can never be re-billed. Demo scaffolding ("Route 12"/"Route 08" baked into transport filter logic, decorative filter chips on the admissions dashboard, hardcoded "FY 2026-27" year chips) would surface within minutes of a competent sales evaluation. The foundations, reliability patterns, and teacher UX deserve certification; the money and admission office flows require redesign, not polish.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Teacher daily attendance is world-class for this segment: exception-first dense grid, All-present / Fill-remaining-present, roll-number search, debounced draft autosave surviving phone-lock, sticky live tally, and a post-submit correction-request path (lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_screen.dart:280-390)
- Exam marks entry honors real Indian school rules: AB/ML/DB status codes lock the marks field and never write a fake zero, Enter/Tab keys straight down the roster, bulk Save-all, marks-entry deadline banner, audited grace/moderation with mandatory reason, and a coordinator-verify -> principal-approve publication chain (lib/features/academics/exam_admin/exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:254-321, 641-664, 747-831)
- Fee defaulters recovery matches ground reality: one-tap WhatsApp to the guardian with a prefilled polite reminder, contact logging, and promise-to-pay tracking (lib/features/finance/defaulters/finance_defaulters_screen.dart:516, 646-649, 921-935, 1140)
- Money-path reliability ceremony is honest: offline collections close with an amber 'Payment queued - receipt issued once it syncs' instead of a fake green success; completed ones show amount + receipt number (lib/features/finance/finance_workflow_actions.dart:832-878)
- TC issuance is dues-gated fail-closed with the outstanding amount surfaced, generates a serialled PDF, and flips the student to Transferred (lib/features/sis/sis_workflow_actions.dart:282-373)
- Correct Indian-school vocabulary in data surfaces: admission number in collections/accounts/exports, class-section labels, term labels, day-close lock for the cash counter (finance_collections_screen.dart:501-511, 189)

**Findings:**

### F-101 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-18) — Fee counter can post a payment against the wrong student's invoice
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** finance · **Role:** Fee clerk / cashier
- **Evidence:** lib/features/finance/finance_workflow_actions.dart:728-762 — the Record-collection dialog builds its invoice picker from financeInvoicesProvider (ALL school invoices, page 1 pageSize 200 per lib/features/finance/invoices/finance_invoices_provider.dart:16) and preselects journeyInvoice ?? defaultInvoiceId('inv_1') ?? invoices.first. Labels (lines 734-738) are 'INV-nnn · Term · ₹X due' with NO student name. When opened from a student's account via 'Collect fee' (lib/features/finance/student_accounts/finance_student_accounts_screen.dart:267-275) only defaultAmount/studentLabel are passed — the invoice list is never scoped to that student.
- **Impact:** The highest-frequency daily workflow (fee counter: search student -> collect -> receipt) prefills student A's balance while preselecting an arbitrary, possibly different student's invoice; the cashier cannot tell because invoice labels carry no student name, and a busy counter will hit Record payment. Money posts to the wrong account. Beyond mis-posting, a student whose invoice is not in the first 200 school-wide invoices cannot be collected against at all.
- **Recommendation:** When the collection originates from a student account, filter the picker to that student's open invoices and preselect the oldest due; always render the student name + admission number in the invoice option label; remove the 'inv_1' fallback.

### F-102 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-19) — Web ERP has no fee-collection write path at all
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance · **Role:** Fee clerk (desktop counter)
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/finance/CollectionsPage.tsx:46 — actions={<Button icon="add">Collect fee</Button>} has NO onClick handler; web/src/pages/finance/FinanceDashboardPage.tsx:46-48 — the dashboard 'Collect fee' button merely navigates to /finance/collections (the page with the dead button). web/src/pages/finance/StudentAccountsPage.tsx has no collect action (grep found zero collect handlers across web/src).
- **Impact:** The desktop web app — the natural device for a school fee counter — is read-only for the single most important daily finance workflow. The button renders, invites a click, and does nothing: a broken primary journey that would fail the first minute of a sales demo. The claimed 1:1 Flutter parity does not hold on the money path.
- **Recommendation:** Either implement the collect dialog on web (scoped to a searched student) or remove the dead 'Collect fee' buttons and label the surface read-only until the write path ships.

### F-103 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-20) — Admissions 'View' button silently submits a draft application; no application detail screen exists
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admissions · **Role:** Admissions clerk / counselor
- **Evidence:** lib/features/admissions/applications/admissions_applications_screen.dart:125-148 — _handleApplicationAction: if status==draft it immediately calls submitApplicationProvider.execute (no confirmation); for any other status it shows only a snackbar with the status. The triggering control is labeled 'View' / tooltip 'View application' (lib/features/admissions/applications/widgets/admissions_applications_table.dart:70-71, 131-132). No route or screen renders application contents anywhere in lib/features/admissions/.
- **Impact:** Tapping the only affordance on an application row performs an irreversible workflow mutation the clerk believed was read-only — a half-entered draft gets submitted for approval. And since no detail view exists, staff can never re-open an application to check the child's details, class sought, or documents before approving: the approval queue asks them to approve what they cannot inspect.
- **Recommendation:** Build an application detail screen (profile, documents, status history, edit-while-draft); make 'View' navigate to it; make Submit an explicit, confirmed action on that screen.

### F-104 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-21) — Admission funnel cannot be resumed or safely interleaved — continuity lives only in in-session memory, with a mock store in the live path
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admissions · **Role:** Admissions clerk
- **Evidence:** lib/features/admissions/admissions_journey_context_provider.dart:7-17 — active application/enrollment/approval ids are plain StateProviders defaulting to null (lost on app restart). lib/features/admissions/enrollment/admissions_enrollment_provider.dart:97-113 — submit() binds the enrollment to whatever admissionsActiveApplicationIdProvider holds (null after restart, or the LAST application touched, not necessarily this family's) and then queries MockAdmissionsWriteStore.instance.findApprovalByApplication in the live submit path. The enrollment wizard has no DraftAutosaveMixin (admissions_enrollment_screen.dart:61 uses only an in-memory unsaved-changes guard), unlike fee collection and marks entry.
- **Impact:** Indian admission offices process many families concurrently over days (document chase, fee negotiation). Here, a clerk who approves family A today and opens Enrollment tomorrow produces an enrollment orphaned from (or wrongly linked to) its application; a phone-lock mid-way through the 4-step wizard loses everything. The funnel works only as a single linear demo run, not as a work queue — and the mock-store dependency means the approval linkage silently no-ops in an API build.
- **Recommendation:** Make the enrollment wizard take an applicationId route parameter from the approval/fee-handoff queue; persist wizard drafts like REL-3 does for collections/marks; remove MockAdmissionsWriteStore from the live provider.

### F-105 · [P1] — Exam creation is a per-class-section-subject dialog with free-text dates and stale hardcoded prefills
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** academics/exams · **Role:** Exam coordinator
- **Evidence:** lib/features/academics/exam_admin/widgets/exam_create_dialog.dart:21-24 — termController prefilled 'Term 2', dateController '15 Mar 2026', timeController '9:00 AM - 10:30 AM', venueController 'Room 8A'; date/time/venue are AksharaFormField free-text (lines 144-156), no date picker, no validation ('exam date' accepts anything). Lines 112-126 — exam type dropdown renders raw enum names via type.name: 'unitTest', 'weeklyTest', 'halfYearly' (lib/features/education/education_models.dart:12-19); exam_administration_screen.dart:150 also shows exam.examType.name on cards.
- **Impact:** Exams are the declared top-priority module. A Unit Test across Classes 6-10 (6 subjects x 3 sections) requires ~90 separate dialog submissions with manual retyping of the date each time; a free-text past-date prefill ('15 Mar 2026') will actually get saved by hurried coordinators, corrupting deadlines and schedules; 'halfYearly'/'unitTest' camelCase leaking into a principal-facing dropdown reads as unfinished software.
- **Recommendation:** Add an exam-series creation flow (pick term + classes + subjects grid -> generate sessions), real date/time pickers with future-date validation, and human labels for exam types ('Unit Test', 'Half-Yearly').

### F-106 · [P1] — Transport allocation never raises the transport fee demand; route filters are hardcoded demo names
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** transport + finance · **Role:** Transport manager / Finance
- **Evidence:** lib/features/transport/allocation/transport_allocation_screen.dart:24-29 — filterLabels hardcode 'Route 12'/'Route 08'; lib/features/transport/transport_providers.dart:244-250 — the filter logic literally matches routeName.startsWith('Route 12'). Zero fee/demand/invoice references exist anywhere in lib/features/transport/ (grep across allocation/routes returned nothing). Transport fee exists only as a boolean 'Include transport add-on' toggle at admissions fee-handoff time (lib/features/finance/fee_assignment/finance_fee_assignment_screen.dart:380-382, finance_models.dart:230-237).
- **Impact:** The locked TRN-9 design says Transport raises the demand and Finance collects on the unified student account — but allocating a student to a route (including the very common mid-year join) produces no fee consequence anywhere in the UI, and there is no route/distance-based fee slab at all. Meanwhile a school whose routes are named 'Gandhi Nagar' gets two filter chips that always return empty lists. Transport revenue will silently leak.
- **Recommendation:** Drive route filters from the live route list; on allocation (and deallocation), raise/adjust a transport fee demand on the student's account per route slab, with the pro-rata rule surfaced.

### F-107 · [P1] — Year-2 operations are structurally impossible in a live build: promotion hidden, no re-billing for continuing students
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** sis + finance · **Role:** School admin
- **Evidence:** lib/router/surface_backend_gate.dart:26-28 — '/sis/promotion', '/sis/reshuffle', '/sis/section-balance' are declared backend-less and hidden (route blocked + nav dropped) whenever enableApiMode is on. lib/features/finance/fee_assignment/finance_fee_assignment_screen.dart:54-63 — fee assignment exists ONLY as the 'Admissions handoff queue' ('No students awaiting fee assignment' when empty); there is no class-wise/bulk fee assignment surface for existing students anywhere in lib/features/finance/.
- **Impact:** At the end of the academic year a school must promote ~all students and generate next-year fee accounts for every continuing student. In the live product the promotion screens do not exist (hidden), and even if they did, Finance can only create fee accounts for students arriving through admissions. Every implementation I have run hits this wall in month 10-12; it is a guaranteed churn event, invisible during a pilot's first months.
- **Recommendation:** Prioritize a backend for promotion/section-balance and add a 'Bulk assign fee structure' flow (class + year -> structure + plan -> generate accounts) before any school's second year.

### F-108 · [P1] — TC dues-pending failure is a dead-end snackbar with no path to resolution
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** sis · **Role:** Front office / registrar
- **Evidence:** lib/features/sis/sis_workflow_actions.dart:340-354 — on 409 DUES_PENDING the flow shows a transient SnackBar with the outstanding amount and stops. No action to open the student's fee account, collect, or invoke the dues-waiver maker-checker (which exists per SCE-1) is offered.
- **Impact:** TC issuance is a parent-standing-at-the-desk moment. The clerk sees '₹X pending' flash for four seconds, then must remember the amount, navigate to Finance, re-search the same student, resolve, and return to SIS to retry — typically with the parent watching. A gate without a door.
- **Recommendation:** Replace the snackbar with a dialog showing the dues breakdown and two actions: 'Collect now' (opens the scoped collection flow) and 'Request dues waiver' (maker-checker), then auto-retry TC issuance.

### F-109 · [P1] — Fee handoff silently applies the FIRST fee structure when none was chosen
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admissions + finance · **Role:** Admissions clerk
- **Evidence:** lib/features/admissions/fee_handoff/admissions_fee_handoff_screen.dart:70-73 and 113-116 — onSendToFinance passes feeStructureId: selected.selectedFeeStructureId ?? (feeStructures.isNotEmpty ? feeStructures.first.id : '') — an unselected structure falls back to feeStructures.first with no confirmation.
- **Impact:** 'Send to Finance' with no explicit structure selection bills the child under whatever structure happens to sort first (e.g., a Class 1 structure for a Class 9 admission). Wrong fee demands discovered by parents are among the most escalation-prone errors in a school's first term.
- **Recommendation:** Disable 'Send to Finance' until a fee structure is explicitly selected (or default to the structure mapped to the student's class, clearly shown).

### F-110 · [P1] — Decorative filter chips: admissions dashboard filters are wired to nothing; year chips are hardcoded
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admissions / sis / management · **Role:** Principal / counselor
- **Evidence:** lib/features/admissions/dashboard/admissions_dashboard_provider.dart:13-16 — admissionsDashboardFilterProvider stores the tapped index but admissionsDashboardFutureProvider watches only repositoryQueryProvider; grep shows no other consumer, so 'This month / All counselors / All sources' change nothing. lib/features/sis/dashboard/sis_dashboard_screen.dart:25 hardcodes '2026–27'; lib/features/management/dashboard/management_dashboard_screen.dart:32-34 hardcodes 'FY 2026-27' / 'Q1'.
- **Impact:** Users tap a filter, the chip highlights, the data does not change — this erodes trust in every number on the dashboard ('is anything here real?'). Hardcoded academic-year chips become false labels the day the session rolls over.
- **Recommendation:** Wire the chips to the query (or remove them), and source year/quarter chips from the academic-year config.

### F-111 · [P1] — Out-of-scope verticals (Salon, Restaurant, Healthcare) still shipped: routes, nav entries, and permissions intact
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** navigation · **Role:** Super admin
- **Evidence:** lib/router/app_router.dart:1060-1160 — live GoRoute definitions for healthcare/salon/restaurant surfaces; lib/features/admin/admin_navigation_provider.dart:174-191 — nav items labeled 'Healthcare', 'Salon', 'Restaurant'; lib/core/security/role_permissions.dart:166-173 — superAdmin holds viewHealthcare/viewSalonBusiness/viewRestaurantHospitality/viewAccommodation. Mitigation exists: lib/router/surface_backend_gate.dart:32-35 hides them when enableApiMode is on — i.e., they remain fully reachable in any non-API (mock/dev/demo) build.
- **Impact:** The owner declared these verticals permanently out of scope. A demo or dev-mode build shows 'Salon' and 'Restaurant' in a school ERP's navigation — instantly undermining the 'easiest school ERP' positioning if a prospect or reviewer sees it, and dead code weight in every navigation audit.
- **Recommendation:** Delete the vertical routes, nav entries, and permissions outright rather than gating them; the gate protects live builds only.

### F-112 · [P2] — Internal build codes leak into user-facing copy ('AD-05')
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** sis · **Role:** SIS clerk
- **Evidence:** lib/features/sis/admissions_conversion/sis_admissions_conversion_screen.dart:65 — on-screen helper text reads 'Convert AD-05 enrollment records into SIS student profiles.'
- **Impact:** A clerk has no idea what 'AD-05' means; engineer-speak in body copy signals unfinished software and forces a training-manual explanation for what should be self-evident.
- **Recommendation:** Rewrite as 'Convert approved enrollments into student profiles.'

### F-113 · [P2] — Attendance sticky tally omits half-day and excused counts
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher attendance · **Role:** Class teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_screen.dart:458-459 — the pre-submit tally renders only '$presentCount present · $absentCount absent · $lateCount late' although halfDay/excused marks exist (attendance_models.dart:4, 101-104) and count toward the submit gate.
- **Impact:** A teacher who marked 2 half-day and 1 excused sees numbers that do not add up to class strength at the moment of submission — a brief but daily moment of doubt on a flow that is otherwise excellent.
- **Recommendation:** Include half-day/excused in the tally when non-zero.

### F-114 · [P2] — QR Pay entry binds to a hardcoded 'inv_1' fallback and raw invoice-ID typing
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** finance · **Role:** Cashier
- **Evidence:** lib/features/finance/collections/finance_collections_screen.dart:48 — journeyInvoiceId = financeLastInvoiceIdProvider ?? 'inv_1'; lib/features/finance/finance_qr_payment_screen.dart:40 — the invoice field defaults to the literal 'inv_1' and is a free-text ID the cashier must edit by hand.
- **Impact:** Fresh session + QR Pay = a UPI QR generated against a fictional invoice id unless the cashier hand-types a real one; UPI-at-the-counter is a headline workflow for Indian schools and should start from a student, never from a raw ID.
- **Recommendation:** Launch QR Pay only from a student/invoice context (student accounts or an invoice row); remove the 'inv_1' fallback.

## Reviewer: QA Usability Reviewer — Forms & States  (`qa-usability-reviewer`)
**Vote:** NOT CERTIFIED · **Scores:** overall 5, workflowEfficiency 5, maturity 5, accessibility 6

**Independent executive assessment:** I sampled 14+ form surfaces across both platforms: admission enrollment (Flutter wizard + web page), fee collection (Record Collection dialog, QR Pay, fee structures), exam marks entry, homework creation (both platforms), leave requests (teacher, parent, HR web), settings (both platforms), and login/OTP (both platforms), plus a placeholder/dead-screen sweep. The two platforms are in radically different leagues. The Flutter app has one of the strongest form foundations I have reviewed in the school-ERP class: a shared form library with required-field indicators, inline per-step validation with scroll-to-first-error, an unsaved-changes guard that also arms the browser beforeunload, WhatsApp-grade draft autosave with explicit Resume/Discard on money and marks forms, honest amber "queued" vs green "success" ceremonies for offline payments, keyboard focus-chaining through marks rosters, and optimistic-concurrency guards surfaced as user-comprehensible errors. However, adoption of these patterns is inconsistent: the teacher leave form uses free-text date fields with a silently-disabled submit and a no-feedback failure path; homework creation targets classes and students by free-typed strings; the fee-structure dialog opens prefilled with demo money values and hardcodes a Tuition category regardless of the total entered; and the QR payment screen falls back to a fabricated invoice 'inv_1' and a magic ₹5,000 amount.

The web ERP — positioned as a 1:1 parity recreation and live-certified on prod routes — is, from a forms-and-states standpoint, a read-only shell. Its API layer contains no write method except OTP request; every primary action button on core workflows ("Collect fee", "Assign homework", "Apply for leave", "Hand off to Finance", HR leave Approve/Reject) is either a dead button with no handler or hardcoded disabled; the homework create page renders empty dropdowns with a dead "Assign" button while its caption falsely claims it "posts to the live /teacher/homework endpoint"; settings toggles are no-ops; and the legal page fabricates "Accepted" status for four policies. A route-render certification masked the fact that no core workflow can be completed on web. The Flutter app alone would merit CERTIFIED_MODERATE; the product as shipped across both surfaces cannot be certified from my lens.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Flutter shared form system is genuinely excellent: lib/shared/forms/akshara_form_field.dart (required '*' indicator, inline error, semantics, invisible input bounds), akshara_date_field.dart (picker-backed, malformed input impossible), akshara_multi_step_form.dart with scroll-to-first-error (admissions_enrollment_screen.dart:38-52)
- Data-loss protection is best-in-class where applied: draft autosave with explicit Resume/Discard on the fee-collection money dialog (finance_workflow_actions.dart:943-1026), marks-entry grid drafts that survive phone-lock/app-kill (exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:161-239), parent leave drafts with a visible 'Draft saved' chip (parent_leave_screen.dart:91-144), and AksharaUnsavedChangesGuard incl. web beforeunload (akshara_unsaved_guard.dart:5-11)
- Honest state design: offline fee collection closes with an amber 'Payment queued — receipt issued once it syncs' ceremony instead of a fake green success (finance_workflow_actions.dart:832-854); success ceremonies show amount + receipt number + method
- Marks entry workflow efficiency: Enter/Tab focus-chaining down the roster, per-row save with range validation, batch 'Save all' with 'N saved, M failed' summary, row_version lost-update guard, deadline banner (exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:252-321, 666-688)
- Login ergonomics on both platforms: +91 prefix, digits-only, 10-char cap, actionable inline validation (login_screen.dart:193-238); web login has real error display, loading, autocomplete (LoginPage.tsx:157-175)
- Placeholder discipline in Flutter navigation: the dead AdminModulePlaceholderScreen builder was removed and a comment guards against re-adding it (lib/router/admin_navigation.dart:49-52); zero 'coming soon' dead-ends on primary Flutter journeys

**Findings:**

### F-115 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-22) — Web ERP is read-only: every core-workflow action button is dead or hardcoded disabled
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance / admissions / teacher / hr (web) · **Role:** cashier / teacher / HR / admissions clerk
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/finance/CollectionsPage.tsx:46 — <Button icon="add">Collect fee</Button> has no onClick; web/src/pages/teacher/TeacherPages.tsx:78 ('Assign homework'), :91 ('Assign'), :182 ('Apply for leave') — no handlers; web/src/pages/hr/LeavePage.tsx:40-45 — Approve/Reject hardcoded `disabled`; web/src/pages/admissions/EnrollmentPage.tsx:48 — 'Hand off to Finance' hardcoded `disabled`; web/src/pages/detail/DetailPages.tsx:81,154,184 — 'Edit'/'Download PDF'/'Send' dead; grep of web/src/lib/api/*.ts shows no POST/PUT/PATCH anywhere (only OTP request in AuthContext.tsx). Marks entry does not exist at all on web (TeacherPages.tsx:106-116 subtitle promises 'marks entry' but rows are not even clickable).
- **Impact:** A cashier cannot collect a fee, a teacher cannot assign homework or enter marks, HR cannot approve leave, admissions cannot enroll — on the platform positioned as the desktop 1:1 parity ERP. Buttons render enabled and do nothing on click, which reads as 'broken' not 'not built'. This dead-ends every primary journey and would fail instantly in a sales demo.
- **Recommendation:** Either implement the write layer for the core workflows (fee collect, marks, homework, leave approve, enrollment hand-off) or remove/visibly gate the dead action buttons with an explicit 'available in the mobile app' affordance. Never render an enabled button with no handler.

### F-116 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-23) — Web pages assert false and fabricated state to the user
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** teacher / legal / settings (web) · **Role:** teacher / school admin
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/teacher/TeacherPages.tsx:100 — caption states 'Submitting posts to the live /teacher/homework endpoint. Class and subject options load from your teaching assignments.' while the Selects have options={[]} (lines 94-95) and the Assign button has no submit logic; web/src/pages/misc/MiscPages.tsx:131-137 — LegalAcceptancePage renders StatusChip 'Accepted' unconditionally for Terms of Service, Privacy Policy, DPA and AUP; web/src/pages/settings/SettingsPage.tsx:42-43 — 'Compact density'/'Reduce motion' toggle local useState that affects nothing; :59-60 — notification switches are no-ops (onChange={() => {}}) with hardcoded checked state.
- **Impact:** The UI makes claims that are untrue: a form says it posts live when it cannot; a compliance page shows legal documents as 'Accepted' that were never accepted; settings appear to save but do nothing. This is worse than a missing feature — it erodes trust in every state indicator in the product and misstates legal-acceptance facts.
- **Recommendation:** Remove the false endpoint caption; drive legal-acceptance chips from real acceptance records or remove the status column; wire or remove decoy settings toggles.

### F-117 · [P1] — Teacher leave request: free-text dates, hidden validation rule, and silent failure
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher (Flutter) · **Role:** teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/leave/teacher_leave_screen.dart:194-212 — 'From date'/'To date' are plain free-text TextFields (no picker, no format hint, contradicting the XCT-3 standard in lib/shared/forms/akshara_date_field.dart:3-8 which exists precisely to replace these); leave_models.dart:76-79 — submit enables only when reason ≥ 8 chars, a rule never shown on the teacher form; teacher_leave_screen.dart:227-241 — when submitTeacherLeave returns false there is NO error feedback whatsoever (only a success branch exists).
- **Impact:** A teacher can type 'tomorrow' or '32/13' as a date and submit; the Submit button sits disabled with no explanation until an invisible 8-character reason threshold is met; and a failed submission gives zero feedback — the teacher believes the request was sent when it was not. This is a daily-use staff workflow.
- **Recommendation:** Swap date fields to AksharaDateField, surface the reason-length helper (the parent form at leave_apply_form.dart:117-121 already does this), and add an error snackbar/state on the ok==false path.

### F-118 · [P1] — Parent leave: decoy calendar icons on free-text date fields and a fake attachment control
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent (Flutter) · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent/leave/widgets/leave_apply_form.dart:56-82 — From/To fields render suffixIcon calendar_today but are free-text; tapping the icon does nothing (dates entered as 'e.g. 12 Jun 2026' prose); :104-115 and :142-203 — 'Add medical certificate (optional)' opens a dialog asking the parent to TYPE a document name ('Dr Rao medical certificate.pdf'); no file is uploaded (documented residual PAR-3).
- **Impact:** Parents are the least technical users in the system. A calendar icon that does not open a calendar is a broken affordance they will tap repeatedly; an 'attachment' that is actually a typed label will make parents believe they submitted a medical certificate when the school received only a string.
- **Recommendation:** Use the shared date picker; relabel the attachment action to 'Note certificate name (upload coming later)' or hide it until real upload lands.

### F-119 · [P1] — QR payment screen defaults to fabricated invoice 'inv_1' and magic amount ₹5000
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** finance (Flutter) · **Role:** cashier / finance admin
- **Evidence:** lib/features/finance/finance_qr_payment_screen.dart:39-44 — initState seeds invoice 'inv_1' and amount '5000' when params absent; router passes raw query params with no guard (lib/router/finance_navigation.dart:82-94), so any deep link / direct nav to /finance/qr-payment gets the fake prefill; lib/features/finance/collections/finance_collections_screen.dart:48 — journeyInvoiceId falls back to 'inv_1' and blank amount when no journey invoice is loaded, so the 'QR Pay' button can open the screen with a nonexistent invoice ID visible in the field.
- **Impact:** A cashier can generate a live UPI QR code against a fabricated invoice or a wrong ₹5,000 default and a parent can pay it. This is a money-path correctness risk driven purely by UI defaults, in a screen whose sibling dialog was already fixed to use real dues (P2-UX-2 §2.4).
- **Recommendation:** Start both fields blank with 'Select an invoice' required state; never render 'inv_1' or a hardcoded amount; disable Generate QR until a real invoice is selected.

### F-120 · [P1] — Create-fee-structure dialog prefills demo money values and hardcodes the category breakdown
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** finance (Flutter) · **Role:** finance admin
- **Evidence:** lib/features/finance/finance_workflow_actions.dart:64-66 — controllers prefilled with 'New fee structure', '₹1,85,000', 'Nursery – 12'; :108-114 — categories are hardcoded to a single Tuition ₹1,45,000 line regardless of what total the admin types; no field validation anywhere in the dialog (76-88).
- **Impact:** One hurried tap on Create yields a live ₹1,85,000 fee structure named 'New fee structure', and any custom total silently disagrees with its own hardcoded Tuition breakdown — a governance-grade error in the module that defines what every parent owes.
- **Recommendation:** Start fields empty with required validation; build the category list from user input and validate that categories sum to the total.

### F-121 · [P1] — Marks 'Save all' silently skips out-of-range rows and never identifies failing students
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** academics / exams (Flutter) · **Role:** teacher / exam coordinator
- **Evidence:** lib/features/academics/exam_admin/exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:279 — `if (parsed < 0 || parsed > exam.maxMarks) continue; // guarded by backend too` silently drops the row from the batch; :306-312 — snackbar reports only '${result.savedCount} saved, ${result.failedCount} failed' with no row identification and no count of skipped rows; rows have no inline errorText (only the individual-save path shows a range snackbar at 629-637).
- **Impact:** A teacher who typed 105/100 for one student and taps Save all sees 'N saved, 0 failed', believes the roster is complete, and that student's mark is never persisted — in the product's top-priority module. The entered/total footer may catch it later, but the save action itself lied by omission.
- **Recommendation:** Count skipped rows in the summary ('1 mark out of range — not saved'), set inline errorText on offending rows, and scroll to the first one.

### F-122 · [P1] — Homework creation targets classes and students by free-typed strings with no draft protection
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher (Flutter) · **Role:** teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/homework/teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:141-168 — class targeting is a free-text field ('e.g. 8-A') + Add button, even though resolvedTeacherTeachingContextProvider (line 51-57) already knows the teacher's real class list; :236-242 — single-student targeting is a free-typed 'Student name'; :310-327 — all failures collapse to generic 'Could not create homework.'; screen has no AksharaUnsavedChangesGuard/DraftAutosaveMixin unlike marks/fee/parent-leave forms.
- **Impact:** A typo ('8A' vs '8-A', misspelled student) silently delivers homework to nobody or the wrong target with no feedback; back-navigation discards a fully-typed assignment; server-side validation detail is thrown away. Daily teacher workflow.
- **Recommendation:** Replace free text with chips/dropdown from the teaching context, add a student picker, wire aksharaErrorMessage(error), and add the unsaved-changes guard.

### F-123 · [P2] — Fee-collection amount accepts empty/zero/garbage client-side; failure reopens as a two-step recovery
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** finance (Flutter) · **Role:** cashier
- **Evidence:** lib/features/finance/finance_workflow_actions.dart:1072-1077 — 'Amount collected' has keyboardType number but no inputFormatter, no required/positive validation, no ₹ affordance; :804-813 — 'Record payment' confirms and closes the dialog before any validation, so a server rejection lands as a snackbar after the form is gone (draft Resume prompt at 1098-1140 mitigates, but the cashier must reopen the dialog and tap Resume).
- **Impact:** On web/desktop keyboards letters are typable; an empty or 0 amount travels to the server; each validation failure costs the cashier a dialog round-trip at a counter with a queue.
- **Recommendation:** Validate amount (non-empty, numeric, > 0, warn on overpayment vs outstanding) before allowing Record payment to close the dialog.

### F-124 · [P2] — Out-of-scope verticals (Salon, Restaurant, Healthcare, White Label) remain fully routed and in admin navigation
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** platform / verticals (Flutter) · **Role:** super admin / sales demo
- **Evidence:** lib/features/admin/admin_navigation_provider.dart:173-212 — Healthcare/Salon/Restaurant/Accommodation/White Label are AdminNavDestinations; lib/router/app_router.dart:1060-1090 + route_names.dart:176-196 — full route trees registered; lib/core/security/role_permissions.dart:166-173 — superAdmin holds view/manage grants; lib/router/surface_backend_gate.dart:23-40 hides them ONLY when enableApiMode is on, so any demo/mock build shows 'Salon' and 'Restaurant' in a school ERP's nav.
- **Impact:** Owner decision declared these verticals permanently out of scope. They are invisible on the live pilot, but any offline demo build — the exact build a salesperson or evaluator might run — surfaces a Salon dashboard inside a school product.
- **Recommendation:** Remove the destinations, routes and permissions outright rather than gating them behind API flags.

### F-125 · [P2] — Web error/unavailable states leak API paths and engineering jargon to end users
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** system components (web) · **Role:** all web users
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/admissions/EnrollmentPage.tsx:42-43 — 'Approved applicants awaiting enrolment load here from /admissions/enrollments/pending once the backend is connected.'; CollectionsPage.tsx:51-52, TeacherPages.tsx passim ('once connected'); rendered by AsyncBoundary via ResourceList.tsx:75 whenever the query fails — including transient failures on the live deployment.
- **Impact:** A school admin whose network blips reads raw REST paths and 'backend is connected' developer language, which reads as an unfinished product.
- **Recommendation:** Replace unavailable copy with user language ('Couldn't load collections. Retry.') and keep endpoint detail in console/logs.

### F-126 · [P2] — Enrollment hard-requires a 12-digit Aadhaar with no alternative path
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admissions (Flutter) · **Role:** admissions clerk
- **Evidence:** lib/features/admissions/enrollment/enrollment_validation.dart:34-37 — `if (aadhaar.length != 12) errors['aadhaar'] = 'Aadhaar must be 12 digits'` blocks step 1 unconditionally; no 'Aadhaar not available' option in the student step.
- **Impact:** Indian schools cannot lawfully deny admission for lack of Aadhaar (Supreme Court, 2018); a clerk enrolling a child without one is hard-blocked at step 1 of the wizard and will invent a fake number to proceed — corrupting identity data.
- **Recommendation:** Make Aadhaar optional-but-validated-when-present, or add an explicit 'not provided' checkbox with a follow-up task.

### F-127 · [P2] — Residual 'coming soon' surfaces on student profile and admin profile menu
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** student_app / admin (Flutter) · **Role:** student / school admin
- **Evidence:** lib/features/student_app/profile/student_profile_screen.dart:163 ('App settings, coming soon') and :181 ('Notifications, language, and privacy — coming soon'); lib/features/admin/admin_content_scaffold.dart:109 — profile tap can fire a 'Profile menu coming soon.' snackbar; web/src/pages/misc/MiscPages.tsx:12-22 + 34-39,59-60,92-96,121 — InfoPage brochure screens (Setup wizard, Copilot, Backup & restore, Workflow automation, Layout editor) reachable from web nav with permanently disabled action buttons.
- **Impact:** Students see settings rows that go nowhere; web admins reach whole pages that are marketing copy with a disabled 'Start wizard'/'Backup now' button — dead-ends dressed as features.
- **Recommendation:** Hide unbuilt rows/pages from navigation (hide-first philosophy) instead of shipping them as reachable teasers.

### F-128 · [P2] — Teacher screens route the notifications bell to the parent persona's route
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher (Flutter) · **Role:** teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/homework/teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:126 — onNotificationsTap: () => context.push(RouteNames.parentNotifications) ('/parent/notifications' per route_names.dart:36); same pattern at app_router.dart:2523.
- **Impact:** A teacher tapping the bell lands on a parent-namespaced notifications surface; breadcrumbs/back behavior and any parent-specific framing leak across personas, undermining the USER→ROLE→WORKSPACE model.
- **Recommendation:** Introduce a role-neutral notifications route or a teacher-scoped one.
