## Reviewer: AI Product Experience Reviewer  (`ai-experience-reviewer`)
**Vote:** NOT CERTIFIED · **Scores:** overall 4, aiExperience 3, maturity 4

**Independent executive assessment:** Akshara's AI layer is two products wearing one badge. The governed lane is genuinely impressive: the Adaptive Priority Feed shows a "Why" line and an expandable factor breakdown on every recommendation, offers Dismiss and "Mute this type", honestly labels permission-degraded feeds as partial, and never auto-executes; Parent Insights generates structured, multilingual, PDF-exportable summaries from a real backend with an AI disclaimer; the AI Usage & Cost panel gives schools real spend-cap transparency. This lane proves the team knows what trustworthy school AI should look like.

The second lane undermines it. The client ships a simulated "EdgeAiProvider" that fabricates responses locally (fixed 180ms delay, canned templates) while telling the user "Live inference (akshara-edge-v1)... Based on current school data" — and this fake path is the ONLY AI experience for teachers, parents and students (the mobile-first core personas, who lack full-copilot permission), and it feeds real record-writes: fabricated parent-meeting summaries are saved onto meeting records, and a production-reachable developer harness at /intelligence lets staff hand-type attendance/marks for a prefilled "student_1" and publish AI "guidance" to the real parent hub with the publish toggle defaulted ON. Add a Teacher Assistant button that writes a hardcoded intervention for "student_1" to the live backend, a quick-action prompt pipeline that silently discards the user's chosen prompt, stub replies visually identical to real AI (the only signal is the literal string "read-only stub" leaking to end users), and a web "Copilot" nav entry that is a brochure page with a disabled button. Until the fake-inference lane is removed or honestly labeled and the demo harnesses are unshipped, the AI experience would embarrass the product in a sales demo and can corrupt real school records — the strong governed lane cannot be certified around it.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Adaptive Priority Feed is near-world-class explainable-AI UX: per-item 'Why:' reason, expandable factor breakdown (urgency/impact/recency/learned), Dismiss + 'Mute this type' feedback that trains the feed, honest 'Showing partial results' degraded flag, and pre-staged actions that navigate but never execute (lib/features/adaptive_ai/widgets/adaptive_priority_feed.dart:64-231)
- Parent Insights Center: real backend generation with progress feedback, structured strengths/weaknesses/attendance sections, Hindi/Telugu language preference, genuine PDF export carrying an 'AI-assisted summary' disclaimer, and deliberately no free chat for parents (lib/features/evolution/parent_insights_screen.dart, parent_insights_pdf_service.dart:60-64)
- AI Usage & Cost panel gives schools real spend-cap transparency (At cap / Near cap / On track) and deliberately propagates errors rather than fail-softing to a misleading healthy $0 state (lib/features/intelligence/management/ai_economics_screen.dart:15-75, hybrid_copilot_repository.dart:57-64)
- Governance is visible in the UX: capability filter distinguishes plan-locked ('upgrade — no payment is taken in the app') from school-disabled topics (copilot_capability_filter.dart:35-44); every AI screen re-checks RBAC defense-in-depth; quick actions are deterministic-first with human-confirmed send
- AI entry-point control: users choose where AI lives (floating bubble / bottom-nav center / sidebar / app bar / auto) with a clean settings screen (ai_assistant_settings_screen.dart, ai_access_mode.dart)
- Full copilot send failure UX restores the user's typed message and shows the mapped error instead of losing it (copilot_screen.dart:44-64)

**Findings:**

### F-129 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-24) — Simulated 'EdgeAiProvider' fabricates AI responses labeled as live inference on live surfaces
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** copilot / core-ai · **Role:** teacher, parent, student, staff
- **Evidence:** lib/core/ai/providers/edge_ai_provider.dart:4-6 ('Simulated live inference provider'), :60-67 (returns '**Live inference (akshara-edge-v1)**... Based on current school data... _Generated by Akshara live inference pipeline._'), :89-103 (fabricated stats: 'Rebalance 2 sections with >110% teacher workload', 'reduce 18% idle capacity', 'Priority score: 82'). Wired as PRIMARY provider by default: lib/core/ai/ai_inference_providers.dart:28-42 (LIVE_AI_INFERENCE_ENABLED defaultValue true, no config/live_release.json override). Consumers not behind any API flag: copilot_persona_shell_screen.dart:42, copilot/content/ai_content_providers.dart:10, parent_meetings_mutations_provider.dart:106, resource_optimization_providers.dart:12.
- **Impact:** Teachers, parents and students (who lack full-copilot permission per role_permissions.dart:602-662) receive hardcoded template text presented as data-grounded live AI. Fabricated operational numbers look authoritative. This is deceptive labeling that destroys trust the moment any user notices the same reply for every question, and it violates the project's own deterministic-first firewall in spirit: fake AI masquerading as governed AI.
- **Recommendation:** Remove EdgeAiProvider from production wiring. Route persona-shell/content/meeting-summary inference through the governed backend gateway (which already has a correct degrade-to-deterministic-stub path), or honestly disable those features until wired. Never render 'Live inference' copy from a synthesizer.

### F-130 · [P0] · **verified: REFUTED** (see Part 1 V-25) — Fabricated AI meeting summaries are saved to real parent-meeting records
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent_meetings · **Role:** teacher/staff
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent_meetings/parent_meeting_detail_screen.dart:90-95 ('Generate AI Summary' button) -> parent_meetings_mutations_provider.dart:99-126 (pipeline.complete then repository.saveSummary persists response.content) -> edge_ai_provider.dart:70-81 (canned template inserts claims never discussed: 'Attendance: Reviewed against class benchmark', action items 'Complete pending homework daily', 'Schedule a follow-up in two weeks').
- **Impact:** A canned, partly fabricated narrative becomes the stored record of a real parent conference — the exact 'AI output mistaken for authoritative school records' safety failure. Staff will trust and forward it; parents may be told their child was discussed in ways that never happened.
- **Recommendation:** Block save until real summarization exists; generate strictly from the typed notes via the governed backend, show a draft-review step (editable, explicitly marked AI-drafted) before persisting.

### F-131 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-26) — Production-reachable developer harness can publish hand-typed AI 'guidance' to the real parent hub
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** intelligence · **Role:** staff (viewStudentRisk)
- **Evidence:** lib/features/intelligence/intelligence_screen.dart:25-44 (controllers prefilled 'Arjun Reddy', 'Grade 8', 'student_1', attendance '62', marks '54'), :245-293 (staff types raw Student ID + attendance/marks/homework, 'Publish to parent hub' toggle defaults TRUE, snackbar 'Guidance published — visible in parent hub'). Route live: app_router.dart:617 ('/intelligence'), guard route_guards.dart:44 (only Permission.viewStudentRisk), linked from Platform Operations Hub as 'Intelligence Hub' (platform_operations_hub_screen.dart:157-161).
- **Impact:** Any staffer with student-risk permission can fabricate inputs for an arbitrary student ID and push AI-generated 'guidance' to a real parent, one tap from a production hub link. Also instantly reads as demo-ware in a sales demo (prefilled fake student name).
- **Recommendation:** Unship this screen from production routing (or gate behind an internal/dev flag). Real risk workflows already exist in student_success/. If a manual-guidance tool is wanted, it must pick real students from the roster, source metrics from the system, and default publish OFF behind a review step.

### F-132 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-27) — Teacher Assistant 'Add intervention' writes a hardcoded student_1 record to the live backend
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** evolution / teacher-assistant · **Role:** teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/evolution/teacher_assistant_screen.dart:38-51 — FloatingActionButton.extended calls createIntervention(studentId: 'student_1', interventionType: 'academic', title: 'Schedule doubt-clearing session', priority: 'high') with no dialog or student picker. EVOLUTION_API_ENABLED=true in config/live_release.json:45; backend router exists (supabase/functions/_shared/teacher_assistant/teacher_assistant_router.ts). Class filter also hardcodes only 'Grade 8'/'Grade 7' (lines 30-34).
- **Impact:** The primary action on the teacher's AI surface creates a bogus intervention for a nonexistent student every time it is pressed — garbage in the live intervention tracker and a broken core flow (a teacher cannot actually log an intervention for a real student from here).
- **Recommendation:** Replace with a proper intervention sheet: student picker from the risk list (tapping a flagged student should prefill it), editable type/title/priority, confirm. Populate the class filter from the teacher's actual classes.

### F-133 · [P1] — AI quick-action prompts are silently discarded — copilot opens empty
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** copilot · **Role:** all AI users
- **Evidence:** lib/features/copilot/widgets/copilot_ai_quick_actions.dart:132 stages the chosen prompt into copilotMessageDraftProvider, but a repo-wide grep shows that provider is written here and defined in copilot_provider.dart:19 — never read by any screen; CopilotScreen uses a fresh local TextEditingController (copilot_screen.dart:29). Contradicts the in-file contract 'selecting an action opens the GOVERNED copilot with a pre-filled prompt' (copilot_ai_quick_actions.dart:15-19). No test references the provider.
- **Impact:** The long-press quick-action menu — the fastest advertised AI path — loses the user's intent every time: they pick 'Show at-risk students', land in a copilot with an empty input, and must remember and retype what they asked for.
- **Recommendation:** Have CopilotScreen (and the persona shell) consume and clear copilotMessageDraftProvider on open, prefilling the input; add a widget test asserting the staged prompt appears.

### F-134 · [P1] — Core mobile personas get a single-shot, no-typing AI shell with a meaningless dev toggle
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** copilot / persona shell · **Role:** teacher, parent, student
- **Evidence:** lib/features/copilot/persona/copilot_persona_shell_screen.dart — no TextField anywhere; only canned starter-prompt ActionChips (:216-228); each answer overwrites the previous (_lastReply single field, :36,80-82); no conversation history; 'Streaming reply' SwitchListTile with subtitle 'Show loading, then full streamed response' (:200-214) while _ask buffers the entire stream before a single setState (:68-77), so the toggle changes nothing perceptible. Teachers/parents/students lack viewAiCopilot (role_permissions.dart:602-662) so this IS their whole AI experience.
- **Impact:** The mobile-first product's three biggest audiences cannot ask their own question, cannot see past answers, and are shown a developer plumbing toggle. Combined with the fake provider behind it, the flagship 'AI for every role' claim is hollow on mobile.
- **Recommendation:** Give the persona shell a real governed chat (input + history) against the backend copilot with persona-appropriate scope, or reduce it honestly to deterministic insight cards. Delete the streaming toggle.

### F-135 · [P1] — Degraded/stub AI replies are indistinguishable from real AI; 'read-only stub' jargon is the only signal
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** copilot · **Role:** staff
- **Evidence:** Chat bubble renders content only — no model, no timestamp, no origin badge (copilot_screen.dart:352-379); CopilotSendMessageResult carries stub/model metadata that is never surfaced (copilot_models.dart:105). On any no-key/rate-limit/cap/timeout the backend degrades to a deterministic stub whose visible header is '**FINANCE Assistant (read-only stub)**' (supabase/functions/_shared/copilot/copilot_llm_client.ts:146-148,234-246; copilot_prompt_orchestrator.ts:95); client fallback prints '(context-aware stub)' (copilot_stub_responses.dart:32). No 'AI can make mistakes'-style guidance exists anywhere in copilot/evolution (grep negative).
- **Impact:** Users cannot tell a governed deterministic fallback from genuine model output — the 5-gate deterministic-first firewall the project mandates is invisible in the UX, and the only tell is internal engineering vocabulary leaking into customer-facing chat.
- **Recommendation:** Render origin chips on assistant messages ('From your school data' vs 'AI-generated') from the existing stub/model metadata; rewrite stub headers into user language ('Live AI is unavailable — here is a data summary'); add a standing accuracy disclaimer in the chat surface.

### F-136 · [P1] — Full copilot phone layout: permanent 220px sidebar + fixed 640px height squeezes chat to ~170px
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** copilot · **Role:** principal/director/admin on phone
- **Evidence:** copilot_screen.dart:117-124 — SizedBox(height: isMobile ? 640 : 720) wrapping a Row with SizedBox(width: isMobile ? 220 : 280) sidebar always visible, inside scrollable AdminContentScaffold (nested scrolling). On a 390px phone the conversation column is under half the screen; message input and bubbles render in ~170px.
- **Impact:** For a mobile-first ERP, the flagship staff AI chat is effectively unusable on phones: cramped bubbles, wrapping suggestion chips, double scroll. This is the screen a principal would demo.
- **Recommendation:** On mobile, collapse the assistant/history sidebar into a drawer or top sheet and let the chat own the full width and height (no fixed SizedBox — use the scaffold body).

### F-137 · [P1] — Prediction and finance-copilot rows are dead ends with unexplained scores
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** predictions / finance intelligence · **Role:** leadership, finance
- **Evidence:** predictions_screen.dart:135-147 (fee-default rows), :227-237 (student-risk rows) — ListTiles with no onTap, no route to the student/fee account, no follow-up action; risk chip shows bare 'Critical 92'. finance_copilot_screen.dart:34 ('Confidence 78%' with no basis shown), :53-59 (defaulter predictions likewise inert). Contrast: the adaptive feed proves the team already has the tap-through + explainability pattern.
- **Impact:** Leadership sees 'who is at risk' but cannot act from the screen — every insight requires manually navigating to another module and re-finding the student, which kills the productivity value the surface promises.
- **Recommendation:** Make every predicted row tap through to its student/fee detail and offer one pre-staged action (record follow-up, open defaulters queue); add a one-line 'based on: overdue days, history' explanation like the feed's factor breakdown.

### F-138 · [P1] — Principal Command natural-language query: no loading state, silent failure, no provenance
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** evolution / principal-command · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** principal_command_screen.dart:66-84 — 'Run query' FilledButton awaits queryPrincipalCommand with no spinner, no disabled state, and no try/catch (a network error is an uncaught async exception; the user sees nothing happen); result renders only _queryResult['summary'] text with no links to the students/fees it references. Also :112-113 dumps an internal 'Widgets' map as raw key/value ListTiles, and :85-97 shows 'Priority engine score 82/100' with no explanation.
- **Impact:** The most executive-facing AI feature fails silently on error and answers with an unverifiable paragraph — precisely where trust and drill-down matter most.
- **Recommendation:** Add loading/disabled state and error snackbar; return structured results with entity links; remove the raw 'Widgets' section; add a 'what drives this score' popover.

### F-139 · [P1] — Web parity gap: sidebar 'Copilot' is a static brochure page with a disabled button
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** copilot (web) · **Role:** admin/desktop staff
- **Evidence:** web/src/routes/navConfig.ts:33 (top-level 'Copilot' nav item), router.tsx:470-471; web/src/pages/misc/MiscPages.tsx:12-15 (InfoPage renders Button disabled), :37-40 (CopilotPage/CopilotPersonaShellPage/AiAssistantSettingsPage are all InfoPage placeholders describing what the feature 'connects to').
- **Impact:** The only roles entitled to the full governed copilot are admin staff — the very users on the desktop web app — and for them the flagship AI differentiator is a dead nav item with a disabled 'New chat' button. Live backend endpoints exist (/copilot/*) but the web never calls them.
- **Recommendation:** Either build the web copilot against the existing /copilot API (parity mandate) or remove the nav entry until it exists; a permanent marketing placeholder in primary navigation is worse than absence.

### F-140 · [P1] — Parent insight summaries and PDFs carry no generation date
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** evolution / parent-insights · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** evolution_models.dart:137-165 — ParentInsightSnapshot has id/period/language/content fields but no generatedAt; cards show only period+language chips (parent_insights_screen.dart:345-371); the exported PDF prints Student/Class/Period/Language but no date (parent_insights_pdf_service.dart:39-42).
- **Impact:** A parent who generates weekly summaries accumulates identical-looking 'Weekly · English' cards with no way to tell this week's from last month's; a printed, undated PDF about a child's progress is not a credible school document.
- **Recommendation:** Add generatedAt to the snapshot contract, show relative date on each card, and print the date on the PDF header.

### F-141 · [P2] — AI cannot be turned off — settings only relocate it; plumbing copy leaks
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** copilot / settings · **Role:** all
- **Evidence:** ai_access_mode.dart:4-9 — all five modes are placements (floating/bottom-nav/sidebar/app-bar/auto), none is 'off'; ai_assistant_settings_screen.dart:50-61 only toggles the extra floating bubble. Settings note reads 'Cross-device sync will apply when the user-preferences API is connected' (:69-74).
- **Impact:** Users who distrust or are distracted by AI (a real segment among Indian school staff and parents) cannot opt out; unfinished-integration language in customer settings undermines polish.
- **Recommendation:** Add an 'Off / minimal' access mode (hide dock, nav slot and quick actions; keep the settings entry); replace the sync note with plain language or drop it.

### F-142 · [P2] — AI surface naming/IA is incoherent: three 'intelligence' hubs, two meanings of 'Copilot'
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** intelligence / finance / copilot · **Role:** staff
- **Evidence:** 'Finance Copilot' is a static dashboard with no conversation (finance_models.dart:37, finance_copilot_screen.dart) while 'AI Copilot' is chat (copilot_screen.dart:20); sibling surfaces: '/intelligence' titled 'Akshara Analytics' (intelligence_screen.dart:84), 'Analytics Hub' (intelligence_hub_screen.dart:31,70), 'Organization Trust Analytics' (trust_intelligence_hub_screen.dart:56), plus 'Intelligence Hub' as the cross-link label for /intelligence (platform_operations_hub_screen.dart:160). Empty state instructs 'Run intelligence compute on student and finance modules' (intelligence_hub_screen.dart:360-364).
- **Impact:** Users cannot form a stable mental model of where 'the AI' lives or what 'Copilot' means; internal verbs ('intelligence compute') appear as user instructions.
- **Recommendation:** One naming system: 'Copilot' = conversational only; analytics surfaces get distinct descriptive names; rewrite empty states in user language ('Insights appear after your first attendance and fee data sync').

### F-143 · [P2] — Out-of-scope verticals (salon/restaurant/healthcare/white-label) baked into copilot intelligence
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** copilot · **Role:** n/a (scope hygiene)
- **Evidence:** copilot_role_intelligence.dart:101-129 — copilotIndustryIntelligenceFocus returns 'stylist utilization', 'table turnover', 'order accuracy', 'patient flow'; :204-243 — copilotModuleForRoute/copilotScreenLabelForRoute still map '/salon', '/restaurant', '/healthcare', white-label to 'Salon Dashboard', 'Restaurant Dashboard' context labels shown by the AI dock.
- **Impact:** Owner-decided out-of-scope verticals survive inside the school AI assistant's context engine; if any vertical route is reached, the school ERP's AI dock will announce a 'Salon Dashboard' — instant credibility damage.
- **Recommendation:** Delete vertical branches from the copilot context/label maps alongside the pending vertical-navigation cleanup.

### F-144 · [P2] — Analytics Hub KPI cards waste half the phone width
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** intelligence hub · **Role:** management
- **Evidence:** Golden test/golden/goldens/intelligence_dashboard_390x844.png — fixed-width KPI cards (~165px) stack in a single left-aligned column leaving ~45% of the 390px width empty; source: Wrap of fixed-width _ExecutiveKpi cards (intelligence_hub_screen.dart:131-147).
- **Impact:** The first screen of the management analytics surface looks half-rendered on phones.
- **Recommendation:** Use a responsive 2-column grid at mobile widths (cards flex to fill).

## Reviewer: School Principal (55, phone-primary) — Product Experience Certification  (`journey-principal`)
**Vote:** NOT CERTIFIED · **Scores:** overall 4, workflowEfficiency 4, mobile 5, maturity 4, web 2, visualDesign 7

**Independent executive assessment:** I simulated my real week in the Flutter app from staff OTP login through approvals, exam publishing, circulars, fee status, and the angry-parent Student-360 lookup, then repeated the office-desktop journeys on the web ERP. The craft in the transactional core is genuinely strong: the Principal Approval Center is the best unified maker-checker inbox I have seen in an India school ERP (swipe-to-decide, server-driven "You raised this" SoD badge, required reject reason, batch actions), the exam submit→approve→publish chain is coherent, universal student/staff search is one tap from anywhere, and dark mode and design tokens are consistently applied. But three things a principal does constantly are broken or dishonest. (1) Sending a circular to all parents — the single most common principal communication task — is unreachable on the phone: the only compose screen (BroadcastAdminScreen) lives inside a "School Completion" hub that has zero inbound navigation links, and there is no Communication module in the 25-destination admin nav. (2) The first screen I land on every morning greets me with hardcoded demo numbers ("1,248 Students · 96% Attendance · ₹4.2L Collected today") presented as live stats, and the dashboards below it render filter chips ("This month / All classes / All modes") that are wired to nothing on at least 8 modules. (3) The web ERP, which I would use at my office desk, is a read-only shell: 80 of 103 action buttons in its pages have no onClick handler, its Approval Center has no approve/reject controls, and the only POST in the entire web codebase is login — I can watch my school but not run it.

The mobile skeleton — USER→ROLE→WORKSPACE→TASK, hide-first gating of the salon/restaurant/healthcare/franchise/white-label verticals (verified blocked in nav AND deep links), RBAC-layered route guards — is sound, and what needs fixing is mostly wiring and honesty rather than redesign. But as it stands the product would fail a live sales demo the moment a principal asks "send a notice to all parents" on the phone, or clicks any Add/Approve button on the desktop. That is a certification-blocking state.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Principal Approval Center (Flutter) is world-class for this segment: unified cross-module inbox (14 types incl. fee concession, staff leave, exam results), swipe-to-decide gated on real approve permission, server-flagged maker-checker 'You raised this' badge with disabled approve, mandatory reject reason, batch bar, stale >48h banner, bottom-sheet detail with audit trail (lib/features/management/approval/)
- Exam publish governance is coherent end-to-end: marks entry → 'Submit for principal approval' → approval center examResults → approve auto-invalidates student/parent/teacher exam views; direct publish requires a distinct publishExamResults permission (exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:467-495, approval_center_provider.dart:571-575)
- Universal search from the shell app bar covers students/staff/leads (RBAC- and capability-filtered) and deep-links straight to the student record — the angry-parent lookup is ~3 taps + typing (global_search_overlay.dart, adaptive_ai_providers.dart:77-88)
- Out-of-scope verticals (salon/restaurant/healthcare/accommodation/white-label/franchise) are properly invisible in the school build: hidden from nav AND deep-link blocked by ErpRouteGuard via SchoolBuildScope + backend-less surface gate (school_build_scope.dart, route_guards.dart:409-421)
- Dark mode is real parity, not an afterthought — verified in dark goldens (dark_qw3_finance_dashboard_390x844.png shows correctly toned banners, chips, charts)
- Login/OTP flows are clean: staff email/mobile toggle, remember-session, 30s resend countdown; phone-first parent OTP matches the identity architecture
- Mobile-first is genuinely engineered, not claimed: card layouts replace tables on phone, bottom nav shows the active workspace's top 4 modules + More drawer + floating AI slot (admin_bottom_nav.dart), Semantics labels present on interactive rows

**Findings:**

### F-145 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-28) — Circular/broadcast to parents is unreachable from any navigation on mobile — core principal journey is dead
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** communication · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** The only compose UI is BroadcastAdminScreen (lib/features/communication/broadcast_admin_screen.dart). Its only in-app entry points are: school_completion_hub_screen.dart:94 (the hub itself has ZERO inbound links — grep for RouteNames.schoolCompletionHub across lib/ hits only router registration files app_router.dart:785-788, route_guards.dart:70), communication_delivery_screen.dart:31 (same orphaned hub), and parent_meeting_detail_screen.dart:98 (parentMeetings route is disabled in school_build_scope.dart:76-77). No 'Communication' destination exists among the 25 admin nav entries (admin_navigation_provider.dart labels list) and global_search_registry.dart has no broadcast entry. On a phone there is no URL bar, so the screen is literally unreachable.
- **Impact:** A principal cannot send a notice/circular to all parents — arguably the most frequent school-to-home task. The feature is fully built (audiences, templates, ack tracking, scheduling, history, delivery analytics) and completely wasted.
- **Recommendation:** Add a top-level Communication nav destination (parity with web navConfig.ts:70) and a 'Send circular' quick action on the principal's Management overview panel.

### F-146 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-29) — Admin Hub landing hero shows hardcoded fake stats as live data in production
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admin · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** workspace_landing_config.dart:9-12 admits stats are 'curated demo figures consistent with the seeded school so demos read as live'; lines 31-43 hardcode '1,248 Students', '96% Attendance', '₹4.2L Collected today', '₹1.8L Pending', '92% This month'. AdminHubScreen renders them unconditionally (admin_hub_screen.dart:58-63) with no API-mode gate and no 'demo' label.
- **Impact:** The first screen a principal sees every morning presents fabricated attendance and fee-collection figures as truth. The numbers never change, which destroys trust in every real number elsewhere in the app; in a sales demo a returning prospect will notice the frozen ₹4.2L.
- **Recommendation:** Replace with real summary providers or drop the stat row until endpoints exist (the hero widget already accepts any stat list).

### F-147 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-30) — Web ERP is a read-only shell: 80 of 103 page action buttons have no handler; zero business mutations exist
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** web-platform · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** grep across web/src/pages: 80/103 <Button> instances lack onClick — including 'New broadcast' (engage/EngagePages.tsx:42), 'Add student' (sis/StudentRegistryPage.tsx:71), 'Create exam' (academics/AcademicsPages.tsx:39), 'Approvals' (management/ManagementPages.tsx:49), 'Mark all read' (EngagePages.tsx:52). The web Approval Center renders a table with status chips but NO approve/reject actions (ManagementPages.tsx:106-124). The only POST in the entire web app is login (web/src/lib/auth/AuthContext.tsx). SchoolConfigPage renders 8 'interactive' cards with no onClick (EngagePages.tsx:110-134).
- **Impact:** At the office desk the principal can look but not act: cannot approve a leave or concession, cannot send a circular, cannot add anything. Every primary CTA is a dead control that looks enabled — worse than absent, because it teaches users the product is broken.
- **Recommendation:** Either wire the top-10 principal actions (approve/reject, broadcast compose) or visibly disable dead CTAs with a 'coming soon' state until the write layer ships.

### F-148 · [P1] — No school-wide 'today's attendance' view; principal's Attendance quick action lands on year-scoped analytics
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** management/attendance · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** Principal overview 'Attendance' quick action routes to managementAnalytics (management_principal_overview_panel.dart:91-95), whose only period filter is 'This year' (management_analytics_screen.dart:24-28). The daily register (office_attendance_screen.dart) is one-class-at-a-time with a hardcoded default class '8-A' (line 19). The web app has an /attendance overview surface (navConfig.ts:56, AttendancePages.tsx:16-35) — mobile has none. HR staff attendance offers filters All/Present/Late/'On leave' but no 'Absent' despite HrAttendanceStatus.absent existing (hr_attendance_screen.dart:29-34, hr_models.dart:41, hr_providers.dart:139-150).
- **Impact:** The principal's first question every morning — which classes are missing kids, who is absent, which staff didn't show — requires iterating a class dropdown N times for students and manually scanning an unfiltered list for staff.
- **Recommendation:** Add a 'Today' school-wide attendance rollup (per-class absent counts + unmarked classes + absent staff) as the principal's attendance landing; add the missing Absent filter to HR attendance.

### F-149 · [P1] — Dashboard filter chips are placebo controls on 8+ modules — selection changes nothing
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** finance/sis/transport/hostel/library/inventory/alumni/admissions/management · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** financeDashboardFilterProvider is watched only for chip highlight (finance_dashboard_screen.dart:34-42); financeDashboardFutureProvider queries with plain repositoryQueryProvider (finance_dashboard_provider.dart:15-17). Identical dead pattern verified for sisDashboardFilterProvider, transportDashboardFilterProvider, hostelDashboardFilterProvider, libraryDashboardFilterProvider, inventoryDashboardFilterProvider, alumniDashboardFilterProvider, admissionsDashboardFilterProvider (each has exactly 2 references, both in the screen's chip row) and managementAnalyticsFilterProvider (management_providers.dart:60-65 ignores it). The chips are also semantically wrong: 'This month / All classes / All modes' render as mutually-exclusive single-select options of unrelated dimensions.
- **Impact:** A principal taps 'All classes' on the Finance dashboard expecting scoped numbers and silently gets the same data — misleading and, combined with the fake hub stats, corrosive to trust in every figure.
- **Recommendation:** Wire the chips to query params (the management dashboard already shows the correct pattern, management_providers.dart:16-25) or remove them.

### F-150 · [P1] — Mass broadcast sends with no confirmation, no empty-content validation, and free-text class targeting
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** communication · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** _sendBroadcast (broadcast_admin_screen.dart:219-269) validates only class-required and schedule-time; there is no confirm dialog before dispatch to 'All parents'/'Everyone (school-wide)', no check that title/body are non-empty, and the recipient count is only revealed AFTER sending ('Broadcast sent to N recipients', line 264). Class and section are raw TextFields (lines 532-545), not pickers.
- **Impact:** One accidental tap sends an instant, possibly empty, school-wide push to every parent — a reputational incident with no undo. A typo'd free-text class silently mistargets. Approving a ₹500 expense gets a confirm dialog (approval_queue_table.dart:187-209) but messaging 1,200 families does not.
- **Recommendation:** Add a confirm sheet showing resolved audience size before dispatch, require non-empty title/body, and replace class/section text fields with pickers.

### F-151 · [P1] — Approval detail dead-ends on payload text — no link to the record being approved; detail-panel Approve ignores maker-checker disable
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** management/approval · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** ApprovalDetailPanel shows 'Entity: <type> · <id>' as plain text with generic key-value payload lines and no navigation to the underlying exam results, student ledger, or PO (approval_detail_panel.dart:51-84). Inconsistency: the queue-row Approve button disables when sodBlocked (approval_queue_table.dart:479) but the detail-panel Approve does not (approval_detail_panel.dart:115-120) — the maker taps an enabled button and gets a server 403.
- **Impact:** Approving 'Exam results · Class 10' or a ₹50,000 fee concession blind, or leaving the approval center to manually hunt the record (4+ taps, losing queue position). The inconsistent SoD button teaches makers that errors are random.
- **Recommendation:** Add an 'Open record' deep link per approval type and mirror the sodBlocked disable in the detail panel.

### F-152 · [P1] — Production login screens leak internal/demo jargon to real users
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** auth · **Role:** parent/principal
- **Evidence:** login_screen.dart:159 shows non-demo (production) users 'OTP is verified by the staging server.'; line 78 shows the non-demo OTP failure message 'Unable to send OTP. Use a registered demo-school phone number.'
- **Impact:** The very first product touchpoint tells a paying school it is on a 'staging server' and, on any OTP failure (wrong number, network), instructs a real parent to use a 'demo-school phone number'. Embarrassing in a sales demo and confusing in production.
- **Recommendation:** Production copy: 'Enter your registered mobile number' / 'Could not send OTP. Check the number or try again.'

### F-153 · [P1] — Two competing student detail destinations; Student 360 not directly reachable from the registry on phone
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** sis/student_360 · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** Desktop registry rows offer an Actions popup with both 'View profile' and 'Student 360' and no explanation of the difference (sis_registry_screen.dart:368-390). The mobile card's tap goes only to sisStudentDetail (line 285-287) with no 360 action; reaching the 360 requires entering the profile and finding the 'Student 360 dossier' card's button mid-page (sis_profile_screen.dart:303-315). The 360 itself is the right artifact — 9 tabs incl. Fees/Attendance/Behaviour (student_360_screen.dart:107-115).
- **Impact:** In the angry-parent scenario the principal burns an extra hop and a scroll on the phone, and on desktop must choose between two near-synonymous destinations under time pressure.
- **Recommendation:** Make Student 360 the registry row's primary tap target (or a swipe action) and fold the SIS profile into a 360 tab.

### F-154 · [P2] — School health score is a fabricated composite with hardcoded weights and silent fallback values
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** management · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** management_principal_overview_panel.dart:24-38: score = feeRate*0.55 + margin*0.45, with parse-failure defaults of 68 and 31 — a plausible-looking 52/100 renders even with no data. Subtitle only says 'Blends fee collection and margin trends'.
- **Impact:** A prominent gauge that answers a question nobody asked ('school health'?) while the number the principal actually wants (today's attendance) has no home; fallback values fabricate a score when data is absent.
- **Recommendation:** Drop the invented metric or replace with the pending-approvals + attendance + fees trio, and never render from fallback constants.

### F-155 · [P2] — Tablet Admin Hub wastes the canvas: fabricated hero + a single 220px module card on an empty page
- **Platform:** tablet · **Module:** admin · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** Golden test/golden/goldens/workspace_switcher_multihat_834x1194.png: workspace chips, purple hero with the hardcoded stats, then ONE module card; ~65% of the 834x1194 canvas is blank. _ModuleCard is fixed width:220 (admin_hub_screen.dart:100-101) regardless of breakpoint.
- **Impact:** First impression on iPad/tablets in a demo is an empty product; single-module workspaces make the workspace concept feel like over-engineering.
- **Recommendation:** On tablet, land single-module workspaces directly in the module; scale card grid to the canvas.

### F-156 · [P2] — Raw exception text surfaces in principal-facing snackbars
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** management/approval · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** approval_center_actions.dart:29 and :65 interpolate the raw error ('Unable to approve: $error') — while the exam flows use the aksharaErrorMessage() mapper (exam_lifecycle_actions.dart:79,96).
- **Impact:** A failed approval can show 'Unable to approve: Exception: 403 …' to a 55-year-old principal; inconsistent with the app's own error-mapping standard.
- **Recommendation:** Route all approval errors through aksharaErrorMessage().

### F-157 · [P2] — Principal Command Center (evolution) is prototype-grade and correctly hidden — but the answer to 'is it useful?' is: it would be noise
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** evolution · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** principal_command_screen.dart:76-113 dumps raw maps to the UI (riskOverview dict interpolation line 111, a 'Widgets' section listing raw map entries line 112-113) behind an NL query box. It is unreachable in the school build (school_build_scope.dart:83 hides RouteNames.principalCommand as 'experimental, not wired into real persona flows').
- **Impact:** None today (hidden — the right call). Risk is future re-surfacing as-is: it duplicates the real principal overview in Management and would erode the one-home-for-the-principal IA.
- **Recommendation:** Keep hidden; if ever revived, merge its NL-query idea into the Management principal overview instead of a second command center.

### F-158 · [P1] — Web 'parity' inverts the platform priority: web got the better IA while mobile-first users got the orphaned one
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** navigation/IA · **Role:** principal
- **Evidence:** Web sidebar is cleanly grouped (Overview/People/Academics/Operations/Finance/Engage/Configure) with top-level Attendance (/attendance) and Communication (/communication) entries (web/src/routes/navConfig.ts:30-84). The Flutter admin nav (the product's primary platform per the north star) has neither, and buries broadcast + 19 other admin tools in an unlinked 'School Completion' hub whose name is internal project jargon (school_completion_hub_screen.dart:12).
- **Impact:** The declared mobile-first product delivers its weakest information architecture on mobile; 'School Completion' as a user-facing concept is meaningless to school staff.
- **Recommendation:** Port the web nav grouping back to the Flutter admin drawer/hub and rename/dissolve 'School Completion' into Academics Setup + Communication.

## Reviewer: Class Teacher (32, 6 periods/day, phone-only) — Product Review Board  (`journey-teacher`)
**Vote:** NOT CERTIFIED · **Scores:** overall 5, mobile 6, workflowEfficiency 5, maturity 4

**Independent executive assessment:** I simulated my full teaching day against the actual route definitions, screen widgets, providers, and rendered golden screenshots. The surprise is how good the core interaction design is: the exception-first attendance grid (one tap "All present", tap only exceptions, "Fill remaining present", haptic ticks, draft autosave) genuinely beats a paper register — roughly 7 taps for 42 students with 5 absentees. Marks entry is a real spreadsheet-style grid (Enter advances down the column, per-cell save dots, "Save all"), not 42 forms. Substitution cover is surfaced honestly and tapping a schedule row lands me in attendance with the class preselected. The nav discipline (4 tabs + More) and consistent loading/error/empty states are professional.

But my day still cannot be completed. Four defects break core daily work: (1) the attendance "submitted" flag is one global boolean — after I submit my first class, every other class I teach renders locked behind a false "Attendance submitted successfully" banner, so a 6-period teacher can mark exactly one class per app session; (2) the marks field is digits-only with no AB affordance — the mandated "absent = AB not zero" rule is impossible on my phone, pressuring me to type 0 and corrupt exam data in the top-priority module; (3) the attendance-correction dialog ships a hardcoded date "12 Jun 2026" and a canned reason "Biometric sync error — student was present" as free text, so correcting my 9:40 late arrival silently files against a wrong date with a fabricated excuse unless I notice; (4) homework "attachment" is a typed filename plus a pasted link — no camera, no upload — so photographing the textbook page (the single most common homework action) is impossible and WhatsApp wins. These are targeted completion bugs, not structural flaws — the design system underneath should be kept — but as shipped, a teacher's day breaks before second period, so I cannot certify.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Exception-first attendance marking is world-class in design: 'All present' + tap-only-exceptions + 'Fill remaining present', 68px tap tiles, haptic feedback, long-press picker for half-day/excused (lib/features/teacher/attendance/widgets/attendance_exception_grid.dart, teacher_attendance_screen.dart:280-314) — ~7 taps for a class of 42 with 5 absentees, faster than the paper register
- Draft autosave of a half-marked roster, debounced and flushed on backgrounding, with resume-on-return (teacher_attendance_screen.dart:142-190) — an interrupted teacher between periods never loses work
- Marks entry is true bulk entry, not 42 forms: Enter advances to the next student, per-cell save-state dots, 'Save all (N)' for dirty rows, live column stats (teacher_exams_screen.dart:466-556, lib/shared/marks_grid/marks_grid.dart)
- Substitution/cover duty is surfaced exactly where I need it: self-hiding CoverAlertCard on the dashboard, 'Covering <teacher>' chips on today's periods, and tapping a period jumps straight to attendance with that class preselected (cover_alert_card.dart, teacher_today_screen.dart:50-79, teacher_attendance_screen.dart:34-74)
- Navigation discipline: max 4 primary tabs + a More sheet enforced by spec (lib/shared/navigation/persona_nav.dart:59-61), and out-of-scope verticals (salon/restaurant/healthcare/franchise/white-label) are genuinely blocked at the router (route_guards.dart:411, school_build_scope.dart) — hide-first done right
- Consistent async-state grammar on every teacher screen (skeleton/loading/error-with-retry/empty), Semantics labels on grid tiles and stats, contrast-aware badge text, and deliberate text-scale clamping only in dense grids (attendance_exception_grid.dart:46-52, marks_grid.dart:108-115)
- Staff login is simple and appropriate: email/mobile toggle, 10-digit validation, OTP, remember-session (staff_login_screen.dart)

**Findings:**

### F-159 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-31) — Global 'submitted' flag locks every class after first attendance submit — a 6-period teacher can mark only one class per session
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/attendance · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_provider.dart:22 (teacherAttendanceSubmittedProvider is a single non-autoDispose StateProvider<bool>), :94 (isSubmitted applied to whatever class is currently selected, also OR'd with the global MockAttendanceSyncStore.hasTeacherSubmission), :226 (set true on submit; grep shows no reset anywhere). Class switching only writes teacherAttendanceClassProvider (teacher_attendance_screen.dart:247-249). When isSubmitted is true: updateStudentMark/applyBulkMark/fillRemainingAsPresent early-return (provider :104, :120, :141), the grid disables (screen :355), submit disables (:368), and a false 'Attendance submitted successfully' banner shows (:258-263).
- **Impact:** After submitting period-1 attendance for 8-A, selecting 8-B shows the whole roster read-only under a 'submitted' banner for a class that was never marked. The teacher's core daily loop breaks before second period; the false banner also creates phantom attendance records in her mind.
- **Recommendation:** Make submitted-state per class per day (e.g. Map<classId, bool> keyed like the draft keys already are), reset on class switch, and derive the banner from the selected class's real submission status.

### F-160 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-32) — Attendance correction dialog ships hardcoded demo data: date '12 Jun 2026' and canned reason 'Biometric sync error — student was present'
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/attendance · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_workflow.dart:31-34 — reasonController prefilled with 'Biometric sync error — student was present', dateController prefilled with fixed text '12 Jun 2026'; the date is a free-text AksharaFormField (:63-67), not a date picker; toMark defaults to Present (:30) even when correcting to Late.
- **Impact:** My 9:40 late-arrival correction: unless I notice and retype, the correction is filed for the wrong date (12 Jun 2026) with a fabricated excuse, then routed to principal approval — a wrong, plausible-looking audit record created by the default UI state. Also instantly embarrassing in any sales demo (visible canned text).
- **Recommendation:** Default the date to today via showDatePicker (the homework screen already does this correctly, teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:100-114), leave reason empty and required, preselect the tapped student, and default toMark from context.

### F-161 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-33) — Teacher marks entry cannot record AB — digits-only field, no absent affordance; absent students are permanently 'pending'
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/exams · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/shared/marks_grid/marks_grid.dart:125 (FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly) and :123 (number keyboard); lib/features/teacher/exams/teacher_exams_provider.dart:184-194 (save path is int.parse only); lib/features/teacher/exams/exam_models.dart has no status field (grep for absent/AB returns nothing). The admin-side grid DOES support AB/ML/DB (lib/features/academics/exam_admin/exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:642, :874) — the capability exists but was never brought to the teacher surface.
- **Impact:** Entering unit-test marks for 42 students with 2 absentees can never be completed: 'Pending marks' KPI and '/42 entered' stats never clear, and the only way to make the grid look finished is to type 0 — exactly the data-corrupting behavior the frozen exam-status design (NULL + AB) exists to prevent. This is the top-priority module.
- **Recommendation:** Add a per-row status affordance (long-press or a small AB/ML/DB chip next to the field) that clears the mark and saves a status, mirroring the admin grid; count status'd rows as 'entered' in MarksColumnStats.

### F-162 · [P0] · **verified: P2** (see Part 1 V-34) — Homework photo attachment is impossible — 'attachment' is a typed filename plus a pasted link, no camera/gallery/upload
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/homework · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/homework/teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:34-36 (comment: 'optional teacher attachment (reference/label, not a real upload)') and :246-264 — two plain TextFormFields: 'Attachment name (optional)' (hint 'worksheet.pdf') and 'Attachment reference / link (optional)' (hint 'shared drive link').
- **Impact:** The single most common real homework action — photograph the textbook page and send it — cannot be done. A phone-first teacher between classes will not type a filename and paste a drive link; she will use WhatsApp, and the module loses its daily anchor. The fields also fake an affordance the product doesn't have.
- **Recommendation:** Add camera/gallery pick + real upload (an image_picker + storage upload); until the backend exists, remove the pseudo-attachment fields rather than shipping a decoy.

### F-163 · [P1] — Dashboard 'Students requiring attention — Review' is a dead tap: emits student_risk_<id> which no navigation case handles
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/dashboard + router · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/dashboard/teacher_dashboard_screen.dart:303 emits 'student_risk_${item.sisStudentId}'. lib/router/teacher_navigation.dart:46-50 — case 'student_risk' only matches the exact string (which never starts with 'student_risk_', so the inner startsWith branch is unreachable), and the default branch (:68-80) handles schedule_attendance_/mark_attendance_/class_/thread_ prefixes but not student_risk_. The target route and screen exist and work from the class-teacher dashboard (teacher_class_teacher_dashboard_screen.dart:108).
- **Impact:** A teacher taps the warning banner about an at-risk student on her home dashboard and nothing happens — a silent dead interaction on the highest-visibility alert surface.
- **Recommendation:** Move the student_risk_ prefix handling into the default branch: if (actionId.startsWith('student_risk_')) context.push(RouteNames.teacherStudentRisk(id)).

### F-164 · [P1] — Profile avatar in the app bar routes to Home on every teacher screen; Profile is only reachable via the More sheet
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher (shell-wide) · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/router/teacher_navigation.dart:64-65 (case 'profile' → RouteNames.teacherDashboard); teacher_dashboard_screen.dart:50 (onProfileTap → 'profile'); teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:127 and teacher_class_teacher_dashboard_screen.dart:46 (onProfileTap → context.go(teacherDashboard)). A real TeacherProfileScreen exists and is listed in the More sheet (teacher_shell.dart:67-71).
- **Impact:** Tapping your own avatar — a universal 'my profile' affordance — silently dumps you on the dashboard from every screen. Reads as broken; systemic across the whole teacher lane.
- **Recommendation:** Route the 'profile' action to RouteNames.teacherProfile.

### F-165 · [P1] — Floating AI ball is stacked over the center 'Teach' bottom-nav tab by default, obscuring its icon and stealing its tap area
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** copilot + persona nav · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/shared/navigation/persona_nav.dart:86-119 — NavigationBar in a Stack with CopilotBottomNavAiSlot on top; copilot_bottom_nav_ai_slot.dart:22-32 positions the FAB at top:-22 centered, which with 5 slots sits directly over slot 3 ('Teach' = homework+exams, teacher_shell.dart:37-43); ai_access_preferences_provider.dart:52-53 makes bottomNavCenter the DEFAULT for teacher/parent/student. Golden test/golden/goldens/persona_shell_teacher_390x844.png shows the large circle covering the middle destination — its icon is not visible.
- **Impact:** The tab that houses the teacher's homework and exams — two of her three core tasks — is visually buried under the AI button, and taps in the upper half of that slot open the AI chat instead. The AI entry outranks the work itself.
- **Recommendation:** Reserve a real 5th slot for AI (making Teach a visible 4-slot tab), or dock the AI FAB above the bar's right corner instead of over a live destination.

### F-166 · [P1] — Live-mode attendance first-open: selected class defaults to hardcoded mock id 'class-8a-p1' and empty roster shows a misleading search empty-state
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/attendance · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_provider.dart:14-16 (default StateProvider value 'class-8a-p1'); only writes are user chip taps and the ?class= preselect (teacher_attendance_screen.dart:64, :247-249) — nothing reconciles the default to the first server class (API ids come from server DTOs, api_teacher_repository.dart:35-42). With no matching class, students=[] and the screen shows 'No students match your search.' (teacher_attendance_screen.dart:340-348) even though no search was typed.
- **Impact:** In live API mode the teacher's first open of her most-used screen shows an unselected class strip and a lying empty state blaming a search she never made. Recoverable in one tap but corrosive to trust on the daily flow.
- **Recommendation:** Reconcile selection to the first loaded class when the current id is absent from the roster, and only show the search empty-state when _query is non-empty (otherwise 'Select a class above').

### F-167 · [P1] — Exam Results panel hardcodes 'Unit Test — Mathematics' in the class-average insight regardless of the actual exam
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/exams · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/exams/teacher_exams_screen.dart:203-204 — AksharaInsightCard message: 'Class average is $classAveragePercent% for Unit Test — Mathematics.' while the active exam's real title/subject are available (teacherActiveExamProvider, used at :392 for the context header).
- **Impact:** A Science teacher reviewing her Science unit test reads a card asserting the average is for Mathematics — the screen lies about which exam the number belongs to, on the top-priority module. Demo-embarrassing.
- **Recommendation:** Interpolate the active exam's title and subject into the insight message.

### F-168 · [P1] — Parent-message reply failure is silent, and conversations open at the oldest message
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/messages · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/messages/teacher_conversation_screen.dart:204-207 — catch(_) resets _isSending with no snackbar/banner/retry affordance; :56-63 — ListView.builder without reverse:true, a scroll controller, or scroll-to-end, so a long thread renders top-down starting at the oldest message and new sends don't scroll into view.
- **Impact:** On a flaky school network the teacher taps Send, the button flips back from 'Sending' to 'Send', and she has no idea the parent never received the reply. Opening a busy thread, she must scroll down to find the parent's newest message — the opposite of every chat app she knows.
- **Recommendation:** Show an error snackbar with retry on failure (message text is already preserved), and render the thread bottom-anchored (reverse:true with reversed list) with scroll-to-latest after send.

### F-169 · [P1] — Homework targets classes and students by free-typed text instead of the teacher's known roster
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/homework · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** lib/features/teacher/homework/teacher_homework_create_screen.dart:145-168 — 'Class label' is a bare TextFormField (hint 'e.g. 8-A') + Add button building chips from raw text; :236-241 — 'Student name (blank = whole class)' is also free text. The app already knows her real classes (attendance ClassSelectorStrip from getAttendanceClasses; teaching context prefills one label at :51-57).
- **Impact:** Typing '8A' instead of '8-A', or misspelling a student, silently delivers homework to the wrong/no audience with no validation against reality — on a phone keyboard between classes. Inconsistent with the attendance screen, which correctly presents real classes as chips.
- **Recommendation:** Replace the class text field with selectable chips from the teaching roster, and student targeting with a picker filtered to the selected class.

### F-170 · [P1] — Web ERP teacher persona is a read-only skeleton — attendance rows dead-click, homework Assign has no handler and empty class/subject options, no marks entry
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** web/teacher · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/teacher/TeacherPages.tsx:43-44 (TeacherAttendancePage onRowClick={() => {}} — a wired-dead click), :91 (<Button icon="send">Assign</Button> with no onClick), :94-95 (Select options={[]} — class/subject can never be chosen), :106-116 (Exams page is a list with no row action and no marks entry). The whole teacher persona is 194 lines of list shells versus the Flutter app's full flows.
- **Impact:** Any teacher who reaches the web ERP (e.g. on the staffroom desktop) can see her classes but cannot mark attendance, assign homework, or enter marks — dead buttons included. Contradicts the stated 1:1-parity premise of the web track.
- **Recommendation:** Either implement the three core teacher actions on web or remove the dead affordances (Assign button, empty selects, no-op row clicks) and clearly signpost 'use the mobile app' until parity ships.

### F-171 · [P1] — Tablet teacher dashboard is pinned to the left with a dead right third of the screen
- **Platform:** tablet · **Module:** teacher/dashboard · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** Golden test/golden/goldens/teacher_dashboard_834x1194.png — all content occupies roughly the left 60% (ending ~x530 of 834) with blank surface to the right; section headers wrap to 3 lines in the squeezed left column. Cause: teacher_dashboard_screen.dart:68-74 wraps content in ConstrainedBox inside SingleChildScrollView without the Align(topCenter) that sibling screens use (e.g. teacher_attendance_screen.dart:224-231, teacher_exams_screen.dart:86-93, which center correctly).
- **Impact:** On iPad — the form factor schools actually put in staffrooms — the home screen looks broken/unfinished, undermining an otherwise polished dashboard in any demo.
- **Recommendation:** Wrap the dashboard's ConstrainedBox in Align(alignment: Alignment.topCenter), matching the attendance/exams screens.

### F-172 · [P2] — Dashboard schedule-row tap behavior diverges between phone and tablet
- **Platform:** tablet · **Module:** teacher/dashboard · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** teacher_dashboard_screen.dart:156-157 (phone: schedule row → 'schedule_attendance_<label>' → attendance with class preselected) vs :222-223 (tablet: same row → 'class_<id>' → teacher_navigation.dart:76-77 routes to the weekly timetable instead).
- **Impact:** The same gesture on the same card does different things by device: on tablet the teacher expecting to mark attendance lands on the timetable — muscle-memory breakage for anyone using both.
- **Recommendation:** Emit schedule_attendance_<label> on tablet too.

### F-173 · [P2] — Attendance bulk actions and search sit at the top of the screen — a stretch for one-handed use while standing
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** teacher/attendance · **Role:** Class Teacher
- **Evidence:** teacher_attendance_screen.dart:280-335 — 'All present' / 'All absent' / 'Fill remaining present' buttons and the search field render in the header block above the grid; only the tally + Save draft + Submit are in the bottom action bar (:366-392, correctly thumb-reachable).
- **Impact:** The two most-used bulk actions require reaching the top of a 6.1"+ screen one-handed while standing at the classroom door; everything else about this flow is optimized for exactly that posture.
- **Recommendation:** Move 'All present' and 'Fill remaining present' into (or just above) the sticky bottom action bar; leave the rarely-used 'All absent' up top.
