## Reviewer: Parent Persona Reviewer (38, two kids, mid-range Android, English second language)  (`journey-parent`)
**Vote:** NOT CERTIFIED · **Scores:** overall 4, mobile 6, workflowEfficiency 4, aiExperience 5

**Independent executive assessment:** I audited the parent app the way I would live it: log in, switch between my two kids, pay this month's fee, check today's attendance, tonight's homework, exam results, message the teacher, track the bus. The skeleton is genuinely good — a clean 4-tab + More shell, one screen per job, real empty/error states in newer modules, a well-crafted child-switcher chip, real PDF receipts and report cards, and a structured (no free-chat) AI insights center with language choice. Someone on this team knows how to design for parents like me.

But the three journeys I would actually bet my trust on are broken underneath the polish. The fee payment flow LOOKS complete but is simulated end-to-end: I pick "UPI", tap "Pay now", and the app fabricates a transaction reference client-side and shows "Payment successful" with a receipt number — no UPI app, no QR, no money moved. Worse, the money/attendance/dashboard screens silently fall back to hardcoded demo data ("Ravi Kumar · 8-A", "Present", "₹4,200 due") while loading or when the API fails — I could see a fictional child's fee dues presented as my own, or "Present" for a kid who is absent. The primary Pay CTAs hardcode installment 'term_2' regardless of what is actually due. The More menu offers "PTM" which dead-ends in a bare "Access Denied" screen, and the Transport screen shows a permanently hardcoded "Bus is approximately 8 minutes away" with a dead "Refresh ETA" button. Each of these is a support call to the school; two of them (fake payment success, fake attendance/ETA) are trust-destroying. The IA and visual system deserve certification; the data honesty and money path do not — yet.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Bottom navigation is exactly right for a non-technical parent: Home / Academics / Fees / Messages + More, with formerly-orphaned screens (Leave, Notices, Events, Transport, Profile) given visible homes in the More sheet (lib/features/parent/shell/parent_shell.dart:27-110)
- Child-switcher chip is a best-in-class pattern: avatar + name + class + expand_more affordance + full semantics label, opening a simple bottom sheet with checkmark on the active child (lib/shared/widgets/akshara_app_bar.dart:10-89, lib/features/parent/widgets/parent_child_switcher_sheet.dart); switching correctly invalidates every per-child provider (parent_active_child_provider.dart:81-104)
- Real deliverables where it counts: receipt PDF download/share (parent_receipt_detail_screen.dart), report-card PDF export with honest 'No published results yet' empty state (report_card_screen.dart:39-57), fee certificate and year-statement exporters
- Parent Insights is the right shape for AI toward parents: structured summaries only (no free chat), explicit generate action, Hindi/Telugu/English preference, PDF print, honest loading/error/empty states (lib/features/evolution/parent_insights_screen.dart)
- Deterministic reminder banners capped at 3, driven by real module data with no LLM copy (lib/features/parent/dashboard/widgets/parent_reminder_banners.dart:10-33)
- Exams module demonstrates the correct async discipline — real isLoading/hasError from the future, empty fallback instead of fake data (parent_exams_screen.dart:34-93)
- Attendance screen is genuinely parent-friendly: month calendar with tap-a-day detail sheet, absent-KPI scrolls to explanation banner, WhatsApp button to reach the class teacher (parent_attendance_screen.dart:104-239)
- Notifications inbox is honest in live mode — 'show exactly what the server returns', never demo data (notifications_provider.dart:42-59), with per-child context labels

**Findings:**

### F-174 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-35) — Fee payment is simulated end-to-end — 'Payment successful' + receipt with no money movement
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/payment · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_screen.dart:115 wires the Pay button to submitMockPayment; lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_provider.dart:104-163 fabricates transactionRef: 'txn_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}' client-side and flips phase to success; payment_models.dart:4 offers UPI/Card/NetBanking but selection is cosmetic — no UPI intent, no QR, no gateway is ever invoked. Web is worse: web/src/pages/parent/ParentPages.tsx:98 'Pay now' is `window.location.hash = ''` (a no-op) and :141 shows a placeholder 'gateway load ... once connected'.
- **Impact:** The #1 parent journey (pay this month's fee) produces a fake success ceremony and a receipt number without collecting money. In production this creates phantom receipts and reconciliation chaos; in a sales demo, one probing question ('which gateway?') collapses it. Known owner-gated dependency (P0-02), but the flow must not present success until real.
- **Recommendation:** Until the gateway SDK lands, replace the mock submit with an explicit blocked state ('Online payment coming soon — pay at the school office, here's the exact amount and reference') rather than a fake success. Never render a receipt that no money produced.

### F-175 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-36) — Fees, dashboard, attendance, payment and profile silently render fabricated demo data on load/error
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent (cross-cutting) · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** fees_provider.dart:298-300 `data ?? future.value ?? ParentFeesData.mock()` (mock = '₹4,200 due', isOverdue: true); parent_dashboard_provider.dart:312-314 falls back to ParentDashboardData.mock() ('Ravi Kumar', chip 'Present', '₹4,200 due'); parent_attendance_provider.dart:44-48 AttendanceMonthData.mock(); parent_payment_provider.dart:63-101 _fallbackSummary hardcodes 'Ravi Kumar · 8-A' with a live Pay button; parent_profile_provider.dart:33+ hardcodes 'Suresh Kumar / +91 98765 43210'. The guarding loading/error providers (e.g. fees_provider.dart:281-283) are manual StateProviders that NOTHING sets on real API failure, so the skeleton/error branches in the screens never trigger — mock data shows during every cold load and forever on API error.
- **Impact:** A real parent can see a fictional child's name, a fabricated 'Present' status for an absent child, and fabricated rupee amounts presented as fact. On money and attendance — the two highest-trust surfaces — this is a product-killing trust failure, and it also flashes on every cold start before real data arrives.
- **Recommendation:** Delete every `?? Mock()` fallback in parent providers and adopt the exams pattern (parent_exams_screen.dart:34-42): real async.isLoading -> skeleton, async.hasError -> retry, empty -> honest empty state.

### F-176 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-37) — More menu offers 'PTM' which dead-ends in a bare 'Access Denied' screen
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/navigation · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** parent_shell.dart:94-98 puts PTM in the parent More sheet; persona_nav.dart:205-216 renders spec.more with NO SchoolBuildScope filtering; school_build_scope.dart:76-77 hides RouteNames.parentPtm; app_router.dart:2611-2615 returns AccessDeniedScreen for hidden routes; route_guards.dart:247-259 renders a bare lock icon + 'Access Denied'. Dashboard mock quick-action 'ptm' and hardcoded notice n1/event e2 routing (parent_navigation.dart:62-64,86-87) also lead there.
- **Impact:** The app's own menu invites the parent into a locked door. 'Access Denied' reads as 'my account is broken' to a non-technical parent — a guaranteed support call, and an instant fail in any demo where someone taps every tile.
- **Recommendation:** Filter MoreNavSheet/PersonaNavSpec destinations through SchoolBuildScope.isRouteHidden (and entitlements) so gated modules never render as menu items.

### F-177 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-38) — Transport shows a hardcoded fake bus ETA ('approximately 8 minutes away') with a dead 'Refresh ETA' button
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/transport · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** parent_transport_screen.dart:74-81 — const AksharaInsightCard(message: 'Bus is approximately 8 minutes away (telemetry preview). Live map integration is not enabled in this build.', actionLabel: 'Refresh ETA') with NO onAction (akshara_insight_card.dart:19,28 shows onAction is nullable and none is passed), rendered under the 'AKSHARA SUGGESTS' AI eyebrow.
- **Impact:** A parent deciding when to walk their child to the stop sees a fabricated ETA that never changes, plus developer jargon ('telemetry preview', 'not enabled in this build') and a button that does nothing. This is safety-adjacent fabricated information and it poisons trust in every other 'AKSHARA SUGGESTS' card.
- **Recommendation:** Remove the fake ETA card entirely; show route/stop facts only, with an honest 'Live bus tracking coming soon' note if needed.

### F-178 · [P1] — Primary Pay CTAs hardcode installment 'term_2'; receipt ids fabricated client-side
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/fees · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** parent_fees_screen.dart:108 and :154 (hero + sticky bar both pass installmentId: 'term_2'); parent_navigation.dart:33-36 dashboard 'pay_fee' pushes '?installmentId=term_2'; parent_payment_provider.dart:11-13 default provider state 'term_2'; app_router.dart:2543-2565 maps payment-history/installments to receipt ids via hardcoded 'ph_1'->'rcpt_term_1' switches and 'rcpt_$installmentId' string fabrication.
- **Impact:** With real backend data (API repository maps real fee DTOs — api_parent_repository.dart:69-72), the most prominent Pay buttons request a summary for a non-existent installment and 'View receipt' fabricates ids the backend never issued — the money path misfires precisely when the app goes live. Only the per-row timeline button passes the real id.
- **Recommendation:** Derive the CTA installment from data (first due installment) and use backend-issued receipt ids end-to-end.

### F-179 · [P1] — Child switching only exists on Home — every other screen strands a two-kid parent
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent (cross-cutting) · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** AksharaChildSelectorChip is referenced in exactly two files: akshara_app_bar.dart (definition) and parent_dashboard_screen.dart:54 (sole usage). Fees/Attendance/Homework/Exams/Messages app bars show only a static child-name subtitle (e.g. parent_attendance_screen.dart:60, parent_fees_screen.dart:47-60) with no switch affordance.
- **Impact:** My nightly routine — check both kids' homework and attendance — becomes: Academics -> Home -> tap chip -> switch -> Academics, repeated per module per child. That is 4+ extra taps each time for a family with two kids in the same school, the product's own core scenario (PAR-D2 exists precisely for multi-child).
- **Recommendation:** Make the child chip the app-bar title on every per-child parent screen; the invalidation plumbing (selectParentActiveChild) already supports it.

### F-180 · [P1] — No way to start a conversation with the class teacher; failed sends are silent
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/messages · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** parent_messages_screen.dart:31-117 renders inbox + existing threads only — no compose button/FAB anywhere; a parent with zero threads sees 'No messages from school yet.' dead-end (:48-51). parent_conversation_screen.dart:164-167 catches send failure with `catch (_)` and only stops the spinner — no error message, message silently lost.
- **Impact:** Journey 'message the class teacher' cannot be initiated from the Messages tab (the only WhatsApp-style path is buried inside an attendance day-detail sheet). And when a reply fails on a flaky mobile network — the norm for this persona — the parent believes it was sent.
- **Recommendation:** Add a 'Message teacher' compose entry (teacher list scoped to the child) and a visible send-failure state with retry.

### F-181 · [P1] — Five overlapping 'smart' surfaces on one dashboard — concept overload for a non-technical parent
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/dashboard · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** parent_dashboard_screen.dart stacks: AI copilot app-bar button (:61-63), AdaptivePriorityFeedSection (:117-120), 'Action Needed' tile (:122-128), 'Parent Experience Hub' tile ('Homework status, exam readiness & structured insights', :155-161), 'Parent Insights' tile ('AI summaries...', :163-169), plus AksharaAiSuggestionBar (:170-177). The Experience Hub itself is a 6-tab screen (parent_experience_hub_screen.dart:33). Golden parent_dashboard_390x844.png confirms the wall-of-banners feel: hero + 3 KPI cards + red banner + 3 stacked tiles + card + more tiles before any content the parent asked for.
- **Impact:** 'Action Needed' vs 'Experience Hub' vs 'Insights' vs the AI feed are indistinguishable to my persona — WhatsApp/PhonePe have zero such meta-surfaces. Every extra concept is a support call; this violates the product's own hide-first north star.
- **Recommendation:** Collapse to ONE aggregator ('Action Needed') on the dashboard; move Insights/Hub into it or behind More.

### F-182 · [P1] — Production login copy leaks internals: 'staging server' and 'demo-school phone number'
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** auth · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** login_screen.dart:159 — non-demo subtitle reads 'Enter your registered mobile number. OTP is verified by the staging server.'; :78 — non-demo failure snackbar says 'Unable to send OTP. Use a registered demo-school phone number.'
- **Impact:** The very first screen a parent ever sees speaks developer language. 'Staging server' and 'demo-school' on a production login destroy the professional first impression and confuse an ESL parent ('is this the real app?').
- **Recommendation:** Replace with plain copy: 'We'll send a one-time password to this number' / 'This number isn't registered with the school. Please contact the school office.'

### F-183 · [P1] — No SMS OTP autofill — manual 6-digit entry on the very first interaction
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** auth · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** otp_verification_screen.dart:213-233 — TextFormField has keyboardType/inputFormatters but no autofillHints: [AutofillHints.oneTimeCode] and no Android SMS Retriever/User Consent integration.
- **Impact:** Every app this parent already uses (WhatsApp, PhonePe, GPay) auto-reads the OTP. Manual entry (memorize 6 digits, app-switch, type) is precisely where low-tech-confidence users fail and call the school on day one.
- **Recommendation:** Add AutofillHints.oneTimeCode (iOS keyboard suggestion) and SMS Retriever on Android.

### F-184 · [P1] — Parent dashboard on tablet pins content left with a huge dead right half
- **Platform:** tablet · **Module:** parent/dashboard · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** Golden test/golden/goldens/parent_dashboard_834x1194.png — the entire content column occupies only the left ~530px; the right ~300px is blank background from hero to fold. parent_dashboard_screen.dart:81-87 wraps content in SingleChildScrollView + ConstrainedBox without centering the constrained column.
- **Impact:** On the 834x1194 tablet the app looks broken/half-rendered — unacceptable for demos on iPads, a common sales scenario for schools.
- **Recommendation:** Center the constrained content (Align/Center around the ConstrainedBox) or use a 2-column tablet composition.

### F-185 · [P1] — Web parent portal is a stub that contradicts the 1:1 parity claim
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** web/parent · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/parent/ParentPages.tsx — 258 lines implement the entire parent portal; :98 'Pay now' handler is `window.location.hash = ''` (literal no-op); :141 payment page renders 'The payable amount and gateway load from /parent/fees and /parent/payments/initiate once connected.'
- **Impact:** A parent who logs in on the web (link shared by school on desktop) meets a dead 'Pay now' button and placeholder prose. Fine for a frozen internal lane; not fine while parent web routes are reachable in production navigation (navConfig.ts:98-105).
- **Recommendation:** Either hide the parent web portal until real, or bring the fees/pay pages to at least honest 'pay on the mobile app' guidance.

### F-186 · [P2] — Hardcoded phantom '2 unread notifications' badge on receipt detail
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/receipts · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** parent_receipt_detail_screen.dart:44 — `unreadNotifications: 2` hardcoded in the AksharaAppBar, while every other screen derives it from data (e.g. parent_dashboard_screen.dart:43 uses unreadNotificationsCountProvider).
- **Impact:** A permanent fake red '2' badge trains the parent to ignore the notification bell — the exact spam-blindness the deterministic notification system was built to avoid.
- **Recommendation:** Wire to unreadNotificationsCountProvider.

### F-187 · [P2] — Demo-id routing baked into notice/event taps — real notices lose detail context
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/dashboard · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** parent_navigation.dart:86-93 — actionId 'notice_n1'/'event_e2' hardcoded to PTM, 'notice_n3' to Transport; all other notice_*/event_* ids fall through to the generic list screens, not a detail view.
- **Impact:** Tapping a specific notice card on the dashboard opens the full notices list, forcing the parent to find the same notice again; meanwhile demo ids n1/n3 get magic routing (to a PTM screen that is itself Access-Denied-gated).
- **Recommendation:** Route notice/event taps to a detail view by real id; delete the demo-id special cases.

### F-188 · [P2] — 'Academics' tab label lands on Attendance — label/content mismatch
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/navigation · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** parent_shell.dart:35-45 — tab labelled 'Academics' (school icon) routes to RouteNames.parentAttendance; homework/exams/timetable are reachable only via the shortcuts strip at the top of the attendance screen (parent_attendance_screen.dart:104-111).
- **Impact:** A parent looking for tonight's homework taps 'Academics' and gets an attendance calendar; the shortcut strip saves it, but the first-tap mismatch adds hesitation to a nightly task.
- **Recommendation:** Either label the tab 'Attendance' or land on a small academics hub where Homework is first.

### F-189 · [P2] — Out-of-scope verticals (salon/restaurant/healthcare) still routed and permission-granted
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** admin/verticals · **Role:** superAdmin (not parent-reachable)
- **Evidence:** app_router.dart:1060-1112 registers healthcare/salon/restaurant routes with no SchoolBuildScope check in their builders (only parentPtm checks isRouteHidden at :2613); role_permissions.dart:166-171 still grants viewHealthcare/viewSalonBusiness/viewRestaurantHospitality to superAdmin; nav tiles are hidden by SchoolBuildScope filtering (admin_navigation_provider.dart:231), so they are not visible in menus but remain deep-linkable.
- **Impact:** Owner declared these verticals out of product scope. They are hidden from navigation (good) but remain live routes with granted permissions — one forgotten flag flip or deep link away from a 'why does my school ERP have a Salon?' demo moment.
- **Recommendation:** Guard the vertical route builders with SchoolBuildScope.isRouteHidden like parentPtm, and revoke the vertical permissions from superAdmin.

---

## Reviewer: Student (Class 9) — shared-device persona, Product Review Board  (`journey-student`)
**Vote:** NOT CERTIFIED · **Scores:** overall 4, mobile 5, workflowEfficiency 4

**Independent executive assessment:** The student-facing surface of Akshara is a tale of two products. The screen layer is genuinely good: a clean 4-tab shell (Home / Learn / Schedule / Results + More) with a center AI slot, a modern gradient-hero dashboard with a casual greeting, disciplined loading/error/empty states on every screen, real Semantics labels, dark mode that renders beautifully in the goldens, and a real end-to-end homework loop (teacher creates -> per-student entity -> student sees status -> submits -> teacher reviews). A teenager would not be embarrassed by how this app looks.

But when I simulate actually BEING that student on a real (non-QA-seeded) school, the product collapses at four load-bearing points. (1) There is no way in: the backend fully supports student_id OTP login and scope switching, but neither the Flutter nor the web login screen exposes any student entry — the front door only understands "parent's mobile number", and the production copy on that door literally says "verified by the staging server" and "use a registered demo-school phone number". (2) The home dashboard is wired to a seed-only snapshot with no production writer: on a real tenant it shows "--" attendance while the attendance tab shows real records, zero homework due while the Learn tab lists real homework, a permanently blank exam-reminder card (no empty-guard), and no quick actions at all — the flagship screen contradicts its own tabs. (3) The exam schedule — top-priority module — can never show really-scheduled exams to a student (only examResults gets a live overlay; upcomingExams/averagePercent/subjectScores are seed-frozen), and no admit card exists student-side though staff generate hall tickets. (4) The web student portal is structurally dead: six of nine pages request snapshot-shaped endpoints through list/KPI adapters that can never match, and web has no homework submit at all. Add no logout on the student app (fatal for a shared-device persona) and text-only "attachment reference" homework submission, and the student experience is a well-dressed shell whose primary journeys don't survive contact with production data. NOT_CERTIFIED for this persona until the data spine and access story are fixed.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Real end-to-end homework loop: teacher create writes per-student homework_item entities (pilot_operations_repository.ts:2384), student list overlays real submission state (overlayStudentHomeworkFromSubmissions:1910), submit POST has a live backend (pilot_operations_router.ts:53) with audit + read invalidation (student_mutations_provider.dart:39-61)
- Timetable and attendance screens overlay REAL data (handleTimetableSnapshot with overlayTimetableSnapshotFromSlots, mobile_read_handlers.ts:267-279; dedicated attendance overlay) — these two journeys genuinely work
- Disciplined async UX on every student screen: skeleton -> error-with-retry -> honest empty states ('No periods scheduled today', 'No homework in this filter') — student_dashboard_screen.dart:67-76, student_timetable_screen.dart:43-56
- Modern, teen-appropriate visual language: gradient hero + casual greeting ('Hey Ravi! 👋'), KPI cards, center AI slot in bottom nav, strong dark-mode rendering (test/golden/goldens/dark_student_dashboard_390x844.png)
- Clean, enforced IA: <=4 primary tabs + shared More sheet, drift prevented by persona_nav_test (persona_nav.dart:59-61); strict role-walled routing confines student sessions to /student (app_router.dart:2468-2480)
- Accessibility effort is real: Semantics containers/labels on KPI rows, exam cards, week labels (student_exams_screen.dart:172-175, _StatusKpiRow Semantics 'Status summary')
- Student-side report card PDF export reuses the parent export service and only appears when a published card exists (student_report_card_screen.dart:28-54)
- Achievement publishing does reach students as targeted notices (publisher_dispatch.ts:35,46 -> /student/notices)

**Findings:**

### F-190 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-39) — Student home dashboard is seed-only: on real tenants it renders empty/contradictory data and loses all quick actions
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** student_app/dashboard · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** student_handlers.ts:8-9 (dashboard = plain handleSnapshot('snapshot_dashboard'), NO overlay — unlike attendance:12-13 and timetable:24-26 which overlay real records); buildDefaultStudentSnapshot returns todaySchedule:[], attendanceKpi '--'/'No attendance recorded yet', homeworkDue:[], blank examReminder, quickActions:[], blank aiInsight (pilot_operations_repository.ts:1431-1449); only writers of snapshot_dashboard are migration demo seeds (supabase/migrations/20260614400000_mobile_read_apis.sql:104,135,164) and QA script scripts/qa/live_cert_pilot_full_year.py; teacher homework creation writes only homework_item entities, never the dashboard snapshot (pilot_operations_repository.ts:2382-2406); client renders everything from this payload including server-driven quickActions (student_dashboard_screen.dart:104-206, student_mapper.dart:28)
- **Impact:** A real student's home screen shows no schedule, '--' attendance even when the Attendance tab shows real records, 0 homework due while the Learn tab lists real homework, and the 'Submit HW'/'Report Card'/'Progress' shortcuts vanish entirely. The flagship screen contradicts its own tabs; on the demo tenant it shows frozen seed data ('Marked 9:12 AM', exam dated 12 Jun 2026) forever.
- **Recommendation:** Compute the dashboard server-side from the same live sources the tabs use (attendance records, homework_item entities, published exam schedule), and make quick actions client-defined navigation, not server snapshot content.

### F-191 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-40) — No student login path exists in either client despite full backend support — the persona's access story is incoherent
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** auth · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** Backend supports student_id-type OTP login yielding scope='student' (auth_handlers.ts:217-238, 372-387) and session scope-switching (auth_handlers.ts:571-609); the Flutter app only ever sends identifierType:'mobile' with no scope (auth_provider.dart:246-263, 282-301) and production role hardcoded to parent (login_screen.dart:52); no scope-switch call exists anywhere in lib/ (grep=0); web auth pages have no student entry (web/src/pages/auth/LoginPage.tsx); route guards wall the student shell to student-scope sessions only (app_router.dart:2471)
- **Impact:** A Class 9 student with no phone (the designed persona: OTP goes to the parent) has no discoverable way to reach the 9-screen student app. If the parent's number maps to both parent and student memberships, the client offers no chooser — the server silently picks. The entire student product is effectively unreachable through the front door.
- **Recommendation:** Add a 'Student? Sign in with your Student ID' path (school code + Public Student ID -> OTP to registered parent phone), and/or a parent-side 'Open student mode' using the existing scope-switch endpoint.

### F-192 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-41) — Student exam schedule can never show really-scheduled exams; 'Class average' KPI is seed-frozen
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** student_app/exams · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** handleExams = handleSnapshot('snapshot_exams') (student_handlers.ts:20-22); overlayExamsSnapshotFromResults overlays ONLY examResults, returning seed upcomingExams/averagePercent/subjectScores untouched (pilot_operations_repository.ts:1884-1900); default fallback = averagePercent:0, upcomingExams:[] (1458-1464); grep shows no production writer of upcomingExams anywhere; real exam scheduling + hall tickets exist in the staff exam-administration module but never publish to student snapshots; UI renders the seed values as 'Upcoming' count and 'Class average' (student_exams_screen.dart:89-102, 131-133)
- **Impact:** Exams are the product's top-priority module, yet when a school schedules a mid-term, students see nothing (or stale seed data); the first KPI on the Exams screen reads 'Class average 0%' on real tenants.
- **Recommendation:** Publish scheduled exams from the exam-administration module into the student/parent read path, and compute average from published results (or drop the KPI).

### F-193 · [P0] · **verified: P0** (see Part 1 V-42) — Web student portal is structurally dead: 6 of 9 pages can never render data, and homework cannot be submitted on web
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** web/student · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** StudentDashboardPage + StudentProgressPage expect {kpis:[...]} via KpiPage (KpiPage.tsx:50) from /student/dashboard, which returns a snapshot with no kpis key; Attendance/Exams/ReportCard/Timetable use fetchList (list.ts:19-25 falls back to {items:[]}) against snapshot-shaped responses ({kpi,calendarDays,recentLogs}, {averagePercent,upcomingExams,...}, {days:[...]}) — StudentPages.tsx:30-39, 53-64, 66-77, 106-116; homework page is a read-only table with no submit action (StudentPages.tsx:41-51) while Flutter has a full submit sheet; profile page renders String(v) over Object.entries so the parentContacts array prints '[object Object]' (StudentPages.tsx:134-138 vs payload at pilot_operations_repository.ts:1370-1394)
- **Impact:** A student on a desktop (the only web option) gets permanent 'loads once connected' placeholders on dashboard, attendance, timetable, exams, report card, and progress, and cannot submit homework at all. The claimed 1:1 parity with the Flutter app does not exist for this persona.
- **Recommendation:** Add snapshot adapters per endpoint (or a shared student view-model API), and implement web homework submission against POST /student/homework/submit.

### F-194 · [P1] — Production login screen leaks internal jargon: 'staging server' helper text and 'demo-school' error copy
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** auth · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** login_screen.dart:159 — non-demo branch helper reads 'Enter your registered mobile number. OTP is verified by the staging server.'; login_screen.dart:76-78 — non-demo branch OTP failure reads 'Unable to send OTP. Use a registered demo-school phone number.'
- **Impact:** The very first screen every user (and every sales demo) sees tells production users about staging servers and demo schools — instant credibility damage.
- **Recommendation:** Replace both strings with production copy ('OTP sent to the registered parent mobile number' / 'This number is not registered with your school').

### F-195 · [P1] — No logout anywhere in the student app — fatal on the shared-device persona
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** student_app/profile · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** grep -i 'logout|sign out' across lib/features/student_app/ returns 0 hits; parent profile HAS a logout button (parent_profile_screen.dart:229-232); student profile offers only Appearance + a dead 'App settings — coming soon' tile (student_profile_screen.dart:152-195); the only session-clear path is the QA-build-only persona switcher (qa_visual_switcher.dart:13-19, gated at :27)
- **Impact:** The persona explicitly shares a device with the parent (parent's old phone). Once a student-scope session exists, nobody can sign out to switch back to the parent account without clearing app data.
- **Recommendation:** Add Sign out to the student profile (reuse auth_logout.dart exactly as the parent profile does).

### F-196 · [P1] — Homework 'submission' is a typed text reference — no actual file/photo upload of the work
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** student_app/homework · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** student_homework_screen.dart:172-175 — code comment admits 'a label/URL, not a real uploaded file — no homework storage bucket yet'; the sheet's field hint is 'e.g. answer photo link or file name' (student_homework_screen.dart:214-222)
- **Impact:** The #1 student action is hollow: a Class 9 student who solved an algebra worksheet on paper has no way to hand in the actual work — they are asked to type a 'photo link' with nowhere to host a photo. Teachers cannot see what was submitted.
- **Recommendation:** Add camera/photo attachment upload to a storage bucket before wide rollout; until then relabel the flow 'Mark as done + note' so it doesn't promise a submission it can't carry.

### F-197 · [P1] — No admit card / hall ticket in the student (or parent) app although staff generate them
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** student_app/exams · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** EXM-D4 hall tickets exist staff-side only: akshara_report_export_service.dart:439,1046 ('Hall Ticket / Admit Card' print-ready PDF), exam_reports_provider.dart:191, exam_administration_store.dart:400; grep -i 'admit|hall ticket' across lib/features/student_app/ and lib/features/parent/ = 0 hits
- **Impact:** For an Indian school ERP whose top-priority module is Exams, students/parents cannot view or download their own admit card — it must be printed and physically distributed, undermining the 'easiest ERP' self-service story.
- **Recommendation:** Surface the already-generated per-student hall ticket as a download in the student Exams > Upcoming section (and parent app).

### F-198 · [P1] — Library dead end: student cannot see borrowed books or due dates anywhere
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** student_app / library · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** grep -i 'library' across lib/features/student_app/ and lib/features/parent/ = 0 hits; the library module exists staff-side (lib/features/library/, web library pages); student shell tabs and More sheet contain no library destination (student_shell.dart:22-72); web student nav likewise (navConfig.ts:109-117)
- **Impact:** Journey 'which books do I have and when are they due' is impossible for the borrower — students find out about overdue books only when fined or told by the librarian.
- **Recommendation:** Add a read-only 'My library' card (issued books + due dates) to the student More sheet, fed by the existing library issue records.

### F-199 · [P1] — Same number, two contradictory labels: 'Class average' on Exams vs the student's own 'Average' on Report Card
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** student_app/exams · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** student_exams_screen.dart:89-90 labels data.averagePercent 'Class average'; student_report_card_screen.dart:67 renders the identical field as 'Average: X%' (term summary of the student's own results); no class-average computation exists in the read path (overlay passes seed value through, pilot_operations_repository.ts:1886-1900)
- **Impact:** At least one label lies to the student; on real tenants both show 0. A student judging 'am I above class average?' is reading a fabricated comparison.
- **Recommendation:** Pick one semantic ('My average'), compute it from published results, and delete the other label.

### F-200 · [P1] — Tablet student dashboard pins content to the left with ~40% dead whitespace
- **Platform:** tablet · **Module:** student_app/dashboard · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** test/golden/goldens/student_dashboard_834x1194.png — content column occupies the left ~528px, right side fully blank; cause: student_dashboard_screen.dart:89-94 applies ConstrainedBox(contentConstraints) inside the scroll view WITHOUT the Align(topCenter) wrapper that sibling screens use (student_homework_screen.dart:70-77, student_timetable_screen.dart:64-70 both center correctly)
- **Impact:** On iPads/Android tablets the home screen looks broken/unfinished — the first screen a student sees on the device class schools most often buy.
- **Recommendation:** Wrap the dashboard content in Align(alignment: Alignment.topCenter) like every other student screen.

### F-201 · [P1] — Blank exam-reminder card renders unconditionally when no exam exists
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** student_app/dashboard · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** exam_reminder_card.dart:20-60 has no empty-guard — with the production fallback payload (title:'', subject:'', dateLabel:'', daysUntil:0 at pilot_operations_repository.ts:1446) it renders a styled card whose Semantics label reads 'Exam reminder: , , , in 0 days'; contrast DailyScheduleStrip which self-guards (daily_schedule_strip.dart:45-51)
- **Impact:** Real-tenant students see a permanent empty reminder card on Home; screen-reader users hear gibberish.
- **Recommendation:** Return SizedBox.shrink() when reminder.id/title is empty, mirroring AdaptivePriorityFeedSection's self-hide behavior.

### F-202 · [P2] — Two different report-card UIs in one persona; Exams entry bypasses the router
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** student_app/exams · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** Exams > Results > 'View report card' pushes the PARENT ReportCardScreen via raw MaterialPageRoute (student_exams_screen.dart:253-259), while More > Report Card routes to StudentReportCardScreen via go_router (/student/report-card, student_shell.dart:51-55); the MaterialPageRoute covers the bottom nav and has no URL/deep-link
- **Impact:** The same artifact looks different depending on entry point; back behavior and deep-linking are inconsistent.
- **Recommendation:** Point the Exams button at RouteNames.studentReportCard and delete the parent-screen push.

### F-203 · [P2] — Attendance is orphaned in mobile IA: reachable only via a dashboard KPI tap, highlights the 'Results' tab
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** student_app/attendance · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** Only navigation entry is actionId 'attendance' from the dashboard KPI card (student_navigation.dart:15-16); attendance is absent from tabs and the More sheet (student_shell.dart:22-72) yet its route highlights the trophy-icon 'Results' tab via matchPrefixes (student_shell.dart:42-48); web nav shows Attendance as a first-class item (navConfig.ts:111) — cross-platform IA mismatch; nav label 'Results' also mismatches the screen title 'Exams'
- **Impact:** Since the dashboard KPI is seed-broken on real tenants (shows '--'), the sole doorway to attendance history advertises wrong data; when inside, the highlighted trophy tab confuses orientation.
- **Recommendation:** Add Attendance to the More sheet, give it its own highlight (or none), and rename the tab or screen so 'Results'/'Exams' agree.

### F-204 · [P2] — 'Coming soon' dead tile on student profile violates the hide-first philosophy
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** student_app/profile · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** student_profile_screen.dart:160-195 — permanent 'App settings / Notifications, language, and privacy — coming soon' ListTile with null onTap in the default build; product north star mandates hide-first
- **Impact:** Advertises absent functionality to every student; also occupies the slot where Sign out should live.
- **Recommendation:** Remove the tile until the feature exists (replace with Sign out).

### F-205 · [P2] — Report card screen renders section headers and 'Average: 0.0%' with zero data (no empty state)
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** student_app/progress · **Role:** Student
- **Evidence:** student_report_card_screen.dart:57-89 — ListView always renders 'Term summary', 'Average: ...%', 'Subject scores' and 'Recent results' headers with no isEmpty guard; on real tenants averagePercent=0 and subjectScores=[] (pilot_operations_repository.ts:1458-1464)
- **Impact:** A student opening Report Card before results publish sees 'Average: 0.0%' — reads as a failing grade — above empty headers.
- **Recommendation:** Add an 'No report card published yet' empty state and hide the average until results exist.

### F-206 · [P2] — Out-of-scope vertical routes (salon/restaurant/healthcare/franchise) remain registered in the live router
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** router · **Role:** Platform (observed while auditing student guards)
- **Evidence:** app_router.dart:95-119 imports and :729 (franchise), :1060-1084 (healthcare) register live routes; salon screens contain live internal navigation (salon_dashboard_screen.dart:41-51); student sessions are blocked by the role wall (app_router.dart:2468-2480) but staff sessions can deep-link to them
- **Impact:** Owner-decided out-of-scope verticals are still deployable surface area — an embarrassment risk if any staff user deep-links during a demo.
- **Recommendation:** Remove the vertical route registrations and navigation files from the app target.

## Reviewer: School Accountant (web-primary, 200 fee transactions/day peak)  (`journey-accountant`)
**Vote:** NOT CERTIFIED · **Scores:** overall 3, web 2, workflowEfficiency 2, maturity 3

**Independent executive assessment:** I simulated my working day in the web ERP (web/src/pages/finance/) and cross-checked every flow against the backend (supabase/functions/_shared/finance/) and the Flutter app. The web finance module is a visually polished, honestly-stated, read-only shell: the ONLY POST request in the entire web application is auth login/verify-otp (web/src/lib/auth/AuthContext.tsx:115,127 — no other file in web/src contains a mutation). I cannot collect a fee, approve a refund, assign a discount, create a fee structure, resolve a bounced cheque, close a day, print a receipt, or export a single report from the tool I would sit in from 9 to 5. Every action button in finance is either dead (no onClick), permanently disabled, or — worst — fake: the shared ModuleSettingsPage shows a "Settings saved" success toast without ever calling the existing PUT endpoint. On top of that, at least 4 of the 14 finance tabs (Reconciliation, Executive, Copilot, Discounts) are wired to response shapes the backend does not send, so they render permanently-empty states against the live API, and Student Accounts calls a list endpoint that does not exist server-side. The prior "229/229 routes" web certification measured reachability, not task completability — the project's own Product Reality Audit warned exactly about this failure mode.

The tragedy is that the underlying product is real: the backend has a governed, audited, workflow-complete finance API (create/cancel collections with mandatory reasons, refund maker-checker, day-close, offline reconcile/bounce, printable ledgers, late-fee waivers), and the Flutter app exposes most of it well (student-search-to-collect seeded with real balances, draft autosave, honest offline "queued receipt" ceremony, receipt PDF print/share with audited duplicate-reprint, telecaller call queue with promise-to-pay). But my persona's primary platform is the desktop web app, and there it is not an ERP — it is a report viewer whose reports are also switched off. Even cross-platform, two money-truth gaps remain: the daily summary is hardwired to CURRENT_DATE (yesterday cannot be reconciled anywhere) and its mode split computes only cash + UPI, so cheque/DD amounts fall into no bucket and the visible figures do not tie.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Honest empty/error states everywhere on web: AsyncBoundary (web/src/components/system/AsyncBoundary.tsx) never fabricates data, distinguishes module-disabled (403) and needs-filters (422) states with human copy — a genuinely good pattern.
- Backend finance API is workflow-complete and governed: mandatory cancellation reasons (finance_collections_handlers.ts:385-390), refund approve/reject maker-checker (finance_router.ts:292-300), day-close lock (394-397), offline reconcile/bounce (327-334), printable student ledger (207-211), all with mutation audits and per-route RBAC.
- Flutter counter journey is thoughtfully built: cashier searches by name/admission/class (student_accounts screen:63-73), 'Collect fee' on the row seeds the dialog with the student's REAL balance (260-277), drafts autosave against interruption, offline collections close with an amber 'queued receipt' ceremony instead of a false green (finance_workflow_actions.dart:772-840).
- Receipt discipline on Flutter: build/print/share PDF plus DUPLICATE-stamped reprint that is audited server-side (finance_receipt_pdf_service.dart:218-256; collection_detail screen:57-77).
- Web shell fundamentals are right: ⌘K command palette with arrow-key navigation (CommandPalette.tsx), financeAdmin lands directly on /finance/dashboard (roles.ts:48-50), sortable tables, consistent M3-token visual system, dark-mode goldens present.

**Findings:**

### F-207 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-43) — Entire web finance module is read-only — zero business mutations exist in the web app
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** grep for POST across web/src: only web/src/lib/auth/AuthContext.tsx:115,127 (login/verify-otp). No finance page contains any write call. Dead buttons: CollectionsPage.tsx:46 ('Collect fee', no onClick), FeeStructuresPage.tsx:31 ('New structure'), DiscountsPage.tsx:30 ('Assign discount'), FinanceExtraPages.tsx:30 ('Assign fee'), :45 ('Record payment'), :58 ('New QR'). Backend supports all of these (finance_router.ts:164,221,288,310,320,341).
- **Impact:** The accountant's primary 9-5 tool cannot perform one single money action. Every daily workflow — collect, refund, concession, resolve, close day — is blocked. Would fail instantly in a sales demo the moment a prospect clicks any button.
- **Recommendation:** Treat web finance as unbuilt, not frozen. Implement the collect-fee flow first (student search → outstanding → amount → mode → receipt), then refunds, then offline resolution, wired to the already-certified endpoints.

### F-208 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-44) — No fee-collection flow on web: 'Collect fee' buttons dead or circular
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance/collections · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** CollectionsPage.tsx:46 <Button icon="add">Collect fee</Button> has no onClick. FinanceDashboardPage.tsx:46-48 'Collect fee' navigates to /finance/collections — the read-only list itself. Backend POST /finance/collections exists with idempotency (finance_router.ts:221-223); Flutter has the full dialog (finance_workflow_actions.dart:720-811).
- **Impact:** The counter journey (200 transactions/day in peak season) cannot even START on web. Click count to collect a payment: infinite.
- **Recommendation:** Row-level and global 'Collect fee' opening a drawer seeded from the student account balance, exactly as the Flutter dialog does; keyboard-first (autofocus search, Enter to submit).

### F-209 · [P0] · **verified: P1** (see Part 1 V-45) — Student Accounts page calls a nonexistent endpoint and rows are dead — 'find student fast' impossible on web
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance/student-accounts · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** StudentAccountsPage.tsx:8 fetches GET /finance/student-accounts (list). finance_router.ts has only /finance/student-accounts/{id} (213-216) and /{id}/ledger (207-211) — no list route, so live returns 404 → ErrorState. The page also defines no onRowClick (whole file, lines 35-51) and no collect/ledger action. Flutter builds this list by composing fee-assignments + per-student fetches (api_finance_repository.dart:88-104).
- **Impact:** The primary student-lookup surface for counter work errors out on live; even if it loaded, a found student is a dead end — no ledger view, no outstanding drill-down, no collect action. The parent-at-the-counter journey dies at step 1.
- **Recommendation:** Add the list endpoint (or compose like Flutter), make rows open a student fee ledger (backend /ledger already exists and is 'printable'), with a prominent Collect action.

### F-210 · [P0] — Refund maker-checker cannot be completed: Approve/Reject permanently disabled and bound to nothing
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance/refunds · **Role:** accountant (checker)
- **Evidence:** RefundsPage.tsx:39-46 renders <Button disabled>Reject</Button> / <Button disabled>Approve</Button> below the table, with no row selection mechanism anywhere in the file. Backend approve/reject endpoints exist: finance_router.ts:292-300. The page subtitle even promises 'Refund requests — maker/checker' (RefundsPage.tsx:26).
- **Impact:** Governed refund reversal — the persona's mistake-recovery path — is view-only. A checker cannot check. Money reversibility requirement fails on web.
- **Recommendation:** Row selection or row detail drawer with Approve/Reject + reason, posting to /finance/refunds/{id}/approve|reject.

### F-211 · [P0] — Fake success: 'Save changes' on settings shows 'Settings saved' toast without calling the backend
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** all module settings (incl. finance) · **Role:** accountant/admin
- **Evidence:** ModuleSettingsPage.tsx:57: onClick={() => { setEdits({}); toast.success('Settings saved'); }} — no apiFetch, while PUT /finance/settings exists (finance_router.ts:415) and GET loads real sections (finance_settings_repository.ts:164-171). Generic component, so every module's settings page on web fakes saves.
- **Impact:** The single most dangerous defect found: a finance admin changing fee/receipt/reconciliation configuration gets positive confirmation while the change is silently discarded. Violates the product's own honest-state doctrine and would destroy trust the day it is discovered.
- **Recommendation:** Wire the PUT, show optimistic-save with server confirmation, and disable the button during flight. Until then, remove the toast — an honest dead button is safer than a lying live one.

### F-212 · [P0] — Month-end reports and exports are switched off across the web ERP
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance/reports · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** ReportsHub.tsx:25 'ready = false' default; :51-57 Preview/Export rendered disabled; :61-64 footer 'Report generation activates once the module's export endpoint is connected.' FinanceReportsPage.tsx:4-21 never passes ready. No CSV/XLSX/Tally export exists anywhere in web/src. Flutter exports mode-wise summary and transaction-level CSV (finance_collections_screen.dart:268-277,302-330).
- **Impact:** Month-end journey (revenue reports, Excel/Tally handoff to the auditor) is impossible on the desktop — the one platform where an auditor handoff would happen. The accountant would export from a phone or not at all.
- **Recommendation:** Ship daily-collection and dues CSV export first (endpoints /finance/collections/daily-summary and /finance/analytics/head-wise-dues already return the data); add date-range parameters server-side.

### F-213 · [P0] — Four of fourteen finance tabs render permanently empty on live due to response-shape mismatches
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance (reconciliation, executive, copilot, discounts) · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** Reconciliation and Executive use KpiPage which requires {kpis:[]} and shows empty otherwise (KpiPage.tsx:50); /finance/collections/daily-summary returns flat fields (finance_mapper.ts:303-318) and /finance/intelligence/executive returns FinanceExecutiveSnapshot with no kpis (finance_intelligence_service.ts:36-43). Copilot page expects {items:[{title,detail}]} (FinanceExtraPages.tsx:72) but backend returns FinanceCopilotSnapshot (service.ts:26-34). DiscountsPage fetches a list (DiscountsPage.tsx:8) but GET /finance/discounts returns {kpis, scholarships, rules, assignments:[]} (finance_discounts_handlers.ts:71-98) — fetchList (list.ts:19-25) finds no array and yields 0 rows forever.
- **Impact:** Morning reconciliation tab shows 'Nothing to show yet' even with a full day of collections in the DB; concessions oversight shows an empty table while scholarships exist. Silent wrongness — worse than an error, because it looks like clean data.
- **Recommendation:** Contract tests between web pages and serializers; project daily-summary flat fields into KPI cards client-side (the dashboard page already does exactly this pattern at FinanceDashboardPage.tsx:21-28).

### F-214 · [P0] — Offline/failed payment resolution impossible on web: no reconcile/bounce actions
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance/offline-payments · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** OfflinePaymentsPage (FinanceExtraPages.tsx:34-47) is a bare list with a dead 'Record payment' button and no row actions; status chips include 'bounced' but nothing can be done about one. Backend: POST /finance/payments/offline/{id}/reconcile and /{id}/bounce exist (finance_router.ts:327-334). Flutter has both actions with segmented Pending/Reconciled/Bounced views (finance_offline_payments_screen.dart:38-39,219-238).
- **Impact:** The 'a failed payment appears — resolve it' journey dead-ends. A bounced cheque on web can be seen but never resolved; the pendingReconciliation count can never go down.
- **Recommendation:** Row actions: Reconcile (with reference confirm) and Mark bounced (with reason), matching the Flutter interaction.

### F-215 · [P1] — Yesterday cannot be reconciled anywhere: daily summary is hardwired to CURRENT_DATE with no date parameter
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** finance/reconciliation · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** finance_collections_repository.ts:820-832 — SQL filters collection_date = CURRENT_DATE; handleDailySummary (finance_collections_handlers.ts:347-371) parses no date query param. Neither web ReconciliationPage nor Flutter collections KPI row offers a date picker.
- **Impact:** The universal accountant morning ritual — verify yesterday's cash/UPI/cheque against the counterfoils before banking — has no supporting view on any platform. Only same-day totals exist; after midnight the evidence window closes.
- **Recommendation:** Accept ?date= on daily-summary and add a date selector; day-close entries should render a historical day-wise register.

### F-216 · [P1] — Mode split omits cheque/DD/card: cash + UPI do not sum to the total, with no 'other' bucket
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** finance/reconciliation · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** finance_collections_repository.ts:823-828 computes only LOWER(payment_method)='cash' and ='upi'; the offline-payments subsystem explicitly supports cheque/DD/PDC instruments (finance_router.ts:332-334, bounce reasons in Flutter screen:178-183). dailySummaryToApi (finance_mapper.ts:314-315) exposes only cashAmount and upiAmount.
- **Impact:** On any day with a cheque payment, the visible mode figures do not tie to the total — the exact discrepancy a reconciliation view exists to catch. An accountant will assume missing money.
- **Recommendation:** GROUP BY payment_method server-side and render every mode plus a computed check-total row.

### F-217 · [P1] — Money amounts rendered as bare unformatted strings: no ₹, no Indian grouping, paise stripped on integers
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance (all tables) · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** Backend formatAmount returns '45000' or '45000.5'-style strings (finance_mapper.ts:70-74); web tables render them raw (CollectionsPage.tsx:32 {r.amount}, StudentAccountsPage.tsx:29-31, DefaultersPage.tsx:25). Meanwhile the dashboard formats numerics properly with formatCurrency/formatCompactCurrency (FinanceDashboardPage.tsx:23-26,89) — two inconsistent money styles in the same module. Flutter defaulters shows '₹${entry.outstanding}' by manual prefix (defaulters screen:633).
- **Impact:** In a 200-row collections grid, '45000' vs '4500' misreads are how counting errors happen; the persona's 'every amount visible with paise' rule fails everywhere amounts are strings.
- **Recommendation:** Send integer paise (or decimal) from the API and format exclusively client-side with en-IN currency + always two decimals in money-critical tables.

### F-218 · [P1] — Defaulter follow-up on web is a bare list: no reminder, no contact, no class filter, no bulk anything
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance/defaulters · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** DefaultersPage.tsx:39-55 — only name search and aging-bucket filter; guardianPhone rendered as plain monospaced text (line 36), not even a tel: link; no row or bulk actions. Backend recovery suite exists: call-queue, contacts, promises, targets (finance_router.ts:354-386). Flutter has per-row WhatsApp + log-contact + promise-to-pay (defaulters screen:640-680) but no BULK send on any platform.
- **Impact:** Peak-season journey 'filter by class, send reminders in bulk' is impossible on web and manual-per-row on mobile. Chasing 150 defaulters one WhatsApp at a time is a full day's work.
- **Recommendation:** Class filter + multi-select + bulk reminder via the communication module; surface the existing call queue on web.

### F-219 · [P1] — No receipt print/share exists anywhere on web; parent-side 'Download PDF' is a dead button
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance/collections + parent receipts · **Role:** accountant / parent
- **Evidence:** CollectionDetailPage (DetailPages.tsx:120-130) renders payment info with no print/share/download action at all. ReceiptDetailPage 'Download PDF' (DetailPages.tsx:154) has no onClick. Flutter: full PDF build/print/share + audited duplicate reprint (finance_receipt_pdf_service.dart:218-256).
- **Impact:** Counter flow ends without a receipt — the one artifact the parent came for. Even a completed historical payment cannot be re-issued from the desk.
- **Recommendation:** Server-rendered receipt PDF (ledger endpoint already exists as printable precedent) + window.print stylesheet as an interim.

### F-220 · [P1] — 200-row fetch cap with no pagination controls and no date filter on Collections
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance/collections · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** CollectionsPage.tsx:10 hardcodes pageSize: 200; ResourceList paginates nothing — it client-filters the single fetched page and shows 'X of {data.total}' (ResourceList.tsx:76-108) with no page controls; backend supports page/pageSize (finance_collections_handlers.ts:191). No date filter exists on the page (filters = status only, line 48-50).
- **Impact:** At the persona's own volume the list truncates within one working day; search silently searches only the loaded 200, so a receipt lookup can falsely return 'nothing matches'.
- **Recommendation:** Server pagination controls + date-range filter + receipt-number server search.

### F-221 · [P1] — No keyboard path for counter work; ⌘K palette searches nav labels only; table rows mouse-only
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** shell/finance · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** CommandPalette.tsx:18-29 — items are nav entries only ('Ports the copilot universal search intent' but matches i.label of nav items); no entity (student/receipt) search. DataTable rows are <tr onClick> with no tabIndex/keyboard handler (DataTable.tsx:102-110). No other shortcuts exist in web/src outside dialog Esc handling.
- **Impact:** 'Can you do it without touching the mouse?' — No. Even in a future where collect exists, every transaction costs full mouse round-trips; at 200/day that is the difference between a 30-second and 2-minute transaction.
- **Recommendation:** ⌘K should search students/receipts (SIS + finance endpoints); make rows focusable with Enter-to-open; add a global 'C' collect shortcut.

### F-222 · [P1] — 14-tab finance strip mixes daily tools with executive/AI surfaces; orphan fee-assignment route unreachable from nav
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance IA · **Role:** accountant
- **Evidence:** navConfig.ts:244-258 — 14 tabs (Overview…Settings) including 'Executive' and 'Copilot' as peers of Collections; ModuleTabs is a plain overflow-x-auto strip with no overflow indicator (ModuleTabs.tsx:13). /finance/fee-assignment is routed (router.tsx:409) but absent from MODULE_TABS and all nav — reachable only by typing the URL.
- **Impact:** Cognitive overload for the daily user and hidden functionality: the fee-assignment page (part of next-year setup) is effectively invisible; tabs past ~10 scroll out of view with no affordance on 1366px laptops.
- **Recommendation:** Group tabs (Collect | Accounts | Recovery | Setup | Reports | More); move Executive/Copilot under an Insights group; add fee-assignment to Setup.

### F-223 · [P1] — Flutter collect dialog opened from Collections is invoice-centric: picker labels have no student name, default 'inv_1'
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** finance/collections · **Role:** accountant/cashier
- **Evidence:** finance_workflow_actions.dart:723 defaultInvoiceId = 'inv_1' hardcoded fallback; :734-738 picker labels are '${invoiceNumber} · ${termLabel} · ${outstanding} due' — no student identity. The well-seeded path exists only via student accounts search (student_accounts screen:267-277).
- **Impact:** A cashier tapping the primary 'Record collection' button on the Collections screen must pick from anonymous invoice numbers — selecting the wrong student's invoice is one mis-tap away, on a money write.
- **Recommendation:** Make the student the first-class selector in the dialog (search-as-you-type), show student name in the invoice picker labels, and drop the 'inv_1' magic default.

### F-224 · [P2] — Web test suite asserts only that page titles render — parity/cert evidence was reachability, not usability
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** finance QA · **Role:** review-board note
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/finance/finance.test.tsx — every test is renderWithProviders + expect(getByText(title)); nothing exercises a click, mutation, or contract shape. This is how the dead buttons, fake save, and shape mismatches survived a '229/229 routes' live certification.
- **Impact:** Certification theater: the gap between 'route renders' and 'task completes' is exactly where all P0s above live; future waves will regress silently under the same tests.
- **Recommendation:** Add journey-level tests (collect → receipt; approve refund) and API-contract tests pinning page expectations to serializer output.
