## Reviewer: Front Office Panel — Receptionist / Office Clerk / Librarian (with Transport spot-check)  (`journey-frontoffice`)
**Vote:** NOT CERTIFIED · **Scores:** overall 4, workflowEfficiency 3.5, mobile 5, web 2.5, visualDesign 7, maturity 4

**Independent executive assessment:** I audited Akshara ERP from the front desk: capturing a walk-in enquiry while the phone rings, issuing a bonafide/transfer certificate, updating a family's contact details, and running a library counter (issue, return, overdue, fines), plus a transport spot-check. The information architecture is genuinely good on mobile — the USER→ROLE→WORKSPACE→TASK model is real code, not a slogan: a librarian's phone shows only the Library module, the out-of-scope verticals (salon/restaurant/healthcare/white-label) are correctly hidden by SchoolBuildScope at both nav and route level, and the SIS registry search, TC no-dues gate, and duplicate-phone lead check are exactly the right shapes for these jobs.

But every one of my three personas hits a dead end on a daily task. The librarian's issue/return dialogs can only see the currently loaded 20-row page of members/books/loans — scanning a real book's ISBN fails with "No available book with ISBN X" for any catalog beyond one page — and fine collection offers only "Waive" plus a button literally labeled "Finance FN-02" that lands the librarian on an Access Denied screen. The receptionist's create-lead flow shows "Lead created successfully ()" even when creation failed, with zero field validation. The clerk cannot update a student's address at all (displayed, never editable), cannot attach a real document (no file picker exists in the app — uploads are filename text), and cannot issue certificates without being granted full schoolAdmin (14-module hub), because no front-office role carries manageSis. On the web — the natural device for a front desk — all of these workflows are read-only: "New lead", "Issue book", "Return book", "Add student", "Edit" are enabled-looking buttons with no onClick at all. The chrome is world-class; the counter workflows underneath are not yet trustworthy, so I cannot certify.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Hide-first workspace model is genuinely implemented on mobile: hub grid and bottom nav are workspace-scoped (lib/features/admin/screens/admin_hub_screen.dart:28, lib/features/admin/admin_bottom_nav.dart:47), so a librarian or counselor sees a small, obvious surface, not the full ERP.
- Out-of-scope verticals (salon, restaurant, healthcare, accommodation, industry, white-label) are correctly unreachable — hidden from nav AND route-prefix blocked in lib/core/config/school_build_scope.dart:22-41,54-57. The owner's scope decision is honored in code.
- Create-lead dialog is right-sized for a 2-minute walk-in capture: 4 fields plus a warn-only duplicate-phone check with an 'Open existing' shortcut (lib/features/admissions/leads/widgets/admissions_create_lead_dialog.dart:29-32,151-157).
- TC issuance has a real no-dues gate with the outstanding amount surfaced verbatim from the 409, and certificates render straight to a printable PDF (lib/features/sis/sis_workflow_actions.dart:282-373).
- SIS registry has a debounced student/admission-number search with clean empty states (lib/features/sis/registry/sis_registry_screen.dart:245-257).
- Library module design intent is strong: ISBN-first issue/return dialogs, renew-only-active rule, circulation guardrail settings, audit-logged fine waivers, overdue reminders and CSV/PDF exports (lib/features/library/library_workflow_actions.dart, lib/features/library/overdue/library_overdue_screen.dart:85-113).
- Consistent async discipline everywhere: loading/error/empty states with retry, status chips, Semantics labels on tables and cards (e.g. lib/features/library/issues/library_issues_screen.dart:151-153,212), and dark-theme goldens confirm coherent theming (test/golden/goldens/dark_admin_hub_834x1194.png).
- Transport stop editor supports add/reorder/edit/remove plus roster PDF/CSV export in one dialog (lib/features/transport/routes/transport_stop_editor.dart:65-86), and the GPS tracking placeholder is honestly labeled with a real 'Notify parents of delay' action (lib/features/transport/tracking/transport_tracking_screen.dart:96-129).

**Findings:**

### F-225 · [P0] — Library issue/return dialogs only see the current 20-row page — ISBN scan fails for most of a real catalog
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** library · **Role:** Librarian
- **Evidence:** lib/features/library/library_workflow_actions.dart:37-40 reads libraryMembersProvider and libraryCatalogProvider for the dialog dropdowns; those providers return only the current page items (lib/features/library/library_providers.dart:65-67 and 207-209, query built with withPage at :47-51 and :189-193; kDefaultRepositoryPageSize = 20 in lib/core/repositories/paginated_result.dart:98). The ISBN resolver matches only within that page (library_workflow_actions.dart:73-76 issue, :219-222 return) and otherwise shows 'No available book with ISBN X'. Return dialog open-loans list is likewise page-1 only (:193).
- **Impact:** The librarian's core counter workflow breaks at any real scale: with the school's own advertised '8,450 titles / 1,200 students', a member past row 20 cannot be issued a book, and scanning/typing the barcode of a physical book in hand fails unless that book happens to be on the loaded page. Same for returns.
- **Recommendation:** Resolve member/book/loan by querying the backend (search-as-you-type and GET-by-ISBN endpoints), never from the paginated page cache; make the ISBN field the primary path with server-side lookup.

### F-226 · [P0] — Fine collection is impossible: only 'Waive' exists, and the escape hatch is a 'Finance FN-02' button that lands on Access Denied
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** library · **Role:** Librarian
- **Evidence:** lib/features/library/fines/library_fines_screen.dart:128 and 206-223 — the only per-fine action is Waive (waiveLibraryFine, library_workflow_actions.dart:665-706). The screen's only other affordance is an OutlinedButton labeled 'Finance FN-02' (:44-51) that context.go's to RouteNames.financeFeeStructures, which requires Permission.viewFinance (lib/router/route_guards.dart:29); the librarian role holds only viewAdminHub/viewLibrary/manageLibrary (lib/core/security/role_permissions.dart:597-601), so PermissionGuard renders AccessDeniedScreen (route_guards.dart:308-327).
- **Impact:** A student pays a Rs.10 fine in cash at the counter and the librarian has no way to record it — the only actions are waiving it (audit-logged as a waiver, i.e. wrong) or leaving it pending. The one visible button dead-ends on an Access Denied screen and leaks an internal spec ID ('FN-02') into the UI.
- **Recommendation:** Add a 'Collect fine' / 'Mark paid' action in the library module (or a clean hand-off that posts the fine to the student's finance account), and remove or re-scope the finance deep-link for roles without viewFinance.

### F-227 · [P0] — Lead creation shows 'Lead created successfully ()' even when creation failed, and accepts fully empty submissions
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admissions · **Role:** Receptionist
- **Evidence:** lib/features/admissions/admissions_workflow_actions.dart:37-53 — createLeadProvider.execute uses AsyncValue.guard and returns state.valueOrNull (lib/features/admissions/admissions_mutations_provider.dart:99-118), so failures return null without throwing; the success snackbar at :49-53 fires unconditionally with 'Lead created successfully (${lead?.id ?? ''})'. The dialog itself pops with whatever is typed — 'Create' has no validation (lib/features/admissions/leads/widgets/admissions_create_lead_dialog.dart:166-177), so empty parent/student/phone submits.
- **Impact:** The receptionist's #1 task silently loses walk-in enquiries: a rejected/failed create (likely, given empty fields are allowed) is confirmed as success, the family is never followed up, and in a sales demo the visible 'Lead created successfully ()' with an empty ID is an obvious defect. The identical null-return false-success bug was already found and fixed in the SIS edit sheet (RT-26 comment in sis_profile_edit_sheet.dart:215-217) but not here.
- **Recommendation:** Require name+phone before enabling Create; on null result show the mutation's error and keep the dialog open — mirror the RT-26 fix.

### F-228 · [P0] — Web ERP is read-only for every front-office workflow — primary buttons ('New lead', 'Issue book', 'Return book', 'Add student', 'Edit') have no onClick
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** admissions / library / sis · **Role:** Receptionist / Office Clerk / Librarian
- **Evidence:** web/src/pages/admissions/LeadsPage.tsx:61-64 ('Import CSV', 'New lead') and :139-142 ('Log call', 'Advance stage'); web/src/pages/library/IssuesPage.tsx:32 ('Issue book') and :52 ('Send reminders' explicitly disabled); web/src/pages/library/ReturnsPage.tsx:32 ('Return book'); web/src/pages/sis/StudentRegistryPage.tsx:66-71 ('Import CSV', 'Add student'); web/src/pages/detail/DetailPages.tsx:81 ('Edit'), :154 ('Download PDF'), :184 ('Send') — none pass onClick, and Button is a plain <button> that does nothing without one (web/src/components/ui/Button.tsx:37-57). Library fines page similarly has no collect/waive handlers.
- **Impact:** The front desk's natural device is a desktop. On web, the receptionist cannot capture a lead, the clerk cannot edit a student or issue anything, and the librarian cannot circulate a single book — yet every one of these renders as an enabled, primary-styled button that swallows the click with zero feedback. This is worse than absent: it looks complete and does nothing, which is demo-fatal and trust-destroying.
- **Recommendation:** Either wire these actions (backend endpoints exist — the Flutter app calls them) or render them visibly disabled with a 'view-only on web' explanation until parity lands.

### F-229 · [P1] — No role below full school admin can issue certificates — the office clerk persona forces hide-first to collapse
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** sis / roles · **Role:** Office Clerk
- **Evidence:** Certificate issuance requires manageSis (issueCertificateProvider gated; only superAdmin/schoolAdmin/principal/vicePrincipal hold it — lib/core/security/role_permissions.dart:44,188,309,412). admissionsCounselor gets only viewSis (:583), and the Front Office workspace contains only admissions+marketing modules (lib/core/workspace/workspace.dart:104-112). The schoolAdministration workspace a clerk must be given shows ~14 module cards (test/golden/goldens/dark_admin_hub_834x1194.png) and full finance/HR/exam-publish permissions (role_permissions.dart:177-298).
- **Impact:** Bonafide/TC printing is the single most common Indian school front-desk task. Today the school must either give the desk clerk the keys to the whole ERP (14 modules, manageFinance, approveRefunds — a least-privilege and hide-first failure) or route every certificate to the principal. Both defeat the 'easiest school ERP' promise for this persona.
- **Recommendation:** Add an officeClerk/frontOffice role (or extend admissionsCounselor) with viewSis+manageSis-scoped-to-certificates, and put SIS registry+certificates into the Front Office workspace.

### F-230 · [P1] — Student address is displayed but not editable anywhere — a routine clerk task is impossible
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** sis · **Role:** Office Clerk
- **Evidence:** Profile shows parent.address (lib/features/sis/profile/sis_profile_screen.dart:390; model field lib/features/sis/sis_models.dart:179,186), but the edit sheet's fields are name/admission/class/section/year/gender/dob/guardian/phone/email only (lib/features/sis/profile/sis_profile_edit_sheet.dart:114-130) and UpdateStudentRequest has no address member (lib/features/sis/sis_requests.dart:33-57). Web detail page 'Edit' is a dead button (web/src/pages/detail/DetailPages.tsx:81).
- **Impact:** Families move; 'update the address' is a weekly front-office request. The clerk sees the stale address on screen and has no path to correct it on any platform — a visible-but-uneditable field reads as a bug to end users.
- **Recommendation:** Add address to the edit sheet and UpdateStudentRequest (backend permitting), or hide fields that cannot be maintained.

### F-231 · [P1] — Document 'upload' collects only a typed filename — no file picker exists anywhere in the app
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** sis · **Role:** Office Clerk
- **Evidence:** showSisDocumentUploadDialog uploads only type + fileName text, prefilled 'transfer_certificate.pdf' (lib/features/sis/sis_workflow_actions.dart:28-92). pubspec.yaml contains no file_picker/image_picker, and the library CSV importer's own comment confirms 'there is no file_picker in the app, staff paste CSV text' (lib/features/library/library_workflow_actions.dart:854).
- **Impact:** The clerk's 'handle a document request' journey is metadata theater: the system records that a TC/aadhaar/birth-certificate exists without ever holding the document. Verify/reject flows (sis_workflow_actions.dart:96-172) then adjudicate documents nobody can open. Schools will keep their paper files and distrust the module.
- **Recommendation:** Integrate a real picker + storage upload, or relabel the flow honestly as a physical-document checklist.

### F-232 · [P1] — All admissions filter chips are decorative — selected filter never reaches any query
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admissions · **Role:** Receptionist
- **Evidence:** admissionsLeadsFilterProvider is set by chips but the data future uses only page (lib/features/admissions/leads/admissions_leads_provider.dart:14,22-33). Repo-wide grep shows the four admissions filter providers (dashboard/leads/applications/documents) are watched only by their screens for chip highlight — never by a provider that filters data — unlike library/transport/hr which do consume theirs (lib/features/library/library_providers.dart:72,120 etc.). Chip labels are 'All sources / All stages / All scores' (admissions_leads_screen.dart:26-30), which as a single-select row is semantically meaningless anyway.
- **Impact:** The receptionist taps 'All stages' expecting to narrow the pipeline and nothing changes; the CRM list is effectively browse-only. Fake controls in the top bar of the flagship CRM module erode trust in every other control.
- **Recommendation:** Wire real stage/source filters into the leads query (backend already supports filtering elsewhere) or remove the chip bar until it does something.

### F-233 · [P1] — No lead search on the Leads screen — phone follow-ups require paging 20 rows at a time
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** admissions · **Role:** Receptionist
- **Evidence:** AdmissionsLeadsScreen renders table + pagination only (lib/features/admissions/leads/admissions_leads_screen.dart:57-145); grep across lib/features/admissions shows the only lead search field lives inside the applications lead-picker dialog (applications/widgets/admissions_lead_picker_dialog.dart:77-78). SIS registry proves the pattern exists in-house (sis_registry_screen.dart:245-257).
- **Impact:** 'I enquired last week about my son' is the receptionist's most common inbound call. With no name/phone search and dead filter chips, she pages through the pipeline 20 leads at a time while the caller waits. The duplicate-check-by-phone endpoint already exists (create dialog uses it) but isn't exposed as search.
- **Recommendation:** Add the same debounced search bar the SIS registry has, querying name/phone.

### F-234 · [P1] — Specialist roles land on the generic Admin Hub instead of their own workspace dashboard, greeted by hardcoded fake stats
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** auth / admin hub · **Role:** Receptionist / Librarian / Transport
- **Evidence:** homeRouteForStaffErp special-cases only finance/inventory/principal/VP/superAdmin and defaults every other role to RouteNames.admin (lib/features/auth/qa_login_persona.dart:207-216), ignoring the workspace catalog's declared homeRoutes (lib/core/workspace/workspace.dart:104-148: frontOffice→admissionsDashboard, library→libraryDashboard). The hub hero then renders hardcoded 'curated demo figures' — '23 Open enquiries', '9 Visitors today', '7 Overdue', 'Rs.4.2L Collected today' — admitted as fake in lib/features/admin/workspace_landing_config.dart:9-12 and rendered at admin_hub_screen.dart:58-63. There is no visitor feature in Front Office at all (visitor code exists only in hostel), so the '9 Visitors today' stat advertises a nonexistent capability.
- **Impact:** Every login, the librarian/receptionist gets an extra tap through a page whose headline numbers are fiction that never changes. A librarian who trusts '7 Overdue' acts on wrong data; a buyer who spots that the number never moves distrusts the entire product.
- **Recommendation:** Land each role on its workspace homeRoute, and either bind hero stats to real summary endpoints or remove them.

### F-235 · [P2] — Tablet rail and 'More' drawer ignore workspace scoping — same user sees different module sets per device
- **Platform:** tablet · **Module:** navigation · **Role:** All staff
- **Evidence:** AdminNavigationRail (rail and inDrawer variants) watches unscoped adminNavDestinationsProvider (lib/features/admin/admin_navigation_rail.dart:56), while bottom nav and hub grid use workspaceScopedNavDestinationsProvider (admin_bottom_nav.dart:47, admin_hub_screen.dart:28; scoping defined in admin_navigation_provider.dart:272-284). An admissionsCounselor (viewSis granted, role_permissions.dart:583) therefore sees 'Student SIS' in the tablet rail but not in the phone bottom nav; multi-hat users see all hats merged in the rail but only the active hat below.
- **Impact:** The hide-first model silently varies by form factor; users switching phone→tablet find modules appearing/disappearing, and the workspace switcher's meaning is undermined on desktop-class layouts.
- **Recommendation:** Use the workspace-scoped provider in the rail/drawer, with the workspace switcher as the way to see other hats.

### F-236 · [P2] — 'Scan ISBN' button promises camera scanning but opens a manual dropdown/typing dialog
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** library · **Role:** Librarian
- **Evidence:** Primary action on Issues is FilledButton 'Scan ISBN' with a qr_code_scanner icon (lib/features/library/issues/library_issues_screen.dart:48-52); the dialog it opens has two dropdowns and a text field whose own comment admits 'camera Scan button…device-gated (staged seam — no camera package)' (library_workflow_actions.dart:121-124).
- **Impact:** The librarian taps expecting the camera and gets a form; with a hardware barcode gun the flow works only via the text field (and then hits the page-1 resolution bug). Mislabeled primary actions cost trust at the counter.
- **Recommendation:** Rename to 'Issue book' until camera scanning ships; keep the ISBN field as the wedge for USB/BT scanners.

### F-237 · [P2] — Receptionist has no visitor log or message-to-staff capability despite the 'Front Office' framing
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** front office / communication · **Role:** Receptionist
- **Evidence:** Front Office workspace = admissions+marketing only (lib/core/workspace/workspace.dart:104-112). Visitor management exists solely in the hostel module (grep: visitor code only under hostel repositories/screens). The only communication surface is broadcast_admin_screen gated on manageCommunication (lib/router/route_guards.dart:85), held by admin/principal roles only — the counselor has no messaging permission (role_permissions.dart:579-586).
- **Impact:** Two of the receptionist's three daily jobs (visitor/gate-pass handling, taking a telephone message for a teacher) have no home in the product — while the workspace's own hero card advertises '9 Visitors today'. The desk stays on paper registers and personal WhatsApp.
- **Recommendation:** Ship a minimal front-office visitor log and a simple internal note/message-to-staff action, or stop advertising visitors on the landing hero.

### F-238 · [P2] — Student profile edit uses unvalidated free-text for DOB, gender, class and section
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** sis · **Role:** Office Clerk
- **Evidence:** All fields are plain TextFields with maxLength 1000 and no pickers/validators (lib/features/sis/profile/sis_profile_edit_sheet.dart:114-130,155-166) — 'Date of birth' and 'Gender' accept anything; class/section are unconstrained strings.
- **Impact:** Front-desk data entry drifts ('5th', 'V', 'Class 5'), corrupting class rosters, certificates (which print these values), and downstream reports; a mistyped DOB ends up on a legal TC document.
- **Recommendation:** Date picker for DOB, dropdowns for gender/class/section sourced from academic config.

---

## Reviewer: Competitive Product Analyst  (`competitive-analyst`)
**Vote:** CERTIFIED — moderate improvements required · **Scores:** overall 6, maturity 6

**Independent executive assessment:** I benchmarked Akshara against the five ERPs most relevant to Indian K-12 — Teachmint, Fedena, Entab CampusCare, MyClassboard, and NextERP (Next Education) — using review-site and app-store signals (Capterra/G2/Play Store via web research: capterra.com/p/163105/Teachmint, g2.com/products/fedena, play.google.com com.campuscare.entab.ui, softwaresuggest.com/myclassboard, nexterp.in, axoneura.in UPI-AutoPay comparison), then ground-truthed Akshara by tracing its routers, guards, 6 golden screenshots, and the fee-payment, attendance, and parent-dashboard journeys end to end. The market context is striking: incumbents' parent apps are their weakest surface (Entab ~3.3/5 across 123k reviews, MyClassboard iOS ~2.5/5, Fedena criticized as non-intuitive, Teachmint discontinued its fee module) — and Akshara's UX architecture (4-tab + More persona shells, child-switcher chip, parent Action Inbox, exception-first teacher attendance with draft autosave, full dark parity, consistent loading/empty/error/success components) is genuinely ahead of every incumbent I researched. The feared out-of-scope verticals (salon/restaurant/healthcare/white-label) are correctly hidden in the school build by three redundant layers (SchoolBuildScope, ErpRouteGuard, backend-less surface gate) — not a defect in the shipping configuration.

However, Akshara loses exactly where the Indian market's buying decision is made: money and messaging. The parent online fee payment — the single most important parent journey and the incumbents' most-complained-about flow — cannot genuinely complete: the Flutter client has zero gateway (Razorpay) integration, fabricates its transaction reference, and the backend fail-closes live confirms without a gateway signature, so the polished UPI/card selector ends in either a fabricated success ceremony (stub mode) or a guaranteed failure (live mode). A second P0 is a plain navigation dead end: every parent's "More" sheet offers a PTM tile that lands on a full-screen "Access Denied." Supporting P1s include a hardcoded 'term_2' installment on both Pay Now CTAs, a fabricated "Ravi Kumar 8-A" fallback on the money screen, "staging server" copy on the production login screen, WhatsApp missing from the core broadcast pipeline despite an admin WhatsApp-provider setup screen, and no parent-facing online admission form. These are completion and wiring gaps, not design flaws — the underlying UX system needs finishing, not redesign. Honest limits: golden screenshots render text as blocks and I could not run the app, so runtime feel, latency, real OTP delivery, and most screen copy are unverifiable from code inspection.

**Strengths observed (context for what must not regress):**
- Teacher attendance marking is best-in-class for the Indian market: exception-first dense grid, All-present/All-absent, 'Fill remaining present', roll/name search, draft autosave with resume, post-submit correction request, CSV/PDF export, success haptics (lib/features/teacher/attendance/teacher_attendance_screen.dart:280-314,350-379) — faster than the list-of-toggles pattern in Teachmint/Fedena/Entab.
- Parent IA beats incumbents: 4 primary tabs + curated More sheet (lib/features/parent/shell/parent_shell.dart:27-110), child-switcher chip in the app bar for multi-child families (lib/features/parent/dashboard/parent_dashboard_screen.dart:54-58), consolidated 'Action Needed' inbox and family view — no researched competitor has an action-inbox pattern.
- Out-of-scope verticals are properly invisible in the shipping school build via three independent layers: SchoolBuildScope hidden modules/prefixes (lib/core/config/school_build_scope.dart:22-84), ErpRouteGuard deep-link blocking (lib/router/route_guards.dart:409-439), and the backend-less surface gate (lib/router/surface_backend_gate.dart:22-55).
- Mature design system: 30+ shared akshara_* components with consistent loading/empty/error/success states and Semantics labels across 26 shared widget files (lib/shared/widgets/), plus money-screen a11y labels (lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_screen.dart:206-209,300-303).
- Full dark-theme parity — dark goldens mirror light layouts 1:1 with correct surfaces and preserved status colors (test/golden/goldens/dark_parent_dashboard_390x844.png vs parent_dashboard_390x844.png); no researched Indian incumbent ships dark mode.
- Plan-gating UX is sophisticated: plan-locked modules show locked + upgrade instead of vanishing, school-disabled modules hide (lib/features/admin/admin_navigation_provider.dart:241-262) — correct hide-first vs upsell distinction.
- Clean parent login: single +91 mobile field, digits-only, OTP, school branding, clearly separated 'Staff ERP portal' path (lib/features/auth/login_screen.dart:188-276).

**Findings:**

### F-239 · [P0] — Parent online fee payment cannot genuinely complete — no gateway integration in client; success is fabricated in stub mode, guaranteed failure in live mode
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/fees-payment · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_provider.dart:139 fabricates the gateway reference ('txn_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}') and sends no razorpay_payment_id/signature; grep for 'razorpay' across lib/ returns zero files (no checkout SDK); backend supabase/functions/_shared/payment/payment_service.ts:219-232 fail-closes: live (non-stub) confirm REQUIRES a verified Razorpay payment id + signature the app can never supply. UI offers UPI/card selector and a full success ceremony with receipt number (parent_payment_screen.dart:100-116,338-347). Matches tracked owner dependency P0-02, but from the UX lens the shipped flow either fakes a payment success or always errors. Competitor context: online fee payment is the most-marketed parent feature of Entab/MyClassboard/NextERP (entab.in, myclassboard.com, nexterp.in).
- **Impact:** The #1 parent journey and the primary competitive battleground is a dead end: a parent selects UPI, taps 'Pay now', and either sees a fabricated 'Payment successful' with a receipt while no money moved (trust catastrophe, potential dispute) or a permanent 'Payment could not be completed'. Unsellable in a demo against any incumbent.
- **Recommendation:** Integrate the Razorpay (or chosen gateway) checkout SDK in the client, pass payment id + signature to confirm, and until credentials exist, replace the UPI/card options with an honest 'online payment coming soon — pay at school / via QR at counter' state instead of a submittable mock flow.

### F-240 · [P0] — Parent 'More' sheet lists a PTM tile that dead-ends on a full-screen 'Access Denied'
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/navigation · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent/shell/parent_shell.dart:94-98 puts PTM in the More sheet; RouteNames.parentPtm is scope-hidden because PTM has no backend (lib/core/config/school_build_scope.dart:78-81); the route builder returns AccessDeniedScreen (lib/router/app_router.dart:2610-2615, lock icon + 'Access Denied' per route_guards.dart:247-260); the More sheet renders spec.more with NO SchoolBuildScope filter (lib/shared/navigation/persona_nav.dart:197-216).
- **Impact:** Every parent in the shipping build can tap an offered tile and be told 'Access Denied' — reads as a broken app or a permissions insult, on the persona whose trust the product depends on. Instantly demo-embarrassing.
- **Recommendation:** Filter More-sheet destinations through SchoolBuildScope.isRouteHidden (same predicate the route builder uses) so hidden features never render a tile; also replace parent-facing AccessDeniedScreen fallbacks with a friendlier 'not available for your school' state.

### F-241 · [P1] — Both Pay Now CTAs hardcode installment 'term_2' regardless of what is actually due
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/fees-payment · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent/fees/parent_fees_screen.dart:108 and :154 call _handlePayNow(installmentId: 'term_2') from the hero card and sticky bottom bar; the provider default is also 'term_2' (lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_provider.dart:11-13). Only the per-installment timeline rows pass the real installment.id (:118-121).
- **Impact:** Whenever the outstanding installment is not Term 2 (Term 1 arrears, Term 3, custom plans), the primary payment entry points open the wrong installment with the wrong amount — on a money screen. Wrong-amount payment risk plus parent confusion.
- **Recommendation:** Derive the CTA's installment from the fees data (first unpaid/overdue installment), never a literal; make the payment screen refuse to render without an explicit installment id.

### F-242 · [P1] — Money screen falls back to fabricated demo data ('Ravi Kumar, 8-A') in the production path
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/fees-payment · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent/payment/parent_payment_provider.dart:63 'return data ?? async.value ?? _fallbackSummary(installmentId)' and :66-101 hardcode child 'Ravi Kumar', class '8-A', amounts and breakdown incl. a ₹200 late fee — reachable whenever the summary fetch yields nothing.
- **Impact:** A real parent can see a fictional child's name and invented amounts (including a late fee) on the payment screen — the fastest possible way to destroy trust in a school-fees product; incumbents' worst reviews are exactly about wrong fee data (e.g., MyClassboard parent complaints, softwaresuggest.com/myclassboard).
- **Recommendation:** Delete the fallback summary from the production provider; render the existing error/empty states instead. Keep demo data exclusively behind the mock repository.

### F-243 · [P1] — Production login copy says 'OTP is verified by the staging server'
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** auth · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/auth/login_screen.dart:156-159 — non-demo branch subtitle: 'Enter your registered mobile number. OTP is verified by the staging server.' This is the first screen every parent sees.
- **Impact:** Engineering jargon ('staging server') on the very first touchpoint undermines confidence and would be caught by any prospect in a sales demo.
- **Recommendation:** Change to user copy, e.g. 'We'll send a one-time password to this number.'

### F-244 · [P1] — WhatsApp is not a channel in the core communication/broadcast pipeline, despite an admin WhatsApp-provider setup screen
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** communication · **Role:** schoolAdmin
- **Evidence:** supabase/functions/_shared/communication/communication_service.ts:651-653 validates channel to only 'sms', 'email', 'push'; the admin app ships a WhatsApp Provider config screen (lib/router/app_router.dart:842-848) and a separate school_completion WhatsApp sender (supabase/functions/_shared/school_completion/whatsapp_providers.ts, msg91/gupshup) that the broadcast pipeline does not use. Competitor context: WhatsApp updates are headline features of Teachmint and MyClassboard (capterra.com/p/163105/Teachmint, myclassboard.com).
- **Impact:** In India, WhatsApp is THE parent communication channel; schools evaluating against Teachmint/MyClassboard will treat its absence from notices/broadcasts as a table-stakes failure — and the setup screen creates an expectation the broadcast composer cannot fulfil (admin configures a provider, then finds no WhatsApp option when sending).
- **Recommendation:** Wire the existing whatsapp_providers sender into communication_service as a fourth channel (template-based), or remove/relabel the provider setup screen until it exists.

### F-245 · [P1] — Counter QR/UPI collection demands internal IDs and dumps raw session data — no student search
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** finance/collections · **Role:** cashier
- **Evidence:** lib/features/finance/finance_qr_payment_screen.dart:97-108 free-text 'Invoice ID' field is the only way to start; :138-156 shows 'Session ${session.id}', raw status enum (session.status.name), raw expiresAt.toLocal() timestamp, and the raw UPI payload as selectable text; confirmation is a manual receipt-number field.
- **Impact:** A front-desk cashier at the fee counter (peak-load moment) must know internal invoice IDs by heart; every incumbent's counter flow starts from student search → pending dues. This makes Akshara's counter collection slower than the market despite having real UPI QR generation.
- **Recommendation:** Start from the existing student/receipt lookup (finance_collections_screen.dart:195-198) → pick pending invoice → auto-fill amount; hide session internals behind a status chip ('Waiting for payment · expires in 9:59').

### F-246 · [P1] — No parent-facing online admission application/enquiry form
- **Platform:** web · **Module:** admissions · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** web/src/routes/router.tsx has no public/apply route (grep 'apply|public' returns none); admissions module is staff-side lead entry only (lib/router/app_router.dart:1256-1333: dashboard/leads/applications/enrollment — all behind staff auth). Competitor capability: Entab, MyClassboard and NextERP all offer online admission/enquiry portals for parents (entab.in, myclassboard.com/best-school-erp-software-in-india, nexterp.in).
- **Impact:** Admissions season is the ERP's ROI showcase; without a shareable online enquiry/application form, front office staff re-key every lead by hand and schools comparing vendors see a missing checkbox on the most revenue-relevant module.
- **Recommendation:** Ship a minimal public enquiry form (name, phone, class) that creates an admissions lead — the leads pipeline it feeds already exists.

### F-247 · [P2] — Parent homework/exams/timetable are buried 2 taps behind an 'Academics' tab that lands on Attendance
- **Platform:** all-mobile · **Module:** parent/navigation · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** lib/features/parent/shell/parent_shell.dart:35-45 — the 'Academics' tab routes to RouteNames.parentAttendance; Homework/Exams/Timetable are reachable only via in-page links on the attendance screen (lib/features/parent/attendance/parent_attendance_screen.dart:105-110) and are absent from the More sheet.
- **Impact:** Homework is the highest-frequency parent check (daily); competitors surface it one tap from home. The Academics label landing on Attendance also mislabels the destination.
- **Recommendation:** Land the Academics tab on a small academics hub or segmented header (Attendance | Homework | Exams | Timetable) so the daily journey is 1 tap + 1 in-place switch.

### F-248 · [P2] — No live transport tracking for parents while the web nav promises a 'Live Tracking' page
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** transport · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** Deliberate owner deferral (GPS = Phase 2) and honestly disclosed in-app: lib/features/parent/transport/parent_transport_screen.dart:77 'Live map integration is not enabled in this build.' But web/src/routes/navConfig.ts:240 labels '/transport/tracking' as 'Live Tracking' (web/src/pages/transport/TrackingPage.tsx exists). Competitors NextERP and Entab market live bus tracking to parents (nexterp.in, entab.in/mobile-apps.html).
- **Impact:** Consistent top-3 parent-requested feature in this market; the deferral is a conscious trade-off, but the web label promises a capability the product line has deferred — expectation mismatch for staff and demos.
- **Recommendation:** Rename the web nav item to 'Trip Log/Attendance' until GPS ships; keep the honest mobile disclosure pattern.

### F-249 · [P2] — No UPI AutoPay / recurring fee mandate — a nascent differentiator Akshara could own
- **Platform:** all · **Module:** finance/fees · **Role:** parent
- **Evidence:** No autopay/mandate concept anywhere in lib/features/finance or the payment backend (payment_service.ts models one-shot intents only). Market: virtually no incumbent has it either — axoneura.in/blog/best-school-management-software-india documents that Entab/Fedena/MyClassboard/Edunext all require manual monthly parent action; only one niche vendor offers native UPI AutoPay.
- **Impact:** Missed opportunity rather than a gap: recurring UPI mandates would leapfrog the entire incumbent field on collection rates — but this is meaningless until the P0 gateway integration lands.
- **Recommendation:** After the gateway P0 is resolved, add UPI AutoPay mandate setup to the fee-payment roadmap as a differentiating feature.

### F-250 · [P2] — Tablet layouts under-use the canvas — workspace switcher leaves the lower half empty
- **Platform:** tablet · **Module:** shared/shell · **Role:** staff
- **Evidence:** test/golden/goldens/workspace_switcher_multihat_834x1194.png — all content occupies the top ~45%; the lower ~55% is blank white. Phone-first compositions are centered/max-width constrained (e.g., compactContentMaxWidth in parent_fees_screen.dart:37) but not recomposed for tablet.
- **Impact:** Tablets are the natural form factor for school front desks and principals; sparse tablet screens read as an unfinished port next to desktop-grade competitor consoles.
- **Recommendation:** Add tablet-specific two-pane compositions for hub/switcher screens; the golden suite already renders 834x1194, so regressions are cheap to catch.

## PART 3 — BOARD-PROTECTED 'NEVER CHANGE' REGISTER

*Patterns the board certifies as correct. A remediation that removes or weakens one of these is a regression, not a fix.*
*Any change to them requires an APPENDIX C entry in the master roadmap.*

- **NC-01** (principal-product-designer) The token pipeline (primitives → semantic tokens → ColorScheme/ThemeExtension) and the zero-hardcoded-hex discipline in feature screens — this is the backbone everything else can be fixed on.
- **NC-02** (principal-product-designer) The PersonaNav contract: max 4 primary tabs + More sheet with the workspace switcher, test-enforced (persona_nav.dart) — correct mobile IA for USER→ROLE→WORKSPACE→TASK.
- **NC-03** (principal-product-designer) The golden screenshot suite across 3 viewports + dark variants — keep and extend it (with production theming), never delete it.
- **NC-04** (principal-product-designer) SchoolBuildScope's reversible hide-first mechanism with documented owner decisions inline.
- **NC-05** (principal-product-designer) The web primitives-as-documented-ports pattern (KpiCard.tsx 'Ports AksharaKpiCard') and CSS-variable token mirroring — the right way to keep two codebases on one design system.
- **NC-06** (principal-product-designer) Accessibility floor tokens (WCAG ratios, 11px min label, single sanctioned text-scale clamp) and the rendered-contrast audit test pattern.
- **NC-07** (senior-ux-researcher) The PersonaNavSpec contract — max 4 primary tabs + shared More sheet, single source of truth across teacher/parent/student shells, enforced by persona_nav_test (lib/shared/navigation/persona_nav.dart:58-61)
- **NC-08** (senior-ux-researcher) The workspace scoping model itself (USER→ROLE→WORKSPACE→TASK) — role-bounded module visibility is the right architecture; fix the exams catalog omission, keep the mechanism (lib/core/workspace/workspace.dart)
- **NC-09** (senior-ux-researcher) SchoolBuildScope's paired nav-hide + route-block pattern, where guard and nav share one source of truth (lib/core/config/school_build_scope.dart + route_guards.dart:411)
- **NC-10** (senior-ux-researcher) Exam marks entry lifecycle UX: phase-gated actions, focus-advance rows, AB/ML/DB statuses, inline explanation of what is blocking the next step (exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:419-497)
- **NC-11** (senior-ux-researcher) Teacher attendance context carry-over: tapping a today-period pre-selects that class, with bulk-mark actions and submit guard (teacher_attendance_screen.dart:34-35, 288-312)
- **NC-12** (senior-ux-researcher) The honest no-fake-data policy in empty states — never simulate data; only the wording needs de-jargoning
- **NC-13** (senior-ux-researcher) Parent/child model: phone+OTP to the parent, students never need a phone — the login flow correctly reflects this
- **NC-14** (senior-ux-researcher) Web sidebar's grouped IA (Overview/People/Academics/Operations/Finance/Engage/Configure) as the organizing skeleton — fix the Configure labels, keep the grouping
- **NC-15** (mobile-ux-specialist) The offline reliability platform: SyncBanner shown only when offline/pending/conflicted, lifecycle draft flush, ReliableWriter outbox with optimistic outcomes and honest 'saved on this device' messaging (lib/app/app.dart, lib/core/reliability/) — this is better than most commercial mobile SaaS
- **NC-16** (mobile-ux-specialist) PersonaNavSpec contract of <=4 primary tabs + shared More sheet, enforced by test — it is what keeps three persona shells coherent
- **NC-17** (mobile-ux-specialist) Layered scope gating (SchoolBuildScope -> backend-less surface gate -> ChainScope -> capability gating -> RBAC) that makes out-of-scope verticals and mock-only surfaces genuinely unreachable rather than merely hidden
- **NC-18** (mobile-ux-specialist) Marks-entry ergonomics: per-row focus advance, dirty-cell state, save-all with per-student error reporting
- **NC-19** (mobile-ux-specialist) Locked-vs-hidden distinction in admin nav (plan-locked modules show an upgrade state instead of vanishing)
- **NC-20** (mobile-ux-specialist) Accessibility discipline: Semantics labels on icon-only buttons, dense-grid-only text-scale clamping, dedicated semantics tests
- **NC-21** (web-ux-specialist) The honest-state doctrine: DataUnavailable/Empty/Error states and the hard rule against fabricated business data (useModuleQuery IS_DEMO gate) — this is rarer and more valuable than teams realize
- **NC-22** (web-ux-specialist) The 1:1 M15 token port (index.css CSS variables + tailwind.config.ts) including the light/dark channel system and the subset icon font
- **NC-23** (web-ux-specialist) Deep-linkable architecture: real browser paths for every page, localStorage session restore, parametric :id detail routes with breadcrumbs
- **NC-24** (web-ux-specialist) Demo-gating discipline in RoleSwitcher (persona switching hidden in live mode) — extend this pattern to LoginPage rather than weakening it
- **NC-25** (web-ux-specialist) The ResourceList/AsyncBoundary/KpiPage composition model — it is the right chassis; it needs pagination and actions added, not replacement
- **NC-26** (web-ux-specialist) Entitlement-aware error mapping (403 MODULE_DISABLED → 'Module not enabled' instead of a raw error)
- **NC-27** (enterprise-saas-designer) The five-layer gating stack (SchoolBuildScope → ChainScope → school capabilities → RBAC longest-prefix permissions → backend-less surface gate) enforced identically in nav filters and ErpRouteGuard — this architecture is why deep-link leaks are rare here (route_guards.dart:396-457).
- **NC-28** (enterprise-saas-designer) Locked-not-hidden plan entitlement UX with WhatsApp sales CTA and no in-app payment (entitlement_module_gate.dart) — perfectly tuned for the Indian school market.
- **NC-29** (enterprise-saas-designer) The ≤4-primary-tabs + More-sheet persona shell pattern with the workspace switcher inside the More sheet (persona_nav.dart, parent/teacher/student shells).
- **NC-30** (enterprise-saas-designer) The single shared AksharaModuleSubNav component with pinned mobile More overflow — one widget preventing 12+ module sub-navs from drifting (akshara_navigation.dart:266+).
- **NC-31** (enterprise-saas-designer) RBAC-scoped entity search (students/staff/leads) reachable from every admin app bar (global_search_overlay.dart).
- **NC-32** (enterprise-saas-designer) The grouped web sidebar IA (Overview/People/Academics/Operations/Finance/Engage/Configure) with Examinations as a first-class item — this should become the model for the Flutter admin IA, not the reverse.
- **NC-33** (enterprise-saas-designer) Longest-prefix route-permission resolution with denied-access audit logging (route_guards.dart:158-173).
- **NC-34** (hci-specialist) The Resume/Discard draft-recovery pattern on money and marks forms — never silently prefill a recovered money amount (finance_workflow_actions.dart:889-896 documents this deliberately); keep it
- **NC-35** (hci-specialist) The amber 'queued' vs green 'success' ceremony distinction for offline vs server-confirmed payments — this honesty is rare and valuable
- **NC-36** (hci-specialist) The `if (state.isLoading) return` guard convention at the top of every mutation notifier, plus idempotency-key dedupe for collections
- **NC-37** (hci-specialist) Mandatory typed-reason confirmation for cancelling collections/invoices (FIN-D3 pattern with disabled-until-valid confirm)
- **NC-38** (hci-specialist) The submit button doubling as the completeness gate in attendance ('N unmarked' label on the disabled button) — the state of the gate reads in exactly one place
- **NC-39** (hci-specialist) Marks-field row_version concurrency guard and the never-write-a-spurious-zero rule when toggling a student back to Present (exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:641-664)
- **NC-40** (hci-specialist) SchoolBuildScope hide-first enforcement keeping out-of-scope verticals unreachable at both nav and route-guard levels
- **NC-41** (accessibility-specialist) The theme-level 48dp touch-target enforcement and its lint tests (lib/theme/spacing.dart:43, test/theme/tap_target_lint_test.dart) — never allow a global shrinkWrap or per-widget override culture back in
- **NC-42** (accessibility-specialist) Free text scaling with exactly two documented dense-grid clamp exceptions at 1.3x (lib/theme/accessibility.dart:17-21) — do not add new clamp sites and never introduce a global textScaleFactor: 1.0
- **NC-43** (accessibility-specialist) AksharaAccessibility.onColorFor luminance-picked glyph colors (lib/theme/accessibility.dart:46-51) — this is what keeps badges legible in both schemes
- **NC-44** (accessibility-specialist) Contrast regression tests running in CI (test/theme/rendered_contrast_audit_test.dart, contrast_checker_test.dart) — fix their large-text floor, but never remove them
- **NC-45** (accessibility-specialist) The dark theme's text color calibration (color_tokens.dart dark factory) — it passes AA with 2-3x headroom everywhere; do not 'soften' it for aesthetics
- **NC-46** (accessibility-specialist) Semantics living in the shared widget layer (lib/shared/widgets/*) so screens inherit accessibility by composition — keep new shared widgets to this bar
- **NC-47** (accessibility-specialist) Web Field/Input label association via useId + text-based error messages (web/src/components/ui/Field.tsx) and the aria-hidden decorative Icon pattern (web/src/components/Icon.tsx)
- **NC-48** (accessibility-specialist) Landmark structure (main/nav/aside/header) and html lang="en" in the web shell
- **NC-49** (school-erp-domain-expert) The exception-first attendance model (mark absentees, 'Fill remaining present') with draft autosave and the post-submit correction-request flow — this is exactly how teachers work (teacher_attendance_screen.dart)
- **NC-50** (school-erp-domain-expert) AB/ML/DB exam statuses locking the marks field with NO zero written, and the audited grace/moderation dialog with mandatory reason (exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:641-664, 747-831)
- **NC-51** (school-erp-domain-expert) The honest amber 'Payment queued — receipt issued once it syncs' offline ceremony vs the green success beat — never fake a receipt (finance_workflow_actions.dart:832-878)
- **NC-52** (school-erp-domain-expert) One-tap WhatsApp guardian contact with prefilled reminder text on the defaulters screen (finance_defaulters_screen.dart)
- **NC-53** (school-erp-domain-expert) The fail-closed dues gate on TC issuance (sis_workflow_actions.dart:282-303)
- **NC-54** (school-erp-domain-expert) Day-close lock on the cash counter with explicit reopen (finance_collections_screen.dart:420-494)
- **NC-55** (school-erp-domain-expert) REL-3 draft autosave pattern on money and marks forms — extend it to admissions enrollment, don't remove it anywhere
- **NC-56** (qa-usability-reviewer) The REL-3 draft pattern on money and marks forms: debounced autosave, flush on app-background, and — critically — the explicit Resume/Discard prompt that never silently prefills a money amount (finance_workflow_actions.dart:943-1026)
- **NC-57** (qa-usability-reviewer) The amber 'Payment queued' ceremony for offline-recorded collections vs the green success ceremony — the honest distinction between 'captured' and 'server-confirmed' money (finance_workflow_actions.dart:832-878)
- **NC-58** (qa-usability-reviewer) AksharaUnsavedChangesGuard including the web beforeunload arming (lib/shared/forms/akshara_unsaved_guard.dart) — apply it MORE widely, never remove it
- **NC-59** (qa-usability-reviewer) Marks-entry keyboard flow: Enter/Tab focus-chaining, per-row save, row_version lost-update guard, and the never-write-a-spurious-0 rule when switching a student back to Present (exam_marks_entry_screen.dart:252-264, 647-664)
- **NC-60** (qa-usability-reviewer) Login phone ergonomics: +91 prefix, digits-only formatter, 10-digit cap, actionable inline error (login_screen.dart:193-238)
- **NC-61** (qa-usability-reviewer) The admin_navigation.dart:49-52 discipline of deleting dead placeholder route builders and documenting why — extend this to every surface
- **NC-62** (qa-usability-reviewer) AksharaDateField as the sole date-input mechanism (akshara_date_field.dart) — finish migrating the leave forms to it, never regress to free-text dates
- **NC-63** (ai-experience-reviewer) The Adaptive Priority Feed's explainability contract: visible 'Why:' reason + factor breakdown + Dismiss/Mute on every AI recommendation, and pre-staged actions that navigate but never auto-execute (adaptive_priority_feed.dart)
- **NC-64** (ai-experience-reviewer) Honest degraded-feed labeling: 'Showing partial results — some data needs additional access' instead of silently presenting a subset as complete
- **NC-65** (ai-experience-reviewer) AI Economics panel's refusal to fail-soft: cost/cap monitoring errors propagate as errors, never as a healthy-looking $0 (hybrid_copilot_repository.dart:57-64)
- **NC-66** (ai-experience-reviewer) Parent Insights' no-free-chat design for parents, its PDF 'AI-assisted summary' disclaimer, and deterministic language-preference catalog (English/Hindi/Telugu)
- **NC-67** (ai-experience-reviewer) Capability-filter messaging that distinguishes plan-locked ('no payment is taken in the app') from school-disabled topics
- **NC-68** (ai-experience-reviewer) Full-copilot send-failure UX that restores the user's typed message with a mapped error message
- **NC-69** (ai-experience-reviewer) Defense-in-depth RBAC re-checks on every AI screen (copilot, economics) rather than trusting nav gating alone
- **NC-70** (journey-principal) The Approval Center interaction model: swipe-to-decide gated on real approve permission, server-driven maker-checker badge, mandatory reject reason, batch bar, bottom-sheet detail with audit trail — this is the product's crown jewel

- **NC-75** (journey-principal) Design-token discipline and true dark-mode parity across dashboards
- **NC-76** (journey-teacher) The exception-first attendance grid workflow: 'All present' → tap only exceptions (cycle P→A→L) → 'Fill remaining present', with long-press for half-day/excused — do not regress this to per-student forms or dropdowns

- **NC-82** (journey-parent) The 4-primary-tabs + More bottom navigation for the parent shell — it matches the mental model of every app this persona already uses

- **NC-100** (journey-accountant) financeAdmin's direct landing on /finance/dashboard and the single-sidebar-entry → module-tabs IA pattern (fix tab count, keep the pattern).
- **NC-101** (journey-accountant) DUPLICATE-stamped, server-audited receipt reprints.
- **NC-102** (journey-frontoffice) SchoolBuildScope's double gate (hidden nav modules + blocked route prefixes) keeping salon/restaurant/healthcare/accommodation/industry/white-label unreachable (lib/core/config/school_build_scope.dart:22-57) — the owner's scope decision enforced in one place.
- **NC-103** (journey-frontoffice) Workspace-scoped Admin Hub grid and bottom nav (USER→ROLE→WORKSPACE→TASK) — the hide-first promise as implemented on mobile for specialist roles.

- **NC-111** (competitive-analyst) The three-layer scope discipline (SchoolBuildScope + ErpRouteGuard + backend-less surface gate) that keeps out-of-scope verticals invisible — only fix the one missed filter (More sheet), never weaken the layers.
- **NC-112** (competitive-analyst) The shared Akshara state components (loading/empty/error/success ceremony) and full dark-theme parity — the visual consistency they produce is a real differentiator.

*Generated 2026-07-21 from the audit session's structured reviewer outputs. 250 findings, 45 verified claims, 114 protected patterns. Roadmap planning only — nothing here has been implemented.*
